- [ ] **Step 3: Write `reconcile.ts`**

Створити `lib/domain/reports/reconcile.ts`:

```ts
// lib/domain/reports/reconcile.ts
// The month-end reconciliation (S12): opening + net movement of the report + excluded from analytics + outside the filter
// + transfers + exchange difference = closing, in the company currency, for the chosen accounts and period. What is left
// is «Розбіжність», never rounded away: the balances come from the accounts (balance at a day), the movement from the
// report path, so a drift between the two shows. A balance at the close of day D is valued at the last NBP table published
// on D or before (`valuationDate`: resolveRate's rule is «strictly before», so it is asked for D + 1); a movement keeps the
// rate of its own day (income and expense their stored base, a transfer the S04 rate of its day).
import { addDays } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/days'
import { mulRate } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/money'
import type { AccountReconciliation, CashCell, CurrencyRates, DayCells, RateNeed, RateQuote, Reconciliation, ReportFacts } from './types'

/** The close of the day before the period and of its last day, neither after today (no actual operation is later). */
export function reconciliationDays(from: string, to: string, today: string): { openingDay: string; closingDay: string } {
  const before = addDays(from, -1)
  return { openingDay: before < today ? before : today, closingDay: to < today ? to : today }
}

export function rateKey(currency: string, date: string): string {
  return `${currency}@${date}`
}

/** The day to ask `resolveRate` for when a balance at the close of `day` is valued. */
export function valuationDate(day: string): string {
  return addDays(day, 1)
}

/** The rates a reconciliation was given, by `rateKey`; a missing key is a rate NBP could not give. */
export type RateBook = ReadonlyMap<string, RateQuote>

export interface ReconcileAccount {
  accountId: string
  currency: string
  openingMinor: number
  closingMinor: number
}

function cellsOf(facts: ReportFacts, accountId: string): Array<[string, CashCell]> {
  return [...(facts.cash.get(accountId) ?? []), ...(facts.excludedCash.get(accountId) ?? [])]
}

function byNeed(a: RateNeed, b: RateNeed): number {
  if (a.currency !== b.currency) return a.currency < b.currency ? -1 : 1
  return a.date < b.date ? -1 : a.date > b.date ? 1 : 0
}

/** Each rate the reconciliation needs: the opening and closing valuation of every foreign account and each day it transferred. */
export function ratesForReconciliation(
  accounts: readonly ReconcileAccount[],
  scope: ReportFacts,
  days: { openingDay: string; closingDay: string },
  baseCurrency: string,
): RateNeed[] {
  const needs = new Map<string, RateNeed>()
  const need = (currency: string, date: string): void => {
    needs.set(rateKey(currency, date), { currency, date })
  }
  for (const a of accounts) {
    if (a.currency === baseCurrency) continue
    need(a.currency, valuationDate(days.openingDay))
    need(a.currency, valuationDate(days.closingDay))
    for (const [day, c] of cellsOf(scope, a.accountId)) if (c.transferInMinor !== 0 || c.transferOutMinor !== 0) need(a.currency, day)
  }
  return [...needs.values()].sort(byNeed)
}

function netBase(byAccount: Map<string, DayCells>, accounts: ReadonlySet<string>): number {
  let sum = 0
  for (const [accountId, cells] of byAccount) {
    if (!accounts.has(accountId)) continue
    for (const c of cells.values()) sum += c.inBase - c.outBase
  }
  return sum
}

export interface ReconcileInput {
  baseCurrency: string
  days: { openingDay: string; closingDay: string }
  /** The chosen accounts with their balances at the close of the two days. */
  accounts: readonly ReconcileAccount[]
  /** Every operation of the chosen accounts in the period (the account filter only). */
  scope: ReportFacts
  /** The operations the report chose (every filter). */
  report: ReportFacts
  includeExcluded: boolean
  rates: RateBook
}

export function reconcile(input: ReconcileInput): Reconciliation {
  const { baseCurrency, days, scope, report, rates, includeExcluded } = input
  const ids = new Set(input.accounts.map((a) => a.accountId))
  const missing = new Map<string, RateNeed>()
  const value = (minor: number, currency: string, date: string): number | null => {
    if (currency === baseCurrency) return minor
    const quote = rates.get(rateKey(currency, date))
    if (quote === undefined) {
      missing.set(rateKey(currency, date), { currency, date })
      return null
    }
    return mulRate(minor, quote.rate)
  }

  const reportAnalytics = netBase(report.cash, ids)
  const reportExcluded = netBase(report.excludedCash, ids)
  const reportNetBase = reportAnalytics + (includeExcluded ? reportExcluded : 0)
  const outsideFilterBase = netBase(scope.cash, ids) + netBase(scope.excludedCash, ids) - reportAnalytics - reportExcluded

  const openingDate = valuationDate(days.openingDay)
  const closingDate = valuationDate(days.closingDay)
  const accounts: AccountReconciliation[] = input.accounts.map((a) => {
    let movementMinor = 0
    let incomeExpenseBase = 0
    let transfersBase: number | null = 0
    for (const [day, c] of cellsOf(scope, a.accountId)) {
      movementMinor += c.inMinor - c.outMinor + c.transferInMinor - c.transferOutMinor
      incomeExpenseBase += c.inBase - c.outBase
      if (c.transferInMinor === 0 && c.transferOutMinor === 0) continue
      const moneyIn = value(c.transferInMinor, a.currency, day)
      const moneyOut = value(c.transferOutMinor, a.currency, day)
      transfersBase = transfersBase === null || moneyIn === null || moneyOut === null ? null : transfersBase + moneyIn - moneyOut
    }
    const openingBase = value(a.openingMinor, a.currency, openingDate)
    const closingBase = value(a.closingMinor, a.currency, closingDate)
    // The exchange difference is what the period's movement leaves at the closing rate: a balance that does not agree with
    // the movement is not an exchange difference, it stays in «Розбіжність».
    const expectedClosing = value(a.openingMinor + movementMinor, a.currency, closingDate)
    const fxBase =
      a.currency === baseCurrency
        ? 0
        : openingBase === null || expectedClosing === null || transfersBase === null
          ? null
          : expectedClosing - openingBase - incomeExpenseBase - transfersBase
    return { accountId: a.accountId, currency: a.currency, openingMinor: a.openingMinor, closingMinor: a.closingMinor, movementMinor, openingBase, closingBase, transfersBase, fxBase }
  })

  const total = (pick: (a: AccountReconciliation) => number | null): number | null =>
    accounts.reduce<number | null>((sum, a) => {
      const v = pick(a)
      return sum === null || v === null ? null : sum + v
    }, 0)
  const openingBase = total((a) => a.openingBase)
  const closingBase = total((a) => a.closingBase)
  const transfersBase = total((a) => a.transfersBase)
  const fxBase = total((a) => a.fxBase)
  const excludedBase = includeExcluded ? null : reportExcluded
  const discrepancyBase =
    openingBase === null || closingBase === null || transfersBase === null || fxBase === null
      ? null
      : closingBase - openingBase - reportNetBase - (excludedBase ?? 0) - outsideFilterBase - transfersBase - fxBase
  const currencies = [...new Set(input.accounts.map((a) => a.currency).filter((c) => c !== baseCurrency))].sort()
  const rateLines: CurrencyRates[] = currencies.map((currency) => ({
    currency,
    opening: rates.get(rateKey(currency, openingDate)) ?? null,
    closing: rates.get(rateKey(currency, closingDate)) ?? null,
  }))
  return {
    openingDay: days.openingDay,
    closingDay: days.closingDay,
    valued: missing.size === 0,
    missingRates: [...missing.values()].sort(byNeed),
    openingBase,
    reportNetBase,
    excludedBase,
    excludedIncluded: includeExcluded,
    outsideFilterBase,
    transfersBase,
    fxBase,
    closingBase,
    discrepancyBase,
    accounts,
    rates: rateLines,
  }
}
```

Створити `lib/domain/reports/balances.ts`:

```ts
// lib/domain/reports/balances.ts
// The balances at the close of a day (the dashboard «Гроші зараз» and the Balance page, S12): every account in its own
// currency and in the company currency at the last NBP table of that day; a currency without a rate is left out of the
// total and the answer says it is not complete.
import { mulRate } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/money'
import type { LedgerAccount } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/types'
import { rateKey, valuationDate, type RateBook } from './reconcile'
import type { BalanceLine, BalancesReport, CurrencyTotal, RateNeed } from './types'

/** One rate per foreign currency of the accounts, for the close of `day`. */
export function balanceRates(accounts: ReadonlyArray<Pick<LedgerAccount, 'currency'>>, day: string, baseCurrency: string): RateNeed[] {
  return [...new Set(accounts.map((a) => a.currency).filter((c) => c !== baseCurrency))].sort().map((currency) => ({ currency, date: valuationDate(day) }))
}

export interface BalancesInput {
  date: string
  baseCurrency: string
  accounts: readonly LedgerAccount[]
  /** The balance of each account at the close of `date` (`balancesAt`); an account missing here keeps `balanceMinor`. */
  balances: ReadonlyMap<string, number>
  rates: RateBook
}

export function balancesReport(input: BalancesInput): BalancesReport {
  const { date, baseCurrency, rates } = input
  const quote = (currency: string) => rates.get(rateKey(currency, valuationDate(date))) ?? null
  const accounts: BalanceLine[] = input.accounts.map((a) => {
    const balanceMinor = input.balances.get(a.id) ?? a.balanceMinor
    const rate = a.currency === baseCurrency ? null : quote(a.currency)
    const baseMinor = a.currency === baseCurrency ? balanceMinor : rate === null ? null : mulRate(balanceMinor, rate.rate)
    return { accountId: a.id, name: a.name, type: a.type, currency: a.currency, color: a.color, balanceMinor, baseMinor }
  })
  // The company currency first, then the others alphabetically.
  const currencies = [...new Set(accounts.map((a) => a.currency))].sort((x, y) => (x === baseCurrency ? -1 : y === baseCurrency ? 1 : x < y ? -1 : x > y ? 1 : 0))
  const byCurrency: CurrencyTotal[] = currencies.map((currency) => {
    const lines = accounts.filter((l) => l.currency === currency)
    return {
      currency,
      balanceMinor: lines.reduce((s, l) => s + l.balanceMinor, 0),
      baseMinor: lines.some((l) => l.baseMinor === null) ? null : lines.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.baseMinor ?? 0), 0),
      rate: currency === baseCurrency ? null : quote(currency),
    }
  })
  return {
    date,
    currency: baseCurrency,
    accounts,
    totalBase: accounts.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.baseMinor ?? 0), 0),
    complete: accounts.every((l) => l.baseMinor !== null),
    byCurrency,
  }
}
```

```ts
// tests/unit/domain/reports/fixtures.ts
// Operations, categories, export labels and sample reports for the S12 domain and server tests.
import type { LedgerTransaction } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/types'
import type { ReportCategory } from '@/lib/domain/reports/categories'
import type { ExportLabels } from '@/lib/domain/reports/export'
import type { CashFlowReport, PLReport, Reconciliation } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'

let seq = 0

/** An actual operation in PLN with its accrual on the payment day; `fields` overrides any of it. A transfer gets the same amount in. */
export function op(fields: Pick<LedgerTransaction, 'type' | 'accountId' | 'amountMinor' | 'paymentDate'> & Partial<LedgerTransaction>): LedgerTransaction {
  seq += 1
  const tx: LedgerTransaction = {
    id: `t${seq}`,
    status: 'actual',
    currency: 'PLN',
    accrual: { mode: 'date', date: fields.paymentDate },
    isRecurring: false,
    tags: [],
    fileUrls: [],
    ...fields,
  }
  if (tx.type === 'transfer') {
    tx.toAmountMinor ??= tx.amountMinor
    tx.toCurrency ??= tx.currency
  }
  return tx
}

/** «Sprzedaż» (income) and «IT» (expense) with the child «Chmura». */
export const CATEGORIES: ReportCategory[] = [
  { id: 'cat-in', name: 'Sprzedaż', type: 'income' },
  { id: 'cat-it', name: 'IT', type: 'expense' },
  { id: 'cat-cloud', name: 'Chmura', type: 'expense', parentId: 'cat-it' },
]

export const LABELS: ExportLabels = {
  sheets: { report: 'Report', categories: 'By category', reconciliation: 'Reconciliation' },
  columns: {
    month: 'Month', day: 'Day', income: 'Money in', expense: 'Money out', net: 'Net', running: 'Running total', category: 'Category', type: 'Type',
    amount: 'Amount', total: 'Total', line: 'Line', account: 'Account', currency: 'Currency', opening: 'Opening balance', closing: 'Closing balance', fx: 'Exchange difference',
  },
  types: { income: 'Money in', expense: 'Money out' },
  rows: {
    total: 'Total', totalIncome: 'Total income', totalExpense: 'Total expenses', profit: 'Profit / loss',
    excludedIncome: 'Excluded from analytics: income', excludedExpense: 'Excluded from analytics: expenses', noCategory: 'No category',
  },
  lines: {
    opening: 'Opening balance', net: 'Net movement in the report', excluded: 'Excluded from analytics', included: 'The excluded operations are already in the figures',
    outside: 'Operations outside the filter', transfers: 'Transfers between accounts', fx: 'Exchange difference', closing: 'Closing balance', discrepancy: 'Discrepancy', unvalued: 'No NBP rate:',
  },
}

/** One PLN account «main» from 1000 zł: 500 in, 160.50 out, 230 excluded, so 1109.50 at the end. */
export function sampleReconciliation(): Reconciliation {
  return {
    openingDay: '2026-09-30', closingDay: '2026-10-02', valued: true, missingRates: [],
    openingBase: 100_000, reportNetBase: 33_950, excludedBase: -23_000, excludedIncluded: false, outsideFilterBase: 0,
    transfersBase: 0, fxBase: 0, closingBase: 110_950, discrepancyBase: 0,
    accounts: [{ accountId: 'main', currency: 'PLN', openingMinor: 100_000, closingMinor: 110_950, movementMinor: 10_950, openingBase: 100_000, closingBase: 110_950, transfersBase: 0, fxBase: 0 }],
    rates: [],
  }
}

export function sampleCashFlow(): CashFlowReport {
  return {
    kind: 'cashflow', from: '2026-10-01', to: '2026-10-02', currency: 'PLN', granularity: 'day',
    totals: { incomeBase: 50_000, expenseBase: 16_050, netBase: 33_950 },
    buckets: [
      { key: '2026-10-01', incomeBase: 50_000, expenseBase: 12_000, netBase: 38_000, runningNetBase: 38_000 },
      { key: '2026-10-02', incomeBase: 0, expenseBase: 4_050, netBase: -4_050, runningNetBase: 33_950 },
    ],
    accounts: [{ accountId: 'main', inMinor: 50_000, outMinor: 16_050, transferInMinor: 0, transferOutMinor: 0 }],
    categories: [
      { row: 'cat-in', type: 'income', amountBase: 50_000 },

}

export function samplePL(): PLReport {

}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { categoryTreeRows, parseReportCategory, rowRank } from '@/lib/domain/reports/categories'
import { CATEGORIES } from './fixtures'

describe('categoryTreeRows', () => {
  it('a parent carries its subtree, a child is one level deeper, empty rows are left out, the uncategorised row comes last', () => {
    const rows = categoryTreeRows(
      [
        { row: 'cat-cloud', type: 'expense', amountBase: 4_050 },
        { row: 'cat-it', type: 'expense', amountBase: 12_000 },
        { row: '_none:expense', type: 'expense', amountBase: 1_000 },
        { row: 'cat-in', type: 'income', amountBase: 50_000 },
      ],
      CATEGORIES,
    )
    expect(rows).toEqual([
      { row: 'cat-in', type: 'income', level: 0, amountBase: 50_000 },
      { row: 'cat-it', type: 'expense', level: 0, amountBase: 16_050 },
      { row: 'cat-cloud', type: 'expense', level: 1, amountBase: 4_050 },
      { row: '_none:expense', type: 'expense', level: 0, amountBase: 1_000 },
    ])
  })

  it('a row the tree does not know, or of the other type, counts as uncategorised', () => {
    expect(categoryTreeRows([{ row: 'cat-gone', type: 'expense', amountBase: 300 }, { row: 'cat-in', type: 'expense', amountBase: 200 }], CATEGORIES)).toEqual([
      { row: '_none:expense', type: 'expense', level: 0, amountBase: 500 },
    ])
  })

  it('a parent without amounts of its own shows the sum of its children', () => {
    expect(categoryTreeRows([{ row: 'cat-cloud', type: 'expense', amountBase: 4_050 }], CATEGORIES)).toEqual([
      { row: 'cat-it', type: 'expense', level: 0, amountBase: 4_050 },
      { row: 'cat-cloud', type: 'expense', level: 1, amountBase: 4_050 },
    ])
  })
})

describe('rowRank', () => {
  it('the caller order of the categories, the uncategorised row after them, unknown ids last', () => {
    const rank = rowRank(CATEGORIES)
    const rows = ['_none:expense', 'cat-gone', 'cat-cloud', 'cat-it']
    expect([...rows].sort((a, b) => rank('expense', a) - rank('expense', b))).toEqual(['cat-it', 'cat-cloud', '_none:expense', 'cat-gone'])
  })
})

describe('parseReportCategory', () => {
  it('reads the name, the type and the parent; anything else is null', () => {
    expect(parseReportCategory('c1', { name: 'IT', type: 'expense', parentId: 'p', color: '#000000', orgId: 'o' })).toEqual({ id: 'c1', name: 'IT', type: 'expense', parentId: 'p' })
    expect(parseReportCategory('c2', { name: 'Sprzedaż', type: 'income' })).toEqual({ id: 'c2', name: 'Sprzedaż', type: 'income' })
    expect(parseReportCategory('c3', { name: 'X', type: 'transfer' })).toBeNull()
    expect(parseReportCategory('c4', { type: 'income' })).toBeNull()
    expect(parseReportCategory('c5', null)).toBeNull()
  })
})
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { cashFlowSheets, plSheets, sheetsToCsv, type ExportContext } from '@/lib/domain/reports/export'
import { CATEGORIES, LABELS, sampleCashFlow, samplePL } from './fixtures'

const NAMES: Record<string, string> = { 'cat-in': 'Sprzedaż', 'cat-it': 'IT', 'cat-cloud': 'Chmura' }
const ctx: ExportContext = {
  labels: LABELS,
  periodLabel: (key) => `[${key}]`,
  rowName: (row) => NAMES[row] ?? LABELS.rows.noCategory,
  accountName: (id) => (id === 'main' ? 'Konto główne' : id),
  categories: CATEGORIES,
}

describe('cashFlowSheets', () => {
  it('the report, by category and the reconciliation, with the numbers of the report', () => {
    const [report, categories, reconciliation] = cashFlowSheets(sampleCashFlow(), ctx)
    expect(report).toEqual({
      name: 'Report',
      header: ['Day', 'Money in, PLN', 'Money out, PLN', 'Net, PLN', 'Running total, PLN'],
      rows: [
        ['[2026-10-01]', { minor: 50_000 }, { minor: 12_000 }, { minor: 38_000 }, { minor: 38_000 }],
        ['[2026-10-02]', { minor: 0 }, { minor: 4_050 }, { minor: -4_050 }, { minor: 33_950 }],
        ['Total', { minor: 50_000 }, { minor: 16_050 }, { minor: 33_950 }, ''],
        ['Excluded from analytics', { minor: 0 }, { minor: 23_000 }, { minor: -23_000 }, ''],
      ],
    })
    expect(categories).toEqual({
      name: 'By category',
      header: ['Category', 'Type', 'Amount, PLN'],
      rows: [
        ['Sprzedaż', 'Money in', { minor: 50_000 }],
        ['IT', 'Money out', { minor: 16_050 }],
        ['    Chmura', 'Money out', { minor: 4_050 }],
      ],
    })
    expect(reconciliation).toEqual({
      name: 'Reconciliation',
      header: ['Line', 'Amount, PLN'],
      rows: [
        ['Opening balance', { minor: 100_000 }],
        ['Net movement in the report', { minor: 33_950 }],
        ['Excluded from analytics', { minor: -23_000 }],
        ['Closing balance', { minor: 110_950 }],
        [],
        ['Account', 'Currency', 'Opening balance', 'Closing balance', 'Opening balance, PLN', 'Closing balance, PLN', 'Exchange difference, PLN'],
        ['Konto główne', 'PLN', { minor: 100_000 }, { minor: 110_950 }, { minor: 100_000 }, { minor: 110_950 }, { minor: 0 }],
      ],
    })
  })

  it('a concrete tag, the operations outside the filter and a discrepancy are lines; a missing rate is named', () => {
    const tagged = sampleCashFlow()
    tagged.reconciliation = { ...tagged.reconciliation, excludedBase: null, excludedIncluded: true, outsideFilterBase: 500, discrepancyBase: 7 }
    expect(cashFlowSheets(tagged, ctx)[2].rows.slice(0, 6)).toEqual([
      ['Opening balance', { minor: 100_000 }],
      ['Net movement in the report', { minor: 33_950 }],
      ['The excluded operations are already in the figures', ''],
      ['Operations outside the filter', { minor: 500 }],
      ['Closing balance', { minor: 110_950 }],
      ['Discrepancy', { minor: 7 }],
    ])
    const unvalued = sampleCashFlow()
    unvalued.reconciliation = {
      ...unvalued.reconciliation,
      valued: false,
      missingRates: [{ currency: 'EUR', date: '2026-10-03' }],
      fxBase: null,
      discrepancyBase: null,
      rates: [{ currency: 'EUR', opening: null, closing: null }],
    }
    expect(cashFlowSheets(unvalued, ctx)[2].rows.slice(3, 7)).toEqual([
      ['Exchange difference', ''],
      ['Closing balance', { minor: 110_950 }],
      ['Discrepancy', ''],
      ['No NBP rate:', 'EUR'],
    ])
  })
})

describe('plSheets', () => {
  it('months as columns in the order of the categories, totals, profit and the excluded rows', () => {
    const [report, categories, reconciliation] = plSheets(samplePL(), ctx)
    expect(report.name).toBe('Report')
    expect(report.header).toEqual(['Category', 'Type', '[2026-09]', '[2026-10]', 'Total, PLN'])
    expect(report.rows).toEqual([
      ['Sprzedaż', 'Money in', { minor: 0 }, { minor: 50_000 }, { minor: 50_000 }],
      ['Total income', '', { minor: 0 }, { minor: 50_000 }, { minor: 50_000 }],
      ['IT', 'Money out', { minor: 0 }, { minor: 12_000 }, { minor: 12_000 }],
      ['No category', 'Money out', { minor: 1_000 }, { minor: 2_000 }, { minor: 3_000 }],
      ['Total expenses', '', { minor: 1_000 }, { minor: 14_000 }, { minor: 15_000 }],
      ['Profit / loss', '', { minor: -1_000 }, { minor: 36_000 }, { minor: 35_000 }],
      ['Excluded from analytics: expenses', '', '', '', { minor: 23_000 }],
    ])
    expect(categories.rows).toEqual([
      ['Sprzedaż', 'Money in', { minor: 50_000 }],
      ['IT', 'Money out', { minor: 12_000 }],
      ['No category', 'Money out', { minor: 3_000 }],
    ])
    expect(reconciliation.name).toBe('Reconciliation')
  })
})

describe('sheetsToCsv', () => {
  it('the sheets one after another under their names, ; separated, with the decimal sign of the language', () => {
    const csv = sheetsToCsv(cashFlowSheets(sampleCashFlow(), ctx), ',')
    expect(csv.startsWith('﻿Report\r\nDay;Money in, PLN;')).toBe(true)
    expect(csv).toContain('\r\n[2026-10-02];0,00;40,50;-40,50;339,50\r\n')
    expect(csv).toContain('\r\n\r\nBy category\r\nCategory;Type;Amount, PLN\r\n')
    expect(csv).toContain('\r\nOpening balance;1000,00\r\n')
    expect(csv.endsWith('\r\n')).toBe(true)
    expect(sheetsToCsv(cashFlowSheets(sampleCashFlow(), ctx), '.')).toContain('\r\nOpening balance;1000.00\r\n')
  })

  it('a cell that starts like a formula is defused; a negative amount stays a number', () => {
    expect(sheetsToCsv([{ name: 'S', header: ['A'], rows: [['=HYPERLINK("x")'], [{ minor: -1 }]] }], ',')).toBe('﻿S\r\nA\r\n"\'=HYPERLINK(""x"")"\r\n-0,01\r\n')
  })
})
```

Створити `tests/unit/domain/reports/property.test.ts`:

```ts
// The two paths of a report are one function (S12): for random companies (tests/unit/domain/ledger/model.ts) the Cash Flow
// and the P&L of whole months from the incrementally kept rollups equal the ones recomputed from the operations; the
// reconciliation adds up to the minor unit for random periods and filters; the order of the operations changes nothing.
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS, filterForAnalytics, type AnalyticsFilters } from '@/lib/domain/analytics-filter/filter'
import { categoryTypesOf } from '@/lib/domain/analytics-filter/kinds'
import { matchesSharedChoices } from '@/lib/domain/journal/filter'
import { addDays, monthBounds, monthOf } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/days'
import { datedEffects } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/effects'
import type { LedgerTransaction } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/types'
import { buildCashFlowCore } from '@/lib/domain/reports/cashflow'
import { factsFromRollupDocs, operationRollups, selectRollupDocs, type DayRange } from '@/lib/domain/reports/facts'
import { buildPLCore } from '@/lib/domain/reports/pl'
import { rateKey, ratesForReconciliation, reconcile, reconciliationDays } from '@/lib/domain/reports/reconcile'
import type { RateNeed, RateQuote } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import { ACCOUNTS, BASE, Model, TODAY, randomTx, rng } from '../ledger/model'

// ci2 is gone and ce2 is now an income category: their operations count as uncategorised (kindRowOf).
const TYPES = categoryTypesOf([
  { id: 'ci1', type: 'income' },
  { id: 'ce1', type: 'expense' },
  { id: 'ce2', type: 'income' },
])
const IDS = ACCOUNTS.map((a) => a.id)

function companyOf(seed: number): { ops: LedgerTransaction[]; model: Model } {
  const r = rng(seed)
  const model = new Model()
  for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
    const tx = randomTx(r, `t${i}`)
    if (tx) model.commit(null, tx)
  }
  const ids = [...model.txs.keys()]
  for (let i = 0; i < 10 && ids.length > 0; i++) {
    const id = ids[Math.floor(r() * ids.length)]
    const prior = model.txs.get(id) ?? null
    if (r() < 0.5) {
      const next = randomTx(r, id)
      if (next) model.commit(prior, next)
    } else model.commit(prior, null)
  }
  return { ops: [...model.txs.values()], model }
}

function wholeMonths(r: () => number): DayRange {
  const first = monthOf(addDays(TODAY, -Math.floor(r() * 150)))
  const last = monthOf(addDays(TODAY, Math.floor(r() * 60)))
  return { from: `${first}-01`, to: monthBounds(last).last }
}

/** The balance at the close of `day`: the opening plus every movement up to that day (an oracle independent of the rollups). */
function balanceAt(ops: readonly LedgerTransaction[], accountId: string, opening: number, day: string): number {
  let b = opening
  for (const tx of ops) for (const e of datedEffects(tx)) if (e.accountId === accountId && e.day <= day) b += e.delta
  return b
}

function bookFor(needs: readonly RateNeed[]): Map<string, RateQuote> {
  return new Map(needs.map((n) => [rateKey(n.currency, n.date), { rate: 4 + (Number(n.date.replaceAll('-', '')) % 37) / 100, rateDate: n.date, tableNo: null, final: true }]))
}

describe('report properties', () => {
  it('the rollups and the operations give the same Cash Flow and P&L (random companies, whole months, no filters)', () => {
    for (let seed = 1; seed <= 120; seed++) {
      const { ops, model } = companyOf(seed)
      const range = wholeMonths(rng(seed + 1000))
      for (const includeExcluded of [false, true]) {
        const cfInput = { ...range, accountIds: IDS, includeExcluded, categoryTypes: TYPES }
        const computed = operationRollups(ops, BASE, range, { parts: ['cash', 'cashcat'] })
        expect(computed.uncountable).toEqual([])
        expect(buildCashFlowCore(factsFromRollupDocs(selectRollupDocs(model.rollups, new Set(IDS), range, 'cashflow')), cfInput), `seed ${seed}`).toEqual(
          buildCashFlowCore(factsFromRollupDocs(computed.docs), cfInput),
        )
        const plInput = { ...range, includeExcluded, categoryTypes: TYPES }
        expect(buildPLCore(factsFromRollupDocs(selectRollupDocs(model.rollups, new Set(IDS), range, 'pl')), plInput), `seed ${seed}`).toEqual(
          buildPLCore(factsFromRollupDocs(operationRollups(ops, BASE, range, { parts: ['pl'] }).docs), plInput),
        )
      }
    }
  })

  it('opening + the lines = closing to the minor unit, for random companies, periods and filters', () => {
    for (let seed = 1; seed <= 120; seed++) {
      const { ops } = companyOf(seed)
      const r = rng(seed + 2000)
      const months = wholeMonths(r)
      const period: DayRange = { from: addDays(months.from, Math.floor(r() * 10)), to: months.to }
      const filters: AnalyticsFilters = {
        ...EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS,
        accountIds: r() < 0.4 ? [IDS[Math.floor(r() * IDS.length)]] : [],
        types: r() < 0.3 ? ['expense'] : [],
      }
      const accountIds = filters.accountIds.length > 0 ? filters.accountIds : IDS
      const inScope = filters.accountIds.length > 0 ? ops.filter((tx) => matchesSharedChoices(tx, { accountIds: filters.accountIds, counterpartyIds: [], tagIds: [] })) : ops
      const chosen = filterForAnalytics(ops, filters, TYPES)
      const scope = factsFromRollupDocs(operationRollups(inScope, BASE, period, { accounts: new Set(accountIds), parts: ['cash'] }).docs)
      const report = factsFromRollupDocs(operationRollups(chosen.operations, BASE, period, { accounts: new Set(accountIds), parts: ['cash'] }).docs)
      const days = reconciliationDays(period.from, period.to, TODAY)
      const accounts = ACCOUNTS.filter((a) => accountIds.includes(a.id)).map((a) => ({
        accountId: a.id,
        currency: a.currency,
        openingMinor: balanceAt(ops, a.id, a.openingMinor, days.openingDay),
        closingMinor: balanceAt(ops, a.id, a.openingMinor, days.closingDay),
      }))
      const rec = reconcile({ baseCurrency: BASE, days, accounts, scope, report, includeExcluded: chosen.includeExcluded, rates: bookFor(ratesForReconciliation(accounts, scope, days, BASE)) })
      expect(rec.valued, `seed ${seed}`).toBe(true)
      expect(rec.discrepancyBase, `seed ${seed}`).toBe(0)
      const sum = (rec.openingBase ?? NaN) + rec.reportNetBase + (rec.excludedBase ?? 0) + rec.outsideFilterBase + (rec.transfersBase ?? NaN) + (rec.fxBase ?? NaN)
      expect(sum, `seed ${seed}`).toBe(rec.closingBase)
    }
  })

  it('the order of the operations changes nothing', () => {
    for (let seed = 1; seed <= 60; seed++) {
      const { ops } = companyOf(seed)
      const r = rng(seed + 3000)
      const shuffled = [...ops]
      for (let i = shuffled.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
        const j = Math.floor(r() * (i + 1))
        ;[shuffled[i], shuffled[j]] = [shuffled[j], shuffled[i]]
      }
      const range = wholeMonths(r)
      const build = (list: readonly LedgerTransaction[]) => {
        const facts = factsFromRollupDocs(operationRollups(list, BASE, range, { parts: ['cash', 'cashcat', 'pl'] }).docs)
        return [
          buildCashFlowCore(facts, { ...range, accountIds: IDS, includeExcluded: false, categoryTypes: TYPES }),
          buildPLCore(facts, { ...range, includeExcluded: false, categoryTypes: TYPES }),
        ]
      }
      expect(build(shuffled), `seed ${seed}`).toEqual(build(ops))
    }
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `categories.ts` and `buildCsvSections`**

Створити `lib/domain/reports/categories.ts`:

```ts
// lib/domain/reports/categories.ts
// Categories in the reports (S12): the stored document as the server reads it, the tree of «За категоріями» (a parent with
// its subtree, from the S10f tree `buildKindTree`) and the order of the flat P&L rows. The page and the export share them.
import { buildKindTree, noCategoryId, type KindCategory, type KindNode } from '@/lib/domain/analytics-filter/kinds'
import type { MoneyType } from './types'

export interface ReportCategory extends KindCategory {
  name: string
}

/** A stored category (`organizations/{org}/categories/{id}`: name, type, optional parentId), or null when it is not one. */
export function parseReportCategory(id: string, data: unknown): ReportCategory | null {
  if (typeof data !== 'object' || data === null) return null
  const d = data as Record<string, unknown>
  if (typeof d.name !== 'string' || (d.type !== 'income' && d.type !== 'expense')) return null
  const out: ReportCategory = { id, name: d.name, type: d.type }
  if (typeof d.parentId === 'string') out.parentId = d.parentId
  return out
}

export interface CategoryTreeRow {
  row: string
  type: MoneyType
  /** 0 for a root, +1 per parent. */
  level: number
  /** The row's own amount plus its subtree's. */
  amountBase: number
}

/**
 * The rows of «За категоріями»: per type the tree in the caller's order, each parent with the sum of its subtree, rows
 * without an amount left out, the uncategorised row last. A row the tree does not know, or of the other type, is
 * uncategorised (the S10f rule; the server already maps a deleted category so, this keeps the page safe from a race).
 */
export function categoryTreeRows<C extends KindCategory>(lines: ReadonlyArray<{ row: string; type: MoneyType; amountBase: number }>, categories: readonly C[]): CategoryTreeRow[] {
  const tree = buildKindTree(categories)
  const out: CategoryTreeRow[] = []
  for (const type of ['income', 'expense'] as const) {
    const none = noCategoryId(type)
    const own = new Map<string, number>()
    for (const line of lines) {
      if (line.type !== type) continue
      const key = line.row !== none && tree.nodes.get(line.row)?.category.type === type ? line.row : none
      own.set(key, (own.get(key) ?? 0) + line.amountBase)
    }
    const totals = new Map<string, number>()
    const totalOf = (node: KindNode<C>): number => {
      const sum = (own.get(node.category.id) ?? 0) + node.children.reduce((s, child) => s + totalOf(child), 0)
      totals.set(node.category.id, sum)
      return sum
    }
    for (const root of tree[type].roots) totalOf(root)
    const walk = (node: KindNode<C>, level: number): void => {
      const amountBase = totals.get(node.category.id) ?? 0
      if (amountBase === 0) return
      out.push({ row: node.category.id, type, level, amountBase })
      for (const child of node.children) walk(child, level + 1)
    }
    for (const root of tree[type].roots) walk(root, 0)
    const rest = own.get(none) ?? 0
    if (rest !== 0) out.push({ row: none, type, level: 0, amountBase: rest })
  }
  return out
}

/** The rank of a flat report row: the categories in the caller's order (`members`), the uncategorised row after them, unknown ids last. */
export function rowRank<C extends KindCategory>(categories: readonly C[]): (type: MoneyType, row: string) => number {
  const tree = buildKindTree(categories)
  const rank = new Map<string, number>()
  for (const type of ['income', 'expense'] as const) tree[type].members.forEach((id, i) => rank.set(`${type}|${id}`, i))
  return (type, row) => rank.get(`${type}|${row}`) ?? (row === noCategoryId(type) ? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER - 1 : Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER)
}
```

`lib/domain/journal/csv.ts`: після функції `buildCsv` додати:

```ts

/**
 * Several tables in one file (the sheets of a report, S12): each under its title row, an empty line between them; the
 * cells, the delimiter, the BOM and the line ends of `buildCsv`.
 */
export function buildCsvSections(sections: ReadonlyArray<{ title: string; header: string[]; rows: string[][] }>): string {
  const lines = sections.flatMap((s, i) => [
    ...(i === 0 ? [] : ['']),
    csvCell(s.title),
    ...[s.header, ...s.rows].map((cells) => cells.map(csvCell).join(DELIMITER)),
  ])
  return `﻿${lines.join('\r\n')}\r\n`
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `export.ts`**

Створити `lib/domain/reports/export.ts`:

```ts
// lib/domain/reports/export.ts
// The export of a report (S12): the same response the page shows, as three sheets («Звіт», «За категоріями», «Звірка»),
// then a CSV with the journal's rules (BOM, `;`, CRLF, formula injection defused) or an XLSX (lib/server/reports/xlsx.ts).
// The words come from the caller (the message catalogs `reports.export`), so this file has no language of its own.
import { buildCsvSections, formatAmount } from '@/lib/domain/journal/csv'
import type { KindCategory } from '@/lib/domain/analytics-filter/kinds'
import { categoryTreeRows, rowRank } from './categories'
import type { CashFlowReport, CategoryLine, MoneyType, PLReport, Reconciliation } from './types'

/** A text, or an amount in minor units (a number in XLSX, the language's decimal sign in CSV). */
export type ExportCell = string | { minor: number }

export interface ExportSheet {
  name: string
  header: string[]
  rows: ExportCell[][]
}

export interface ExportLabels {
  sheets: { report: string; categories: string; reconciliation: string }
  columns: {
    month: string
    day: string
    income: string
    expense: string
    net: string
    running: string
    category: string
    type: string
    amount: string
    total: string
    line: string
    account: string
    currency: string
    opening: string
    closing: string
    fx: string
  }
  types: { income: string; expense: string }
  rows: { total: string; totalIncome: string; totalExpense: string; profit: string; excludedIncome: string; excludedExpense: string; noCategory: string }
  lines: { opening: string; net: string; excluded: string; included: string; outside: string; transfers: string; fx: string; closing: string; discrepancy: string; unvalued: string }
}

export interface ExportContext {
  labels: ExportLabels
  /** A day (`YYYY-MM-DD`) or a month (`YYYY-MM`) as the UI language writes it. */
  periodLabel: (key: string) => string
  /** The name of a report row: a category (a default one in the UI language) or the uncategorised row. */
  rowName: (row: string) => string
  accountName: (accountId: string) => string
  /** The company's categories, in the order the tree and the rows follow. */
  categories: readonly KindCategory[]
}

const INDENT = '    '

function money(minor: number | null): ExportCell {
  return minor === null ? '' : { minor }
}

function withCurrency(label: string, currency: string): string {
  return `${label}, ${currency}`
}

function categorySheet(lines: readonly CategoryLine[], currency: string, ctx: ExportContext): ExportSheet {
  const { labels } = ctx
  return {
    name: labels.sheets.categories,
    header: [labels.columns.category, labels.columns.type, withCurrency(labels.columns.amount, currency)],
    rows: categoryTreeRows(lines, ctx.categories).map((r) => [`${INDENT.repeat(r.level)}${ctx.rowName(r.row)}`, labels.types[r.type], money(r.amountBase)]),
  }
}

/** «Звірка»: the lines of the equation as the page shows them, then the accounts with their balances. */
export function reconciliationSheet(rec: Reconciliation, currency: string, ctx: ExportContext): ExportSheet {
  const { lines, columns } = ctx.labels
  const rows: ExportCell[][] = [
    [lines.opening, money(rec.openingBase)],
    [lines.net, money(rec.reportNetBase)],
    rec.excludedBase === null ? [lines.included, ''] : [lines.excluded, money(rec.excludedBase)],
  ]
  if (rec.outsideFilterBase !== 0) rows.push([lines.outside, money(rec.outsideFilterBase)])
  if (rec.transfersBase !== 0) rows.push([lines.transfers, money(rec.transfersBase)])
  if (rec.rates.length > 0) rows.push([lines.fx, money(rec.fxBase)])
  rows.push([lines.closing, money(rec.closingBase)])
  if (rec.discrepancyBase !== 0) rows.push([lines.discrepancy, money(rec.discrepancyBase)])
  if (!rec.valued) rows.push([lines.unvalued, [...new Set(rec.missingRates.map((m) => m.currency))].join(', ')])
  rows.push([])
  rows.push([
    columns.account,
    columns.currency,
    columns.opening,
    columns.closing,
    withCurrency(columns.opening, currency),
    withCurrency(columns.closing, currency),
    withCurrency(columns.fx, currency),
  ])
  for (const a of rec.accounts) {
    rows.push([ctx.accountName(a.accountId), a.currency, money(a.openingMinor), money(a.closingMinor), money(a.openingBase), money(a.closingBase), money(a.fxBase)])
  }
  return { name: ctx.labels.sheets.reconciliation, header: [columns.line, withCurrency(columns.amount, currency)], rows }
}

export function cashFlowSheets(report: CashFlowReport, ctx: ExportContext): ExportSheet[] {
  const { columns, rows: names, lines, sheets } = ctx.labels
  const c = report.currency
  const body: ExportCell[][] = report.buckets.map((b) => [ctx.periodLabel(b.key), money(b.incomeBase), money(b.expenseBase), money(b.netBase), money(b.runningNetBase)])
  body.push([names.total, money(report.totals.incomeBase), money(report.totals.expenseBase), money(report.totals.netBase), ''])
  const excluded = report.excluded
  if (excluded !== null && (excluded.incomeBase !== 0 || excluded.expenseBase !== 0)) {
    body.push([lines.excluded, money(excluded.incomeBase), money(excluded.expenseBase), money(excluded.incomeBase - excluded.expenseBase), ''])
  }
  return [
    {
      name: sheets.report,
      header: [
        report.granularity === 'day' ? columns.day : columns.month,
        withCurrency(columns.income, c),
        withCurrency(columns.expense, c),
        withCurrency(columns.net, c),
        withCurrency(columns.running, c),
      ],
      rows: body,
    },
    categorySheet(report.categories, c, ctx),
    reconciliationSheet(report.reconciliation, c, ctx),
  ]
}

export function plSheets(report: PLReport, ctx: ExportContext): ExportSheet[] {
  const { columns, rows: names, types, sheets } = ctx.labels
  const c = report.currency
  const rank = rowRank(ctx.categories)
  const width = report.months.length
  const ordered = [...report.rows].sort((a, b) => (a.type === b.type ? rank(a.type, a.row) - rank(b.type, b.row) : a.type === 'income' ? -1 : 1))
  const columnSums = (type: MoneyType): number[] =>
    report.months.map((_, i) => report.rows.filter((r) => r.type === type).reduce((s, r) => s + r.values[i], 0))
  const income = columnSums('income')
  const expense = columnSums('expense')
  const body: ExportCell[][] = []
  for (const type of ['income', 'expense'] as const) {
    for (const r of ordered.filter((x) => x.type === type)) body.push([ctx.rowName(r.row), types[type], ...r.values.map(money), money(r.total)])
    body.push(
      type === 'income'
        ? [names.totalIncome, '', ...income.map(money), money(report.totals.incomeBase)]
        : [names.totalExpense, '', ...expense.map(money), money(report.totals.expenseBase)],
    )
  }
  body.push([names.profit, '', ...income.map((v, i) => money(v - expense[i])), money(report.totals.profitBase)])
  const blank = (): ExportCell[] => Array.from({ length: width }, (): ExportCell => '')
  if (report.excluded !== null && report.excluded.incomeBase !== 0) body.push([names.excludedIncome, '', ...blank(), money(report.excluded.incomeBase)])
  if (report.excluded !== null && report.excluded.expenseBase !== 0) body.push([names.excludedExpense, '', ...blank(), money(report.excluded.expenseBase)])
  return [
    { name: sheets.report, header: [columns.category, columns.type, ...report.months.map(ctx.periodLabel), withCurrency(columns.total, c)], rows: body },
    categorySheet(report.rows.map((r) => ({ row: r.row, type: r.type, amountBase: r.total })), c, ctx),
    reconciliationSheet(report.reconciliation, c, ctx),
  ]
}

/** The sheets as one CSV file: one section per sheet, amounts with `decimal` (the journal's CSV rules). */
export function sheetsToCsv(sheets: readonly ExportSheet[], decimal: ',' | '.'): string {
  const text = (cell: ExportCell): string => (typeof cell === 'string' ? cell : formatAmount(cell.minor, decimal))
  return buildCsvSections(sheets.map((s) => ({ title: s.name, header: s.header, rows: s.rows.map((row) => row.map(text)) })))
}
```

```ts
beforeEach(async () => {

  await create({ type: 'income', accountId: 'bank', amountMinor: 50_000, paymentDate: '2026-10-05' })
  await create({ type: 'expense', accountId: 'bank', amountMinor: 12_000, paymentDate: '2026-10-06' })
  await create({ type: 'transfer', accountId: 'bank', toAccountId: 'cash', amountMinor: 30_000, paymentDate: '2026-10-20' })
  await create({ type: 'expense', status: 'planned', accountId: 'bank', amountMinor: 7_000, paymentDate: '2026-10-30' })
})

describe('balancesAt (shared by the journal and the reports)', () => {
  it('the account minus what moved it later; today and later are the account itself; a planned operation moves nothing', async () => {
    const stats: BalanceReadStats = { docsRead: 0, aggregates: 0 }
    const list = await accounts()
    const days = ['2026-10-04', '2026-10-05', '2026-10-19', '2026-10-20', TODAY, '2026-12-31']
    const result = await readSnapshot(db, (reader) => balancesAt(reader, deps, ORG, list, days, TODAY, stats))
    expect(Object.fromEntries(result.get('bank') ?? [])).toEqual({
      '2026-10-04': 100_000, '2026-10-05': 150_000, '2026-10-19': 138_000, '2026-10-20': 108_000, [TODAY]: 108_000, '2026-12-31': 108_000,
    })
    expect(Object.fromEntries(result.get('cash') ?? [])).toEqual({
      '2026-10-04': 0, '2026-10-05': 0, '2026-10-19': 0, '2026-10-20': 30_000, [TODAY]: 30_000, '2026-12-31': 30_000,
    })
    // Three sum aggregates per account and past day; the history before a day is never read.
    expect(stats.aggregates).toBe(3 * 2 * 4)
  })
})
```

Створити `lib/server/ledger/reader.ts`:

```ts
// lib/server/ledger/reader.ts
// The reads of one request through one interface (S12): plainly (the journal) or inside one read-only transaction, so every
// read sees the same snapshot and a write landing in the middle cannot make a report disagree with itself.
import type {
  AggregateQuery,
  AggregateQuerySnapshot,
  AggregateSpec,
  DocumentReference,
  DocumentSnapshot,
  Firestore,
  Query,
  QuerySnapshot,
  Transaction,
} from 'firebase-admin/firestore'

export interface Reader {
  query(q: Query): Promise<QuerySnapshot>
  aggregate<T extends AggregateSpec>(q: AggregateQuery<T>): Promise<AggregateQuerySnapshot<T>>
  getAll(refs: readonly DocumentReference[]): Promise<DocumentSnapshot[]>
}

export function directReader(db: Firestore): Reader {
  return {
    query: (q) => q.get(),
    aggregate: (q) => q.get(),
    getAll: (refs) => (refs.length === 0 ? Promise.resolve([]) : db.getAll(...refs)),
  }
}

export function transactionReader(t: Transaction): Reader {
  return {
    query: (q) => t.get(q),
    aggregate: (q) => t.get(q),
    getAll: (refs) => (refs.length === 0 ? Promise.resolve([]) : t.getAll(...refs)),
  }
}

/** Runs every read of `fn` in one read-only transaction: one consistent snapshot, no locks, nothing can be written. */
export function readSnapshot<T>(db: Firestore, fn: (reader: Reader) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  return db.runTransaction((t) => fn(transactionReader(t)), { readOnly: true })
}
```

Створити `lib/server/ledger/balance-at.ts` (тіла `netAfterDay`, `arrivalsQuery`, `arrivalShift` ті самі, що в журналі, лише читають через `Reader`):

```ts
// lib/server/ledger/balance-at.ts
// The balance of an account at the close of a day (S08 rule, shared by the journal and, since S12, the reports): the
// account's own `balanceMinor` minus everything that moved it after that day. The movement after a day comes from three
// `sum` aggregate queries over the actual operations, not from the rollups: Firestore cannot `sum` over a range of
// document ids. They are billed by index entries, so the cost does not grow with the history before the day.
import { AggregateField, FieldPath, type Query } from 'firebase-admin/firestore'
import { arrivalShiftOf } from '@/lib/domain/journal/balance'
import { addDays } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/days'
import { parseTransaction } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/doc'
import { MAX_ARRIVAL_DAYS, type LedgerAccount } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/types'
import type { LedgerDeps } from './deps'
import { transactionsCol } from './paths'
import type { Reader } from './reader'

/** What the balance reads cost: the aggregate queries and the operation documents of the arrival correction. */
export interface BalanceReadStats {
  docsRead: number
  aggregates: number
}

/**
 * The transfers into the account paid within MAX_ARRIVAL_DAYS of `day`: the only ones whose arrival day (S10b) can be on
 * the other side of `day` than their payment day. The listing query of the journal (index `toAccountId`, `paymentDate`,
 * `createdAt`), so no index of its own. Exported for the rollout check (scripts/check-transfers.ts).
 */
export function arrivalsQuery(deps: Pick<LedgerDeps, 'db'>, orgId: string, accountId: string, day: string): Query {
  return transactionsCol(deps.db, orgId)
    .where('toAccountId', '==', accountId)
    .where('paymentDate', '>=', addDays(day, -MAX_ARRIVAL_DAYS))
    .where('paymentDate', '<=', addDays(day, MAX_ARRIVAL_DAYS))
    .orderBy('paymentDate', 'desc')
    .orderBy('createdAt', 'desc')
    .orderBy(FieldPath.documentId(), 'desc')
}

/** The sums by payment day place the money in of a transfer on the day it left; this moves it to the day it arrived. */
async function arrivalShift(reader: Reader, deps: Pick<LedgerDeps, 'db'>, orgId: string, accountId: string, day: string, stats: BalanceReadStats): Promise<number> {
  const snap = await reader.query(arrivalsQuery(deps, orgId, accountId, day))
  stats.docsRead += snap.size
  let shift = 0
  for (const d of snap.docs) {
    const tx = parseTransaction(d.id, d.data())
    if (tx) shift += arrivalShiftOf(tx, day)
  }
  return shift
}

/**
 * The net movement of an account on every day after `day`: money in (income), money out (expense and transfer out, both
 * `amountMinor`) and transfers into the account (`toAmountMinor`), corrected by the arrival days of transfers (S10b).
 */
export async function netAfterDay(reader: Reader, deps: Pick<LedgerDeps, 'db'>, orgId: string, accountId: string, day: string, stats: BalanceReadStats): Promise<number> {
  const actualAfter = transactionsCol(deps.db, orgId).where('status', '==', 'actual').where('paymentDate', '>', day)
  const sum = async (q: Query, field: string): Promise<number> => {
    const agg = await reader.aggregate(q.aggregate({ total: AggregateField.sum(field) }))
    stats.aggregates++
    return agg.data().total ?? 0
  }
  const [income, outflow, transferIn, arrivals] = await Promise.all([
    sum(actualAfter.where('accountId', '==', accountId).where('type', '==', 'income'), 'amountMinor'),
    sum(actualAfter.where('accountId', '==', accountId).where('type', 'in', ['expense', 'transfer']), 'amountMinor'),
    sum(actualAfter.where('toAccountId', '==', accountId), 'toAmountMinor'),
    arrivalShift(reader, deps, orgId, accountId, day, stats),
  ])
  return income - outflow + transferIn + arrivals
}

/**
 * The balance of each account at the close of each day: `balanceMinor` for today and later (an actual operation is never
 * after today), else `balanceMinor` minus the movement after the day. Account id -> day -> balance.
 */
export async function balancesAt(
  reader: Reader,
  deps: Pick<LedgerDeps, 'db'>,
  orgId: string,
  accounts: readonly LedgerAccount[],
  days: readonly string[],
  today: string,
  stats: BalanceReadStats,
): Promise<Map<string, Map<string, number>>> {
  const out = new Map<string, Map<string, number>>()
  await Promise.all(
    accounts.map(async (account) => {
      const byDay = new Map<string, number>()
      await Promise.all(
        [...new Set(days)].map(async (day) => {
          byDay.set(day, day >= today ? account.balanceMinor : account.balanceMinor - (await netAfterDay(reader, deps, orgId, account.id, day, stats)))
        }),
      )
      out.set(account.id, byDay)
    }),
  )
  return out
}
```

```ts
const NBP_ORIGIN = 'https://api.nbp.pl'
const TABLES_PATH = '/api/exchangerates/tables/A'

/** `NBP_API_BASE` points the e2e run at the stub (tests/e2e/helpers/nbp-stub.mjs); unset means the real NBP. Tests only. */
function tablesBase(): string {
  const origin = process.env.NBP_API_BASE?.replace(/\/+$/, '') || NBP_ORIGIN
  return `${origin}${TABLES_PATH}`
}
```

Створити `tests/e2e/helpers/nbp-stub.mjs`:

```js
// A stand-in for the NBP API (api.nbp.pl) for the Playwright run: the dev server is started with NBP_API_BASE pointing here,
// so no test ever calls the real NBP. Table A for every weekday of the asked range (holidays are not modelled): EUR 4.30,
// USD 4.00, UAH 0.10. Table numbers count the weekdays of the year, so a month read twice never shows a gap
// (`validateRefresh` in lib/server/fx/rates.ts refuses a gap). A range without a weekday is 404, as NBP answers.
//   GET /api/exchangerates/tables/A/{from}/{to}/?format=json
import http from 'node:http'

const PORT = 4012
const MID = { EUR: 4.3, USD: 4.0, UAH: 0.1 }
const DAY_MS = 86_400_000

const dayOf = (d) => d.toISOString().slice(0, 10)
const isWeekday = (d) => d.getUTCDay() !== 0 && d.getUTCDay() !== 6

function tableNo(d) {
  let n = 0
  for (let x = new Date(Date.UTC(d.getUTCFullYear(), 0, 1)); x <= d; x = new Date(x.getTime() + DAY_MS)) if (isWeekday(x)) n++
  return `${String(n).padStart(3, '0')}/A/NBP/${d.getUTCFullYear()}`
}

http
  .createServer((req, res) => {
    const url = new URL(req.url ?? '/', `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`)
    if (url.pathname === '/health') {
      res.end('ok')
      return
    }
    const match = /^\/api\/exchangerates\/tables\/A\/(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})\/(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})\/?$/.exec(url.pathname)
    if (!match) {
      res.statusCode = 400
      res.end('400 BadRequest')
      return
    }
    const tables = []
    for (let d = new Date(`${match[1]}T00:00:00Z`); dayOf(d) <= match[2]; d = new Date(d.getTime() + DAY_MS)) {
      if (!isWeekday(d)) continue
      tables.push({ table: 'A', no: tableNo(d), effectiveDate: dayOf(d), rates: Object.entries(MID).map(([code, mid]) => ({ currency: code, code, mid })) })
    }
    if (tables.length === 0) {
      res.statusCode = 404
      res.end('404 NotFound')
      return
    }
    res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json')
    res.end(JSON.stringify(tables))
  })
  .listen(PORT, '127.0.0.1')
```

Створити `tests/integration/report-helpers.ts`:

```ts
// The company of the S12 integration tests, written through the ledger so the rollups are real.
import type { Firestore } from 'firebase-admin/firestore'
import { addDays } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/days'
import { parseTransactionInput } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/input'
import type { RateSnapshot } from '@/lib/server/fx/types'
import type { LedgerDeps } from '@/lib/server/ledger/deps'
import { createTransaction } from '@/lib/server/ledger/write'
import { ORG, TODAY, UID, clearFirestore, seedAccount, seedCategory, seedOrg, seedTag } from './helpers'

/** EUR in PLN: 4.00 asked up to 2026-10-01, 4.20 up to 2026-10-15, 4.40 after; identity for one currency. */
export const steppedRate: LedgerDeps['resolveRate'] = async (from, to, date): Promise<RateSnapshot> => {
  const same = from === to
  const rate = same ? 1 : date <= '2026-10-01' ? 4 : date <= '2026-10-15' ? 4.2 : 4.4
  return {
    rate, from, to, requestedDate: date, rateDate: same ? date : addDays(date, -1), tableNo: same ? null : `T/${date}`,
    source: same ? 'identity' : 'NBP_A', final: date <= TODAY,
  }
}

/**
 * «pln» from 1000 zł and «eur» from 100 €; in October a sale of 500 (k-in), an expense of 120 (k-ex), VAT of 230 tagged
 * «ПДВ» (excluded from analytics), 50 € in on «eur» (210 zł at 4.20, k-in), a transfer of 430 zł for 100 € on the 20th and a
 * planned expense of 70 zł on the 30th. Today is 2026-10-27.
 */
export async function seedReportCompany(db: Firestore, deps: LedgerDeps): Promise<void> {
  await clearFirestore()
  await seedOrg(db)
  await seedAccount(db, ORG, 'pln', 'PLN', 100_000)
  await seedAccount(db, ORG, 'eur', 'EUR', 10_000)
  await seedCategory(db, ORG, 'k-in', 'income')
  await seedCategory(db, ORG, 'k-ex', 'expense')
  await seedTag(db, ORG, 'vat', { name: 'ПДВ', excludeFromAnalytics: true })
  const create = (body: Record<string, unknown>) => createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, parseTransactionInput(body))
  await create({ type: 'income', accountId: 'pln', amountMinor: 50_000, paymentDate: '2026-10-05', categoryId: 'k-in' })
  await create({ type: 'expense', accountId: 'pln', amountMinor: 12_000, paymentDate: '2026-10-06', categoryId: 'k-ex' })
  await create({ type: 'expense', accountId: 'pln', amountMinor: 23_000, paymentDate: '2026-10-07', tags: ['vat'] })
  await create({ type: 'income', accountId: 'eur', amountMinor: 5_000, paymentDate: '2026-10-10', categoryId: 'k-in' })
  await create({ type: 'transfer', accountId: 'pln', toAccountId: 'eur', amountMinor: 43_000, toAmountMinor: 10_000, paymentDate: '2026-10-20' })
  await create({ type: 'expense', status: 'planned', accountId: 'pln', amountMinor: 7_000, paymentDate: '2026-10-30' })
}
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS } from '@/lib/domain/analytics-filter/filter'
import { parseTransactionInput } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/input'
import type { ReportQuery } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import { FxError } from '@/lib/server/fx/types'
import { verifyLedger } from '@/lib/server/ledger/verify'
import { createTransaction } from '@/lib/server/ledger/write'
import { readCashFlowReport, readPLReport } from '@/lib/server/reports/read'
import { ORG, UID, makeDeps, testDb } from './helpers'
import { seedReportCompany, steppedRate } from './report-helpers'

const db = testDb()
const deps = makeDeps(db, { resolveRate: steppedRate })
const OCT: ReportQuery = { from: '2026-10-01', to: '2026-10-31', filters: EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS }
const withoutCoverage = <T extends { coverage: unknown }>(report: T) => ({ ...report, coverage: null })

beforeEach(async () => {
  await seedReportCompany(db, deps)
})

describe('Cash Flow on the server (S12)', () => {
  it('whole months from the rollups equal the same report from the operations; verifyLedger is clean', async () => {
    const fromRollups = await readCashFlowReport(deps, ORG, OCT)
    const fromOperations = await readCashFlowReport(deps, ORG, OCT, { source: 'operations' })
    expect(fromRollups.report.coverage.source).toBe('rollups')
    expect(fromOperations.report.coverage).toMatchObject({ source: 'operations', uncountable: 0 })
    expect(withoutCoverage(fromRollups.report)).toEqual(withoutCoverage(fromOperations.report))
    expect(fromRollups.report.totals).toEqual({ incomeBase: 71_000, expenseBase: 12_000, netBase: 59_000 })
    expect(fromRollups.report.excluded).toEqual({ incomeBase: 0, expenseBase: 23_000 })
    expect((await verifyLedger(deps, ORG)).ok).toBe(true)
  })

  it('from the rollups it reads the daily documents of the period and two month documents, never the operations of the period', async () => {
    const { stats, report } = await readCashFlowReport(deps, ORG, OCT)
    // pln: the 5th, the 6th, the 20th and the x_ twin of the 7th; eur: the 10th and the 20th; cashcat_ and x_cashcat_ of October.
    expect(stats.rollups).toBe(8)
    expect(report.coverage).toMatchObject({ source: 'rollups', rollupsRead: 8, uncountable: null })
  })

  it('the reconciliation adds up across currencies: the exchange difference of the EUR account, the transfer in PLN at the NBP rate', async () => {
    const { reconciliation } = (await readCashFlowReport(deps, ORG, OCT)).report
    expect(reconciliation).toMatchObject({
      openingDay: '2026-09-30', closingDay: '2026-10-27', valued: true,
      openingBase: 140_000, reportNetBase: 59_000, excludedBase: -23_000, outsideFilterBase: 0,
      transfersBase: 1_000, fxBase: 5_000, closingBase: 182_000, discrepancyBase: 0,
    })
    expect(reconciliation.accounts.find((a) => a.accountId === 'eur')).toMatchObject({ openingMinor: 10_000, closingMinor: 25_000, fxBase: 5_000 })
  })

  it('a concrete tag counts the excluded operation; the rest of the accounts is outside the filter', async () => {
    const { report } = await readCashFlowReport(deps, ORG, { ...OCT, filters: { ...EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS, tagIds: ['vat'] } })
    expect(report.coverage.source).toBe('operations')
    expect(report.totals).toEqual({ incomeBase: 0, expenseBase: 23_000, netBase: -23_000 })
    expect([report.excluded, report.excludedCounted]).toEqual([null, true])
    expect(report.reconciliation).toMatchObject({ excludedIncluded: true, excludedBase: null, reportNetBase: -23_000, outsideFilterBase: 59_000, discrepancyBase: 0 })
  })

  it('one account: its line only, and its transfers with the other accounts are a reconciliation line', async () => {
    const { report } = await readCashFlowReport(deps, ORG, { ...OCT, filters: { ...EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS, accountIds: ['eur'] } })
    expect(report.accounts.map((a) => a.accountId)).toEqual(['eur'])
    expect(report.totals.incomeBase).toBe(21_000)
    expect(report.reconciliation).toMatchObject({ openingBase: 40_000, reportNetBase: 21_000, transfersBase: 44_000, fxBase: 5_000, closingBase: 110_000, discrepancyBase: 0 })
  })

  it('a planned operation is in no report', async () => {
    const cashFlow = (await readCashFlowReport(deps, ORG, OCT, { source: 'operations' })).report
    expect(cashFlow.totals.expenseBase).toBe(12_000)
    const pl = (await readPLReport(deps, ORG, OCT, { source: 'operations' })).report
    expect(pl.totals.expenseBase).toBe(12_000)
  })

  it('above the read limit the answer is a refusal with the cap, never a partial figure', async () => {
    const tagged: ReportQuery = { ...OCT, filters: { ...EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS, tagIds: ['vat'] } }
    await expect(readCashFlowReport(deps, ORG, tagged, { cap: 3 })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'too_many_operations', cap: 3 })
    await expect(readCashFlowReport(deps, ORG, OCT, { cap: 3 })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'too_many_operations', cap: 3 })
    expect((await readCashFlowReport(deps, ORG, tagged, { cap: 50 })).report.totals.expenseBase).toBe(23_000)
  })

  it('a missing NBP rate leaves the reconciliation unvalued and names it; the figures of the report stay', async () => {
    const down = makeDeps(db, {
      resolveRate: async (from, to, date) => {
        if (from !== to) throw new FxError('provider_unavailable')
        return steppedRate(from, to, date)
      },
    })
    const { report } = await readCashFlowReport(down, ORG, OCT)
    expect(report.totals.netBase).toBe(59_000)
    expect(report.reconciliation).toMatchObject({ valued: false, discrepancyBase: null })
    expect(report.reconciliation.missingRates).toEqual([
      { currency: 'EUR', date: '2026-10-01' },
      { currency: 'EUR', date: '2026-10-20' },
      { currency: 'EUR', date: '2026-10-28' },
    ])
  })
})

describe('P&L on the server (S12)', () => {
  it('an accrual period: whole months from the rollups equal the operations; a part of a month is its share of the days', async () => {
    await createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, parseTransactionInput({
      type: 'expense', accountId: 'pln', amountMinor: 61_000, paymentDate: '2026-10-12', categoryId: 'k-ex', accrual: { mode: 'period', from: '2026-09-15', to: '2026-11-14' },
    }))
    const quarter: ReportQuery = { from: '2026-09-01', to: '2026-11-30', filters: EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS }
    const fromRollups = await readPLReport(deps, ORG, quarter)
    const fromOperations = await readPLReport(deps, ORG, quarter, { source: 'operations' })
    expect(withoutCoverage(fromRollups.report)).toEqual(withoutCoverage(fromOperations.report))
    expect(fromRollups.report.months).toEqual(['2026-09', '2026-10', '2026-11'])
    expect(fromRollups.report.rows.find((r) => r.row === 'k-ex')?.values).toEqual([16_000, 12_000 + 31_000, 14_000])
    const part = await readPLReport(deps, ORG, { from: '2026-10-10', to: '2026-10-20', filters: EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS })
    expect(part.report.coverage.source).toBe('operations')
    expect(part.report.rows.find((r) => r.row === 'k-ex')?.values).toEqual([11_000])
  })

  it('the P&L has the same reconciliation as the Cash Flow of the period', async () => {
    const pl = (await readPLReport(deps, ORG, OCT)).report
    const cashFlow = (await readCashFlowReport(deps, ORG, OCT)).report
    expect(pl.reconciliation).toEqual(cashFlow.reconciliation)
  })
})
```

```ts
})

describe('balances on a day (S12)', () => {

    const { report } = await readBalances(deps, ORG, '2026-10-15')
    expect(report.accounts.map((a) => [a.accountId, a.balanceMinor, a.baseMinor])).toEqual([
      ['eur', 15_000, 66_000],
      ['pln', 115_000, 115_000],

    const { report, stats } = await readBalances(deps, ORG, '2026-12-31')
    expect(report.date).toBe(TODAY)
    expect(report.accounts.map((a) => [a.accountId, a.balanceMinor])).toEqual([
      ['eur', 25_000],
      ['pln', 72_000],

})

describe('the dashboard «Гроші зараз» (S12)', () => {

    await db.doc(`organizations/${ORG}/bankAccounts/ba0`).set({ connectionId: 'c0', ebAccountUid: 'u0', accountId: 'pln', currency: 'PLN', importFrom: '2026-01-01', status: 'disconnected', lastSyncAt: '2026-10-27T09:00:00.000Z' })
```

Створити `lib/server/reports/rates.ts`:

```ts
// lib/server/reports/rates.ts
import { rateKey } from '@/lib/domain/reports/reconcile'
import type { RateNeed, RateQuote } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import { FxError } from '@/lib/server/fx/types'
import type { LedgerDeps } from '@/lib/server/ledger/deps'

/**
 * The NBP rates a report needs, each distinct (currency, day) asked once: the memo of one request, gone with its response
 * (the months behind them stay in the resolver's own process cache, S04). A rate NBP cannot give (no table before the day,
 * an unknown currency, NBP down) is left out, so the reconciliation names it instead of the whole report failing.
 */
export async function quoteRates(resolve: LedgerDeps['resolveRate'], baseCurrency: string, needs: readonly RateNeed[]): Promise<Map<string, RateQuote>> {
  const book = new Map<string, RateQuote>()
  const distinct = new Map(needs.map((n) => [rateKey(n.currency, n.date), n]))
  await Promise.all(
    [...distinct].map(async ([key, n]) => {
      try {
        const s = await resolve(n.currency, baseCurrency, n.date)
        book.set(key, { rate: s.rate, rateDate: s.rateDate, tableNo: s.tableNo, final: s.final })
      } catch (e) {
        if (!(e instanceof FxError)) throw e
      }
    }),
  )
  return book
}
```
