Створити `lib/server/reports/read.ts`:

```ts
// lib/server/reports/read.ts
// The server reports (S12). All the reads of one request are one read-only snapshot (`readSnapshot`), then the pure
// builders of lib/domain/reports. Without filters over whole months the figures come from the rollups (the daily
// documents of the period's accounts and two documents a month); otherwise from the operations of the period. Either way
// the queries are counted first in the same snapshot: more than the cap is a refusal before any document is read.
import { FieldPath, type Firestore, type Query, type QueryDocumentSnapshot } from 'firebase-admin/firestore'
import { filterForAnalytics, includesExcluded } from '@/lib/domain/analytics-filter/filter'
import { categoryTypesOf, type CategoryTypes } from '@/lib/domain/analytics-filter/kinds'
import { matchesSharedChoices } from '@/lib/domain/journal/filter'
import { addDays, monthsBetween } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/days'
import { parseAccount, parseTransaction } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/doc'
import { LedgerError } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/errors'
import { addInto, cashCatDocId, cashDocId, excludedDocId, flatten, plDocId, type RollupDocs } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/rollups'
import { MAX_ARRIVAL_DAYS, type LedgerAccount, type LedgerTransaction } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/types'
import { buildCashFlowCore } from '@/lib/domain/reports/cashflow'
import { parseReportCategory, type ReportCategory } from '@/lib/domain/reports/categories'
import { ReportError } from '@/lib/domain/reports/errors'
import { factsFromRollupDocs, operationRollups, selectRollupDocs, type DayRange } from '@/lib/domain/reports/facts'
import { buildPLCore } from '@/lib/domain/reports/pl'
import { reportSource } from '@/lib/domain/reports/query'
import { ratesForReconciliation, reconcile, reconciliationDays, type ReconcileAccount } from '@/lib/domain/reports/reconcile'
import {
  REPORT_SCAN_CAP,
  type CashFlowReport,
  type Coverage,
  type PLReport,
  type Reconciliation,
  type ReportFacts,
  type ReportKind,
  type ReportQuery,
  type ReportSource,
} from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import { balancesAt, type BalanceReadStats } from '@/lib/server/ledger/balance-at'
import type { LedgerDeps } from '@/lib/server/ledger/deps'
import { accountsCol, categoriesCol, orgRef, rollupsCol, transactionsCol } from '@/lib/server/ledger/paths'
import { readSnapshot, type Reader } from '@/lib/server/ledger/reader'
import { quoteRates } from './rates'

/** The fields a report reads of an operation: what `parseTransaction` checks and what `contributions` and the filters use. */
export const REPORT_FIELDS = [
  'schemaVersion', 'type', 'status', 'accountId', 'amountMinor', 'currency', 'paymentDate', 'accrual', 'isRecurring', 'tags', 'fileUrls',
  'toAccountId', 'toAmountMinor', 'toCurrency', 'toDate', 'categoryId', 'counterpartyId', 'base', 'excluded',
] as const

/** What one request read: operation documents, rollup documents, aggregate queries (count and sum). */
export interface ReportReadStats {
  operations: number
  rollups: number
  aggregates: number
}

export interface ReportContext {
  baseCurrency: string
  accounts: LedgerAccount[]
  categories: ReportCategory[]
  categoryTypes: CategoryTypes
}

export interface ReadReportOptions {
  /** Tests and the rollout check: another read limit, or one path forced. */
  cap?: number
  source?: ReportSource
}

export interface ReportRead<T> {
  report: T
  stats: ReportReadStats
  /** The company's accounts and categories the report was built with (names for the export). */
  context: ReportContext
}

/** Operations paid in [from, to] (index `transactions (status ASC, paymentDate ASC)`, S11). */
export function paymentsQuery(db: Firestore, orgId: string, from: string, to: string): Query {
  return transactionsCol(db, orgId).where('status', '==', 'actual').where('paymentDate', '>=', from).where('paymentDate', '<=', to)
}

/** Operations whose accrual day is in [from, to] (the automatic single-field index of `accrual.date`; planned ones too). */
export function accrualDaysQuery(db: Firestore, orgId: string, from: string, to: string): Query {
  return transactionsCol(db, orgId).where('accrual.date', '>=', from).where('accrual.date', '<=', to)
}

/** Operations whose accrual period ends on or after `from` (the automatic single-field index of `accrual.to`); the start is checked in memory. */
export function accrualPeriodsQuery(db: Firestore, orgId: string, from: string): Query {
  return transactionsCol(db, orgId).where('accrual.to', '>=', from)
}

/** One account's daily rollups of [from, to], or their `x_` twins, by document id (the built-in `__name__` index). */
export function cellsQuery(db: Firestore, orgId: string, accountId: string, from: string, to: string, excluded: boolean): Query {
  const col = rollupsCol(db, orgId)
  const id = (day: string): string => (excluded ? excludedDocId(cashDocId(accountId, day)) : cashDocId(accountId, day))
  return col.where(FieldPath.documentId(), '>=', col.doc(id(from))).where(FieldPath.documentId(), '<=', col.doc(id(to)))
}

/** The company currency, its accounts (schema 2) and, unless `withCategories` is false, its categories. */
export async function readContext(r: Reader, db: Firestore, orgId: string, withCategories = true): Promise<ReportContext> {
  const [org] = await r.getAll([orgRef(db, orgId)])
  if (!org?.exists) throw new LedgerError('not_found', 'org')
  const [accountsSnap, categoriesSnap] = await Promise.all([r.query(accountsCol(db, orgId)), withCategories ? r.query(categoriesCol(db, orgId)) : Promise.resolve(null)])
  const accounts = accountsSnap.docs.map((d) => parseAccount(d.id, d.data())).filter((a): a is LedgerAccount => a !== null)
  const categories = (categoriesSnap?.docs ?? []).map((d) => parseReportCategory(d.id, d.data())).filter((c): c is ReportCategory => c !== null)
  return { baseCurrency: org.get('currency') as string, accounts, categories, categoryTypes: categoryTypesOf(categories) }
}

interface Budget {
  cap: number
  used: number
}

/**
 * Counts the queries, refuses when their documents would pass the cap, then reads exactly what was counted: one snapshot,
 * so the count is what the reads return. Completeness comes from what was examined, never from a hope.
 */
async function readCounted(r: Reader, queries: readonly Query[], budget: Budget, stats: ReportReadStats, fields?: readonly string[]): Promise<QueryDocumentSnapshot[][]> {
  const counts = await Promise.all(queries.map(async (q) => (await r.aggregate(q.count())).data().count))
  stats.aggregates += queries.length
  const total = counts.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0)
  if (budget.used + total > budget.cap) throw new ReportError('too_many_operations', { cap: budget.cap })
  budget.used += total
  return Promise.all(queries.map(async (q, i) => (counts[i] === 0 ? [] : (await r.query((fields ? q.select(...fields) : q).limit(counts[i]))).docs)))
}

function parseOps(docs: readonly QueryDocumentSnapshot[], unreadable: Set<string>): LedgerTransaction[] {
  const out: LedgerTransaction[] = []
  for (const d of docs) {
    const tx = parseTransaction(d.id, d.data())
    if (tx) out.push(tx)
    else unreadable.add(d.id)
  }
  return out
}

async function readRollups(r: Reader, db: Firestore, orgId: string, accountIds: readonly string[], range: DayRange, kind: ReportKind, budget: Budget, stats: ReportReadStats): Promise<RollupDocs> {
  const before = budget.used
  const queries = accountIds.flatMap((id) => [cellsQuery(db, orgId, id, range.from, range.to, false), cellsQuery(db, orgId, id, range.from, range.to, true)])
  const docs: RollupDocs = new Map()
  for (const snaps of await readCounted(r, queries, budget, stats)) for (const d of snaps) docs.set(d.id, flatten(d.data()))
  const ids = monthsBetween(range.from, range.to).flatMap((month) => {
    const id = kind === 'cashflow' ? cashCatDocId(month) : plDocId(month)
    return [id, excludedDocId(id)]
  })
  if (budget.used + ids.length > budget.cap) throw new ReportError('too_many_operations', { cap: budget.cap })
  budget.used += ids.length
  for (const s of await r.getAll(ids.map((id) => rollupsCol(db, orgId).doc(id)))) if (s.exists) docs.set(s.id, flatten(s.data()))
  stats.rollups += budget.used - before
  return docs
}

interface FactsRead {
  context: ReportContext
  /** The chosen accounts (every account without an account filter). */
  accounts: LedgerAccount[]
  includeExcluded: boolean
  /** Every operation of the chosen accounts in the period. */
  scope: ReportFacts
  /** The operations the report chose. */
  report: ReportFacts
  days: { openingDay: string; closingDay: string }
  balances: Map<string, Map<string, number>>
  coverage: Coverage
  stats: ReportReadStats
}

async function readFacts(deps: LedgerDeps, orgId: string, q: ReportQuery, kind: ReportKind, opts: ReadReportOptions): Promise<FactsRead> {
  const { db } = deps
  const today = deps.today()
  const days = reconciliationDays(q.from, q.to, today)
  const source = opts.source ?? reportSource(q)
  const includeExcluded = includesExcluded(q.filters)
  const range: DayRange = { from: q.from, to: q.to }
  return readSnapshot(db, async (r) => {
    const stats: ReportReadStats = { operations: 0, rollups: 0, aggregates: 0 }
    const budget: Budget = { cap: opts.cap ?? REPORT_SCAN_CAP, used: 0 }
    const context = await readContext(r, db, orgId)
    const chosen = new Set(q.filters.accountIds)
    const accounts = chosen.size === 0 ? context.accounts : context.accounts.filter((a) => chosen.has(a.id))
    const scopeIds = new Set(accounts.map((a) => a.id))
    const base = context.baseCurrency
    let scope: ReportFacts
    let report: ReportFacts
    let uncountable: number | null = null

    if (source === 'rollups') {
      const docs = await readRollups(r, db, orgId, [...scopeIds], range, kind, budget, stats)
      scope = factsFromRollupDocs(selectRollupDocs(docs, scopeIds, range, kind))
      report = scope
    } else {
      const unreadable = new Set<string>()
      const before = budget.used
      // A transfer's money arrives within MAX_ARRIVAL_DAYS of its payment day: those payments are read too, their days kept.
      const [paidDocs] = await readCounted(r, [paymentsQuery(db, orgId, addDays(q.from, -MAX_ARRIVAL_DAYS), addDays(q.to, MAX_ARRIVAL_DAYS))], budget, stats, REPORT_FIELDS)
      const paid = parseOps(paidDocs, unreadable)
      const inScope = chosen.size === 0 ? paid : paid.filter((tx) => matchesSharedChoices(tx, { accountIds: q.filters.accountIds, counterpartyIds: [], tagIds: [] }))
      const scoped = operationRollups(inScope, base, range, { accounts: scopeIds, parts: ['cash'] })
      const reported = operationRollups(filterForAnalytics(paid, q.filters, context.categoryTypes).operations, base, range, {
        accounts: scopeIds,
        parts: kind === 'cashflow' ? ['cash', 'cashcat'] : ['cash'],
      })
      for (const id of scoped.uncountable) unreadable.add(id)
      if (kind === 'pl') {
        const [byDay, byPeriod] = await readCounted(r, [accrualDaysQuery(db, orgId, q.from, q.to), accrualPeriodsQuery(db, orgId, q.from)], budget, stats, REPORT_FIELDS)
        const accrued = new Map<string, LedgerTransaction>()
        for (const tx of parseOps(byDay, unreadable)) accrued.set(tx.id, tx)
        for (const tx of parseOps(byPeriod, unreadable)) if (tx.accrual.mode === 'period' && tx.accrual.from <= q.to) accrued.set(tx.id, tx)
        const recognised = operationRollups(filterForAnalytics([...accrued.values()], q.filters, context.categoryTypes).operations, base, range, { parts: ['pl'] })
        for (const id of recognised.uncountable) unreadable.add(id)
        addInto(reported.docs, recognised.docs)
      }
      stats.operations += budget.used - before
      scope = factsFromRollupDocs(scoped.docs)
      report = factsFromRollupDocs(reported.docs)
      uncountable = unreadable.size
    }

    const balanceStats: BalanceReadStats = { docsRead: 0, aggregates: 0 }
    const balances = await balancesAt(r, deps, orgId, accounts, [days.openingDay, days.closingDay], today, balanceStats)
    stats.aggregates += balanceStats.aggregates
    stats.operations += balanceStats.docsRead
    const coverage: Coverage = { source, operationsRead: stats.operations, rollupsRead: stats.rollups, uncountable }
    return { context, accounts, includeExcluded, scope, report, days, balances, coverage, stats }
  })
}

async function reconciliationOf(deps: LedgerDeps, read: FactsRead): Promise<Reconciliation> {
  const base = read.context.baseCurrency
  const accounts: ReconcileAccount[] = read.accounts.map((a) => ({
    accountId: a.id,
    currency: a.currency,
    openingMinor: read.balances.get(a.id)?.get(read.days.openingDay) ?? a.balanceMinor,
    closingMinor: read.balances.get(a.id)?.get(read.days.closingDay) ?? a.balanceMinor,
  }))
  // The rates come after the snapshot: they are not part of the equation's data and may wait for NBP.
  const rates = await quoteRates(deps.resolveRate, base, ratesForReconciliation(accounts, read.scope, read.days, base))
  return reconcile({ baseCurrency: base, days: read.days, accounts, scope: read.scope, report: read.report, includeExcluded: read.includeExcluded, rates })
}

export async function readCashFlowReport(deps: LedgerDeps, orgId: string, q: ReportQuery, opts: ReadReportOptions = {}): Promise<ReportRead<CashFlowReport>> {
  const read = await readFacts(deps, orgId, q, 'cashflow', opts)
  const core = buildCashFlowCore(read.report, {
    from: q.from,
    to: q.to,
    accountIds: read.accounts.map((a) => a.id),
    includeExcluded: read.includeExcluded,
    categoryTypes: read.context.categoryTypes,
  })
  const reconciliation = await reconciliationOf(deps, read)
  return {
    report: { kind: 'cashflow', from: q.from, to: q.to, currency: read.context.baseCurrency, ...core, reconciliation, coverage: read.coverage },
    stats: read.stats,
    context: read.context,
  }
}

export async function readPLReport(deps: LedgerDeps, orgId: string, q: ReportQuery, opts: ReadReportOptions = {}): Promise<ReportRead<PLReport>> {
  const read = await readFacts(deps, orgId, q, 'pl', opts)
  const core = buildPLCore(read.report, { from: q.from, to: q.to, includeExcluded: read.includeExcluded, categoryTypes: read.context.categoryTypes })
  const reconciliation = await reconciliationOf(deps, read)
  return {
    report: { kind: 'pl', from: q.from, to: q.to, currency: read.context.baseCurrency, ...core, reconciliation, coverage: read.coverage },
    stats: read.stats,
    context: read.context,
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `balances.ts`**

Створити `lib/server/reports/balances.ts`:

```ts
// lib/server/reports/balances.ts
// The balances on a day and the dashboard «Гроші зараз» (S12): the account documents and the shared balance at a day in one
// read-only snapshot, valued at NBP rates afterwards. Never cached longer than the request.
import { balanceRates, balancesReport } from '@/lib/domain/reports/balances'
import type { BalancesReport, BankSync, DashboardReport } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import { bankAccountsCol } from '@/lib/server/banking/paths'
import { parseBankAccount } from '@/lib/server/banking/types'
import { balancesAt, type BalanceReadStats } from '@/lib/server/ledger/balance-at'
import type { LedgerDeps } from '@/lib/server/ledger/deps'
import { readSnapshot } from '@/lib/server/ledger/reader'
import { quoteRates } from './rates'
import { readContext, type ReportReadStats } from './read'

/** Every account at the close of `date` (a later day is today). */
export async function readBalances(deps: LedgerDeps, orgId: string, date: string): Promise<{ report: BalancesReport; stats: ReportReadStats }> {
  const today = deps.today()
  const day = date < today ? date : today
  const read = await readSnapshot(deps.db, async (r) => {
    const context = await readContext(r, deps.db, orgId, false)
    const balanceStats: BalanceReadStats = { docsRead: 0, aggregates: 0 }
    const balances = await balancesAt(r, deps, orgId, context.accounts, [day], today, balanceStats)
    const stats: ReportReadStats = { operations: balanceStats.docsRead, rollups: 0, aggregates: balanceStats.aggregates }
    return { context, balances, stats }
  })
  const base = read.context.baseCurrency
  const rates = await quoteRates(deps.resolveRate, base, balanceRates(read.context.accounts, day, base))
  const atDay = new Map(read.context.accounts.map((a) => [a.id, read.balances.get(a.id)?.get(day) ?? a.balanceMinor]))
  return { report: balancesReport({ date: day, baseCurrency: base, accounts: read.context.accounts, balances: atDay, rates }), stats: read.stats }
}

/** The last bank sync of each FinFlow account behind an active bank link (several links: the latest time). */
export async function readBankSync(deps: Pick<LedgerDeps, 'db'>, orgId: string): Promise<BankSync[]> {
  const latest = new Map<string, string | null>()
  for (const d of (await bankAccountsCol(deps.db, orgId).get()).docs) {
    const link = parseBankAccount(d.data())
    if (!link || link.status !== 'active') continue
    const prior = latest.get(link.accountId)
    if (prior === undefined || (link.lastSyncAt !== null && (prior === null || link.lastSyncAt > prior))) latest.set(link.accountId, link.lastSyncAt)
  }
  return [...latest].map(([accountId, lastSyncAt]) => ({ accountId, lastSyncAt })).sort((a, b) => (a.accountId < b.accountId ? -1 : a.accountId > b.accountId ? 1 : 0))
}

export async function readDashboard(deps: LedgerDeps, orgId: string): Promise<{ report: DashboardReport; stats: ReportReadStats }> {
  const [balances, bank] = await Promise.all([readBalances(deps, orgId, deps.today()), readBankSync(deps, orgId)])
  return { report: { ...balances.report, bank }, stats: balances.stats }
}
```

```js
}
function merge(target, source) {
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(source)) {
    if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value) && target[key] !== null && typeof target[key] === 'object') merge(target[key], value)
    else target[key] = value
  }
}
for (const [lang, fragment] of Object.entries(ADD)) {
  const file = `messages/${lang}.json`
  const src = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8')
  const data = JSON.parse(src)
  merge(data, fragment)
  let out = JSON.stringify(data, null, 2) + '\n'
  if (src.includes('\r\n')) out = out.replace(/\n/g, '\r\n')
  fs.writeFileSync(file, out)
}
```

```ts
import { categoryTypesOf } from '@/lib/domain/analytics-filter/kinds'
import { reportFile } from '@/lib/server/reports/file'
import type { ReportContext } from '@/lib/server/reports/read'

  categories,

    expect(csv).toContain('\r\nBez kategorii;Wydatki;160,50\r\n')

    expect(strings).toContain('No category')
  })
})
```

`lib/i18n/format.ts`: у кінець файлу додати:

```ts

/** The decimal sign of amounts in a file (CSV): a comma in Polish and Ukrainian, a point in English. */
export function decimalSignOf(locale: Locale): ',' | '.' {
  return locale === 'en' ? '.' : ','
}
```

`lib/server/session-cookie.ts`: функцію `readSessionCookie` (від коментаря `/** Extracts the \`__session\` value from a request's Cookie header, or null. */` до її кінця) замінити на:

```ts
/** The value of a cookie in a request's Cookie header, or null when it is missing or empty. */
export function readCookie(headers: Headers, name: string): string | null {
  const header = headers.get('cookie')
  if (!header) return null
  for (const part of header.split(';')) {
    const eq = part.indexOf('=')
    if (eq === -1) continue
    if (part.slice(0, eq).trim() !== name) continue
    const value = part.slice(eq + 1).trim()
    return value.length > 0 ? value : null
  }
  return null
}

/** Extracts the `__session` value from a request's Cookie header, or null. */
export function readSessionCookie(headers: Headers): string | null {
  return readCookie(headers, SESSION_COOKIE_NAME)
}
```

`lib/utils/analytics.ts`: рядок `function formatDayLabel(date: Date, locale: Locale): string {` замінити на `export function formatDayLabel(date: Date, locale: Locale): string {`.

Створити `lib/utils/report-view.ts` (Task 11 додає сюди решту подання сторінок):

```ts
// lib/utils/report-view.ts
// The server reports (S12) as the pages and the export show them: labels of the period keys and, from Task 11, the view
// models of the pages. Intl formatting stays in lib/utils and lib/i18n.
import type { Locale } from '@/lib/i18n/config'
import { formatDayLabel, formatMonthLabel } from './analytics'

/** A day (`YYYY-MM-DD`) or a month (`YYYY-MM`) of a report as its label in the UI language («19 жов», «Жов 26»). */
export function periodKeyLabel(key: string, locale: Locale): string {
  const [year, month, day] = key.split('-').map(Number)
  return day === undefined ? formatMonthLabel(year, month - 1, locale) : formatDayLabel(new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day)), locale)
}
```

Створити `lib/i18n/report-labels.ts`:

```ts
// lib/i18n/report-labels.ts
// The words of a report file (S12) in a language, for the server that builds it: the `reports.export` zone of the catalogs
// and the default category names (`localizeCategoryName` outside React). No next-intl: the catalogs are plain JSON.
import en from '@/messages/en.json'
import pl from '@/messages/pl.json'
import uk from '@/messages/uk.json'
import type { ExportLabels } from '@/lib/domain/reports/export'
import type { DefaultCategoryKey } from './category-name'
import type { Locale } from './config'

const CATALOGS = { uk, pl, en } as const

export function reportExportLabels(locale: Locale): ExportLabels {
  return CATALOGS[locale].reports.export
}

/** A translator of the default category names (`defaultCategories.<type>.<slug>`) for `localizeCategoryName`. */
export function defaultCategoryTranslator(locale: Locale): (key: DefaultCategoryKey) => string {
  const names = CATALOGS[locale].defaultCategories
  return (key) => {
    const [, type, slug] = key.split('.')
    const group: Record<string, string> = type === 'income' ? names.income : names.expense
    return group[slug] ?? slug
  }
}
```

Створити `lib/server/reports/xlsx.ts`:

```ts
import 'server-only'
import writeExcelFile, { type Cell } from 'write-excel-file/node'
import type { ExportCell, ExportSheet } from '@/lib/domain/reports/export'

const AMOUNT_FORMAT = '#,##0.00'

function cellOf(cell: ExportCell): Cell {
  // A text stays a text even when it starts with «=»: write-excel-file writes a formula only for `type: 'Formula'`.
  return typeof cell === 'string' ? cell : { value: cell.minor / 100, type: Number, format: AMOUNT_FORMAT }
}

/**
 * The sheets of a report as an `.xlsx` file in memory (S12): the header row bold and frozen, amounts as numbers with two
 * decimals. A report is a few hundred cells, so no stream is needed.
 */
export async function renderXlsx(sheets: readonly ExportSheet[]): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>> {
  const buffer = await writeExcelFile(
    sheets.map((s) => ({
      sheet: s.name,
      stickyRowsCount: 1,
      columns: s.header.map((_, i) => ({ width: i === 0 ? 40 : 18 })),
      data: [s.header.map((h): Cell => ({ value: h, fontWeight: 'bold' })), ...s.rows.map((row) => row.map(cellOf))],
    })),
  ).toBuffer()
  return new Uint8Array(buffer)
}
```

Створити `lib/server/reports/file.ts`:

```ts
import 'server-only'
import { parseNoCategoryId } from '@/lib/domain/analytics-filter/kinds'
import { cashFlowSheets, plSheets, sheetsToCsv, type ExportContext } from '@/lib/domain/reports/export'
import type { AnyReport, ExportFormat } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import { localizeCategoryName } from '@/lib/i18n/category-name'
import type { Locale } from '@/lib/i18n/config'
import { decimalSignOf } from '@/lib/i18n/format'
import { defaultCategoryTranslator, reportExportLabels } from '@/lib/i18n/report-labels'
import { periodKeyLabel } from '@/lib/utils/report-view'
import type { ReportContext } from './read'
import { renderXlsx } from './xlsx'

export interface ReportFile {
  body: string | Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>
  contentType: string
  filename: string
}

/** The export of a report (S12): built from the same response the page shows, in the language of the request. */
export async function reportFile(report: AnyReport, context: ReportContext, format: ExportFormat, locale: Locale): Promise<ReportFile> {
  const labels = reportExportLabels(locale)
  const t = defaultCategoryTranslator(locale)
  const categories = new Map(context.categories.map((c) => [c.id, c]))
  const accounts = new Map(context.accounts.map((a) => [a.id, a.name]))
  const ctx: ExportContext = {
    labels,
    periodLabel: (key) => periodKeyLabel(key, locale),
    rowName: (row) => {
      const category = parseNoCategoryId(row) === undefined ? categories.get(row) : undefined
      return category ? localizeCategoryName(category, t) : labels.rows.noCategory
    },
    accountName: (id) => accounts.get(id) ?? id,
    categories: context.categories,
  }
  const sheets = report.kind === 'cashflow' ? cashFlowSheets(report, ctx) : plSheets(report, ctx)
  const filename = `${report.kind}-${report.from}_${report.to}.${format}`
  if (format === 'csv') return { body: sheetsToCsv(sheets, decimalSignOf(locale)), contentType: 'text/csv; charset=utf-8', filename }
  return { body: await renderXlsx(sheets), contentType: 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet', filename }
}
```

Створити `lib/server/reports/http.ts`:

```ts
import 'server-only'
import { ReportError } from '@/lib/domain/reports/errors'
import { HttpError } from '@/lib/server/http-error'
import { toHttpError } from '@/lib/server/ledger/http'

/** A ReportError becomes an HttpError (400 with `field`, 422 with `cap`); everything else goes to the ledger mapping. */
export function toReportHttpError(e: unknown): unknown {
  if (e instanceof ReportError) {
    if (e.code === 'invalid_input') return new HttpError(400, 'invalid_input', e.field === undefined ? undefined : { field: e.field })
    return new HttpError(422, 'too_many_operations', { cap: e.cap })
  }
  return toHttpError(e)
}
```

Створити `lib/server/reports/index.ts`:

```ts
import 'server-only'
import type { ExportFormat, ReportKind, ReportQuery } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import type { Locale } from '@/lib/i18n/config'
import { ledgerDeps } from '@/lib/server/ledger'
import { readBalances, readDashboard } from './balances'
import { reportFile } from './file'
import { readCashFlowReport, readPLReport } from './read'

/** The reports (S12) bound to the production Firestore, NBP rates and the Warsaw clock. They only read. */
export const reports = {
  cashflow: async (orgId: string, query: ReportQuery) => (await readCashFlowReport(ledgerDeps(), orgId, query)).report,
  pl: async (orgId: string, query: ReportQuery) => (await readPLReport(ledgerDeps(), orgId, query)).report,
  balances: async (orgId: string, date: string) => (await readBalances(ledgerDeps(), orgId, date)).report,
  dashboard: async (orgId: string) => (await readDashboard(ledgerDeps(), orgId)).report,
  exportFile: async (orgId: string, kind: ReportKind, format: ExportFormat, query: ReportQuery, locale: Locale) => {
    const read = kind === 'cashflow' ? await readCashFlowReport(ledgerDeps(), orgId, query) : await readPLReport(ledgerDeps(), orgId, query)
    return reportFile(read.report, read.context, format, locale)
  },
}
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'

import { ReportError } from '@/lib/domain/reports/errors'

import { GET as cashflowRoute } from '@/app/api/orgs/[orgId]/reports/cashflow/route'
import { GET as dashboardRoute } from '@/app/api/orgs/[orgId]/reports/dashboard/route'
import { GET as exportRoute } from '@/app/api/orgs/[orgId]/reports/export/route'
import { GET as plRoute } from '@/app/api/orgs/[orgId]/reports/pl/route'

vi.mock('@/lib/server/firebase-admin', async () => {

  return { getAdmin: () => admin }
})
vi.mock('@/lib/server/reports', () => ({
  reports: { cashflow: vi.fn(), pl: vi.fn(), balances: vi.fn(), dashboard: vi.fn(), exportFile: vi.fn() },
}))

const base = '/api/orgs/org-acme/reports'
const PERIOD = 'from=2026-01-01&to=2026-12-31'

function req(path: string, token: string | null = 'token-alice', extraCookie = ''): Request {
  const cookie = [token ? `__session=${token}` : '', extraCookie].filter((c) => c !== '').join('; ')
  return new Request(`http://localhost:3000${path}`, { method: 'GET', headers: cookie ? { Cookie: cookie } : {} })
}
const ctx = () => ({ params: Promise.resolve({ orgId: 'org-acme' }) })

type Call = { name: string; mock: keyof typeof reports; call: (token: string | null) => Promise<Response> }
const ROUTES: Call[] = [
  { name: 'GET /reports/cashflow', mock: 'cashflow', call: (t) => cashflowRoute(req(`${base}/cashflow?${PERIOD}`, t), ctx()) },
  { name: 'GET /reports/pl', mock: 'pl', call: (t) => plRoute(req(`${base}/pl?${PERIOD}`, t), ctx()) },
  { name: 'GET /reports/balances', mock: 'balances', call: (t) => balancesRoute(req(`${base}/balances?date=2026-10-31`, t), ctx()) },
  { name: 'GET /reports/dashboard', mock: 'dashboard', call: (t) => dashboardRoute(req(`${base}/dashboard`, t), ctx()) },
  { name: 'GET /reports/export', mock: 'exportFile', call: (t) => exportRoute(req(`${base}/export?report=cashflow&format=csv&${PERIOD}`, t), ctx()) },
]

beforeEach(() => {
  resetFakeAdmin()
  vi.spyOn(console, 'error').mockImplementation(() => {})
  for (const fn of Object.values(reports)) vi.mocked(fn).mockReset()

  vi.mocked(reports.pl).mockResolvedValue({ kind: 'pl' } as never)

  vi.mocked(reports.dashboard).mockResolvedValue({ date: '2026-10-27' } as never)
  vi.mocked(reports.exportFile).mockResolvedValue({ body: '﻿Report\r\n', contentType: 'text/csv; charset=utf-8', filename: 'cashflow-2026-01-01_2026-12-31.csv' })
})

describe('authorization of every report route', () => {
  describe.each(ROUTES)('$name', ({ mock, call }) => {

      expect((await call(null)).status).toBe(401)
      expect(reports[mock]).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    })

    it('answers 403 to a user who is not a member', async () => {
      expect((await call('token-mallory')).status).toBe(403)
      expect(reports[mock]).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    })

    it('lets a viewer read, never cached', async () => {

      expect(res.status).toBe(200)
      expect(res.headers.get('Cache-Control')).toBe('no-store')

})

describe('parameters', () => {
  it('Cash Flow passes the parsed period and filters on', async () => {
    expect((await cashflowRoute(req(`${base}/cashflow?${PERIOD}&tg=vat&accounts=acc1`), ctx())).status).toBe(200)
    expect(reports.cashflow).toHaveBeenCalledWith('org-acme', { from: '2026-01-01', to: '2026-12-31', filters: { ...EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS, tagIds: ['vat'], accountIds: ['acc1'] } })
  })

  it('a bad parameter is 400 with its field, before any read', async () => {
    const cases: Array<[() => Promise<Response>, string]> = [
      [() => cashflowRoute(req(`${base}/cashflow?from=2026-01-01`), ctx()), 'to'],
      [() => plRoute(req(`${base}/pl?${PERIOD}&tg=a1,a1`), ctx()), 'tg'],
      [() => balancesRoute(req(`${base}/balances?date=2026-02-30`), ctx()), 'date'],

})
```

```ts
import { parseReportQuery } from '@/lib/domain/reports/query'
import { requireOrgRole, requireUser } from '@/lib/server/auth'
import { errorResponse } from '@/lib/server/http-error'
import { reports } from '@/lib/server/reports'
import { toReportHttpError } from '@/lib/server/reports/http'

export const runtime = 'nodejs'
// A filtered report counts, then reads up to REPORT_SCAN_CAP operations: seconds, inside one minute.
export const maxDuration = 60

type Ctx = { params: Promise<{ orgId: string }> }

// GET /api/orgs/{orgId}/reports/cashflow?from=&to=&types=&cat=&cp=&tg=&accounts=  (owner, accountant, viewer) -> CashFlowReport
export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: Ctx) {
  try {
    const { uid } = await requireUser(req)
    const { orgId } = await params
    await requireOrgRole(uid, orgId, ['owner', 'accountant', 'viewer'])
    const query = parseReportQuery(new URL(req.url).searchParams)
    return Response.json(await reports.cashflow(orgId, query), { headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } })
  } catch (e) {
    return errorResponse(toReportHttpError(e))
  }
}
```

```ts
import { assertNoParams } from '@/lib/domain/reports/query'
```

Створити `app/api/orgs/[orgId]/reports/export/route.ts`:

```ts
import { parseExportRequest } from '@/lib/domain/reports/query'
import { LOCALE_COOKIE } from '@/lib/i18n/config'
import { resolveLocale } from '@/lib/i18n/negotiate'
import { requireOrgRole, requireUser } from '@/lib/server/auth'
import { errorResponse } from '@/lib/server/http-error'
import { reports } from '@/lib/server/reports'
import { toReportHttpError } from '@/lib/server/reports/http'
import { readCookie } from '@/lib/server/session-cookie'

export const runtime = 'nodejs'
// The file is built from the same report: the same reads, then a few hundred cells.
export const maxDuration = 60

type Ctx = { params: Promise<{ orgId: string }> }

// GET /api/orgs/{orgId}/reports/export?report=cashflow|pl&format=csv|xlsx&from=&to=&<filters>  (owner, accountant, viewer) -> the file
// The words follow the language of the app (the NEXT_LOCALE cookie, else Accept-Language, else Polish), as the pages do.
export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: Ctx) {
  try {
    const { uid } = await requireUser(req)
    const { orgId } = await params
    await requireOrgRole(uid, orgId, ['owner', 'accountant', 'viewer'])
    const { kind, format, query } = parseExportRequest(new URL(req.url).searchParams)
    const locale = resolveLocale({ cookie: readCookie(req.headers, LOCALE_COOKIE), acceptLanguage: req.headers.get('accept-language') })
    const file = await reports.exportFile(orgId, kind, format, query, locale)
    return new Response(file.body, {
      headers: { 'Content-Type': file.contentType, 'Content-Disposition': `attachment; filename="${file.filename}"`, 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' },
    })
  } catch (e) {
    return errorResponse(toReportHttpError(e))
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the pure part and the script**

Створити `scripts/lib/check-reports.ts`:

```ts
// Pure part of scripts/check-reports.ts (S12 rollout check). Unit-tested in Vitest.

/** What the check read for one company this month: counts and flags only. */
export interface ReportsCheck {
  accounts: number
  /** Rollup documents this month's Cash Flow and P&L read. */
  rollupDocs: number
  /** Operation documents the same two reports read from the operations; null when the month passed the read limit. */
  operationDocs: number | null
  aggregates: number
  /** The rollups and the operations gave the same totals and P&L rows; null when not compared (over the limit). */
  same: boolean | null
  /** The reconciliation of the month added up; null when an NBP rate was missing. */
  reconciled: boolean | null
}

export interface ReportsSummary {
  companies: number
  rollupDocs: number
  operationDocs: number
  aggregates: number
  overLimit: number
  different: number
  unreconciled: number
  unvalued: number
}

export function reportsCheckLine(orgId: string, c: ReportsCheck): string {
  const operations = c.operationDocs === null ? 'over the read limit' : `${c.operationDocs} operation document(s)`
  const same = c.same === null ? 'not compared' : c.same ? 'yes' : 'NO'
  const reconciled = c.reconciled === null ? 'no NBP rate' : c.reconciled ? 'yes' : 'NO'
  return `org ${orgId}: ${c.accounts} account(s), ${c.rollupDocs} rollup document(s), ${operations}, ${c.aggregates} aggregate(s), rollups = operations: ${same}, reconciled: ${reconciled}`
}

export function sumReportsChecks(checks: readonly ReportsCheck[]): ReportsSummary {
  return checks.reduce<ReportsSummary>(
    (s, c) => ({
      companies: s.companies + 1,
      rollupDocs: s.rollupDocs + c.rollupDocs,
      operationDocs: s.operationDocs + (c.operationDocs ?? 0),
      aggregates: s.aggregates + c.aggregates,
      overLimit: s.overLimit + (c.operationDocs === null ? 1 : 0),
      different: s.different + (c.same === false ? 1 : 0),
      unreconciled: s.unreconciled + (c.reconciled === false ? 1 : 0),
      unvalued: s.unvalued + (c.reconciled === null ? 1 : 0),
    }),
    { companies: 0, rollupDocs: 0, operationDocs: 0, aggregates: 0, overLimit: 0, different: 0, unreconciled: 0, unvalued: 0 },
  )
}

export function reportsSummaryLine(s: ReportsSummary): string {
  return `ALL: ${s.companies} company(ies), ${s.rollupDocs} rollup document(s), ${s.operationDocs} operation document(s), ${s.aggregates} aggregate(s); over the read limit: ${s.overLimit}, rollups != operations: ${s.different}, not reconciled: ${s.unreconciled}, no NBP rate: ${s.unvalued}`
}
```

Створити `scripts/check-reports.ts`:

```ts
// Read-only check for the S12 rollout (server reports). Per company it builds this month's Cash Flow and P&L from the
// rollups and from the operations, the balances of yesterday and the dashboard, the way the routes do, and runs once each
// query shape S12 adds (operations by accrual day and by accrual period, the count of the payments), so a missing index
// shows up as FAILED_PRECONDITION with its creation link. Rates come through a read-only view of the NBP cache: nothing is
// written, not even fxTables.
//   npm run check:reports                                   over the emulator when FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST is set
//   npm run check:reports -- --project=finflow-e94b5        production (the flag is required; nothing is read without it)
//   --org=<id> limits the run to one company.
// Prints counts only, never names or amounts. Exit code: 0 everything read, 1 a read failed, 2 refused or bad arguments.
import fs from 'node:fs'
import path from 'node:path'
import { applicationDefault, cert, initializeApp } from 'firebase-admin/app'
import { getFirestore, type Firestore } from 'firebase-admin/firestore'
import { EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS } from '@/lib/domain/analytics-filter/filter'
import { addDays, monthBounds, monthOf, toDay } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/days'
import { ReportError } from '@/lib/domain/reports/errors'
import type { ReportQuery } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import { fetchNbpTables } from '@/lib/server/fx/nbp'
import { createResolver } from '@/lib/server/fx/resolver'
import { firestoreFxStore } from '@/lib/server/fx/store'
import type { LedgerDeps } from '@/lib/server/ledger/deps'
import { readBalances, readDashboard } from '@/lib/server/reports/balances'
import { accrualDaysQuery, accrualPeriodsQuery, paymentsQuery, readCashFlowReport, readPLReport } from '@/lib/server/reports/read'
import { todayUtcDay } from '@/lib/utils/today'
import { assertReadinessTarget, parseCheckJournalArgs } from './lib/check-journal-args'
import { reportsCheckLine, reportsSummaryLine, sumReportsChecks, type ReportsCheck } from './lib/check-reports'
import { readOnlyFxStore } from './lib/readonly-fx-store'

const PROJECT_ID = 'finflow-e94b5'
const EMULATOR_PROJECT_ID = 'demo-finflow'

function fail(message: string, code: number): never {
  console.error(message)
  process.exit(code)
}

function credential() {
  if (process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS) return applicationDefault()
  const dir = path.resolve('.secrets')
  const file = fs.existsSync(dir) ? fs.readdirSync(dir).find((f) => /^finflow-.*-firebase-adminsdk-.*\.json$/.test(f)) : undefined
  if (!file) fail('No credentials: set GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS or put the service-account JSON in .secrets/.', 2)
  return cert(JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, file), 'utf8')))
}

function describeError(e: unknown): string {
  if (e instanceof Error) {
    const code = (e as { code?: unknown }).code
    return `${code !== undefined ? `code ${String(code)}` : 'error'}: ${e.message}`
  }
  return 'error'
}

/** A report over the read limit is an answer here (counted), not a failure. */
async function unlessOverLimit<T>(read: Promise<T>): Promise<T | null> {
  try {
    return await read
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof ReportError && e.code === 'too_many_operations') return null
    throw e
  }
}

async function checkOrg(deps: LedgerDeps, orgId: string): Promise<ReportsCheck> {
  const today = deps.today()
  const month: ReportQuery = { from: `${monthOf(today)}-01`, to: monthBounds(monthOf(today)).last, filters: EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS }
  const cashFlow = await readCashFlowReport(deps, orgId, month)
  const pl = await readPLReport(deps, orgId, month)
  const cashFlowOps = await unlessOverLimit(readCashFlowReport(deps, orgId, month, { source: 'operations' }))
  const plOps = await unlessOverLimit(readPLReport(deps, orgId, month, { source: 'operations' }))
  const balances = await readBalances(deps, orgId, addDays(today, -1))
  await readDashboard(deps, orgId)
  // The shapes the reads above may not reach, each once; an empty answer is fine.
  await accrualDaysQuery(deps.db, orgId, month.from, month.to).select().limit(1).get()
  await accrualPeriodsQuery(deps.db, orgId, month.from).select().limit(1).get()
  await paymentsQuery(deps.db, orgId, month.from, month.to).count().get()
  const compared = cashFlowOps !== null && plOps !== null
  const same = compared
    ? JSON.stringify([cashFlow.report.totals, pl.report.totals, pl.report.rows]) === JSON.stringify([cashFlowOps.report.totals, plOps.report.totals, plOps.report.rows])
    : null
  const discrepancy = cashFlow.report.reconciliation.discrepancyBase
  return {
    accounts: balances.report.accounts.length,
    rollupDocs: cashFlow.stats.rollups + pl.stats.rollups,
    operationDocs: compared ? cashFlowOps.stats.operations + plOps.stats.operations : null,
    aggregates: cashFlow.stats.aggregates + pl.stats.aggregates + (cashFlowOps?.stats.aggregates ?? 0) + (plOps?.stats.aggregates ?? 0) + balances.stats.aggregates,
    same,
    reconciled: discrepancy === null ? null : discrepancy === 0,
  }
}

async function main(): Promise<number> {
  const parsed = parseCheckJournalArgs(process.argv.slice(2))
  if (!parsed.ok) fail(parsed.message, 2)
  const { org, raw } = parsed.args

  const target = assertReadinessTarget({ emulator: !!process.env.FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST, args: raw })
  if (!target.ok) fail(target.message, 2)

  if (process.env.FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST) {
    initializeApp({ projectId: EMULATOR_PROJECT_ID })
    console.log(`target: Firestore EMULATOR (project ${EMULATOR_PROJECT_ID})`)
  } else {
    initializeApp({ credential: credential(), projectId: PROJECT_ID })
    console.log(`target: PRODUCTION (project ${PROJECT_ID}), read-only`)
  }
  const db: Firestore = getFirestore()
  const today = toDay(todayUtcDay())
  const resolveRate = createResolver({
    store: readOnlyFxStore(firestoreFxStore(db)),
    fetchTables: (first, last) => fetchNbpTables(first, last),
    now: () => Date.now(),
    today: () => today,
  })
  const deps: LedgerDeps = { db, resolveRate, now: () => new Date(), today: () => today }

  const orgIds = org ? [org] : (await db.collection('organizations').select().get()).docs.map((d) => d.id)
  console.log(`companies: ${orgIds.length}`)

  const checks: ReportsCheck[] = []
  const errored: string[] = []
  for (const orgId of orgIds) {
    try {
      const c = await checkOrg(deps, orgId)
      checks.push(c)
      console.log(reportsCheckLine(orgId, c))
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(`org ${orgId}: FAILED (${describeError(e)})`)
      errored.push(orgId)
    }
  }
  const summary = sumReportsChecks(checks)
  console.log(reportsSummaryLine(summary))
  if (summary.different > 0 || summary.unreconciled > 0) console.log('WARN: run verifyLedger (POST /api/orgs/{org}/ledger/rebuild?apply=0) for the companies marked NO.')
  if (errored.length > 0) console.error(`failed companies (${errored.length}): ${errored.join(', ')}`)
  else console.log('DONE: every query ran. Nothing was written.')
  return errored.length > 0 ? 1 : 0
}

main().then(
  (code) => process.exit(code),
  (e) => {
    console.error(`FAILED (${describeError(e)})`)
    process.exit(1)
  },
)
```

`package.json`: у `scripts` після рядка `    "check:recurring": "node --conditions=react-server --import tsx scripts/check-recurring.ts",` додати рядок `    "check:reports": "node --conditions=react-server --import tsx scripts/check-reports.ts",`.

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { LedgerApiError } from '@/lib/api/ledger-client'
import { downloadReport, fetchBalances, fetchCashFlow, fetchDashboard, fetchPL, viewOfFailure } from '@/lib/api/reports-client'
import { EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS } from '@/lib/domain/analytics-filter/filter'
import type { ReportQuery } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import { downloadBlob } from '@/lib/utils/download'

vi.mock('@/lib/utils/download', () => ({ downloadBlob: vi.fn() }))

const fetchMock = vi.fn<(url: string, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>>()
const json = (body: unknown, status = 200): Response => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })
const Q: ReportQuery = { from: '2026-01-01', to: '2026-12-31', filters: { ...EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS, tagIds: ['vat'] } }

beforeEach(() => {
  fetchMock.mockReset()
  vi.mocked(downloadBlob).mockReset()
  vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock)
})
afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllGlobals()
})

describe('reports client', () => {
  it('asks the report routes with the period and the filters, same-origin', async () => {
    fetchMock.mockResolvedValue(json({ kind: 'cashflow' }))
    await fetchCashFlow('org 1', Q)
    expect(fetchMock.mock.calls[0][0]).toBe('/api/orgs/org%201/reports/cashflow?from=2026-01-01&to=2026-12-31&tg=vat')
    expect(fetchMock.mock.calls[0][1]).toMatchObject({ credentials: 'same-origin' })
    fetchMock.mockResolvedValue(json({ kind: 'pl' }))
    await fetchPL('org1', { ...Q, filters: EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS })
    expect(fetchMock.mock.calls[1][0]).toBe('/api/orgs/org1/reports/pl?from=2026-01-01&to=2026-12-31')
    fetchMock.mockResolvedValue(json({ date: '2026-10-31' }))
    await fetchBalances('org1', '2026-10-31')
    expect(fetchMock.mock.calls[2][0]).toBe('/api/orgs/org1/reports/balances?date=2026-10-31')
    fetchMock.mockResolvedValue(json({ date: '2026-10-27' }))
    await fetchDashboard('org1')
    expect(fetchMock.mock.calls[3][0]).toBe('/api/orgs/org1/reports/dashboard')
  })

  it('a refusal is a LedgerApiError with its code and details; the read limit becomes its own view', async () => {
    fetchMock.mockResolvedValue(json({ error: 'too_many_operations', cap: 10_000 }, 422))
    const e = await fetchCashFlow('org1', Q).catch((x: unknown) => x)
    expect(e).toBeInstanceOf(LedgerApiError)
    expect(e).toMatchObject({ status: 422, code: 'too_many_operations', details: { cap: 10_000 } })
    expect(viewOfFailure(e)).toEqual({ status: 'too_large', cap: 10_000 })
    expect(viewOfFailure(new LedgerApiError(500, 'internal'))).toEqual({ status: 'error' })
    expect(viewOfFailure(new TypeError('offline'))).toEqual({ status: 'error' })
  })

  it('the export asks the server for the file and saves it under the name the page gives', async () => {
    fetchMock.mockResolvedValue(new Response('file', { status: 200 }))
    await downloadReport('org1', 'pl', 'xlsx', Q, 'pl-current-year.xlsx')
    expect(fetchMock.mock.calls[0][0]).toBe('/api/orgs/org1/reports/export?report=pl&format=xlsx&from=2026-01-01&to=2026-12-31&tg=vat')
    expect(vi.mocked(downloadBlob).mock.calls[0][0]).toBe('pl-current-year.xlsx')
    fetchMock.mockResolvedValue(json({ error: 'too_many_operations', cap: 10_000 }, 422))
    await expect(downloadReport('org1', 'pl', 'csv', Q, 'x.csv')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(LedgerApiError)
    expect(downloadBlob).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `downloadBlob` and the client**

`lib/utils/download.ts`: функцію `downloadText` (від коментаря `/** Hands a text file to the browser's download. */` до кінця файлу) замінити на:

```ts
/** Hands a file to the browser's download. */
export function downloadBlob(filename: string, blob: Blob): void {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
  const link = document.createElement('a')
  link.href = url
  link.download = filename
  document.body.appendChild(link)
  link.click()
  document.body.removeChild(link)
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
}

/** Hands a text file to the browser's download. */
export function downloadText(filename: string, content: string, mime = 'text/csv;charset=utf-8'): void {
  downloadBlob(filename, new Blob([content], { type: mime }))
}
```

Створити `lib/api/reports-client.ts`:

```ts
// lib/api/reports-client.ts
// The browser side of the server reports (S12): same-origin GETs that authenticate with the session cookie, and the export,
// which the server builds and the browser only names and saves.
import { reportQueryToParams } from '@/lib/domain/reports/query'
import type { BalancesReport, CashFlowReport, DashboardReport, ExportFormat, PLReport, ReportKind, ReportQuery } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import { downloadBlob } from '@/lib/utils/download'
import { LedgerApiError, readApiError } from './ledger-client'

/** What a page shows of one server read. */
export type ServerView<T> = { status: 'loading' } | { status: 'ready'; data: T } | { status: 'too_large'; cap: number } | { status: 'error' }

const reportsPath = (orgId: string): string => `/api/orgs/${encodeURIComponent(orgId)}/reports`

async function getJson<T>(url: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<T> {
  const res = await fetch(url, { credentials: 'same-origin', signal })
  if (!res.ok) throw await readApiError(res)
  return (await res.json()) as T
}

export function fetchCashFlow(orgId: string, query: ReportQuery, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<CashFlowReport> {
  return getJson(`${reportsPath(orgId)}/cashflow?${reportQueryToParams(query)}`, signal)
}

export function fetchPL(orgId: string, query: ReportQuery, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<PLReport> {
  return getJson(`${reportsPath(orgId)}/pl?${reportQueryToParams(query)}`, signal)
}

export function fetchBalances(orgId: string, date: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<BalancesReport> {
  return getJson(`${reportsPath(orgId)}/balances?${new URLSearchParams({ date })}`, signal)
}

export function fetchDashboard(orgId: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<DashboardReport> {
  return getJson(`${reportsPath(orgId)}/dashboard`, signal)
}

/** The file is built by the server from the same report the page shows; `filename` is the page's (it knows the period's name). */
export async function downloadReport(orgId: string, kind: ReportKind, format: ExportFormat, query: ReportQuery, filename: string): Promise<void> {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ report: kind, format })
  for (const [key, value] of reportQueryToParams(query)) params.set(key, value)
  const res = await fetch(`${reportsPath(orgId)}/export?${params}`, { credentials: 'same-origin' })
  if (!res.ok) throw await readApiError(res)
  downloadBlob(filename, await res.blob())
}

/** A period with more operations than the server reads is a state of its own («звузьте період»); anything else is an error. */
export function viewOfFailure(e: unknown): ServerView<never> {
  if (e instanceof LedgerApiError && e.status === 422 && e.code === 'too_many_operations') {
    const cap = Number(e.details?.cap)
    return { status: 'too_large', cap: Number.isFinite(cap) ? cap : 0 }
  }
  return { status: 'error' }
}
```

Створити `lib/hooks/useReports.ts`:

```ts
'use client'

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { onLedgerChanged } from '@/lib/api/ledger-events'
import { fetchBalances, fetchCashFlow, fetchDashboard, fetchPL, viewOfFailure, type ServerView } from '@/lib/api/reports-client'
import { parseReportQuery, reportQueryToParams } from '@/lib/domain/reports/query'
import type { BalancesReport, CashFlowReport, DashboardReport, PLReport, ReportQuery } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'

/** Several changes in a row (an undo of many) read the report once. */
const REFRESH_DEBOUNCE_MS = 300

export interface ServerRead<T> {
  view: ServerView<T>
  /** Reads again (the retry button); what is shown stays until the new answer arrives. */
  refresh: () => void
}

// A key is the company and the address of the read; the readers are module functions, so an effect depends on the key only.
const keyOf = (orgId: string, rest: string): string => `${encodeURIComponent(orgId)}|${rest}`
function partsOf(key: string): [string, string] {
  const bar = key.indexOf('|')
  return [decodeURIComponent(key.slice(0, bar)), key.slice(bar + 1)]
}
const readCashFlow = (key: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<CashFlowReport> => {
  const [orgId, params] = partsOf(key)
  return fetchCashFlow(orgId, parseReportQuery(new URLSearchParams(params)), signal)
}
const readPL = (key: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<PLReport> => {
  const [orgId, params] = partsOf(key)
  return fetchPL(orgId, parseReportQuery(new URLSearchParams(params)), signal)
}
const readBalancesOf = (key: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<BalancesReport> => {
  const [orgId, date] = partsOf(key)
  return fetchBalances(orgId, date, signal)
}
const readDashboardOf = (key: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<DashboardReport> => fetchDashboard(partsOf(key)[0], signal)

/** A counter that a change in this browser bumps (debounced): every server read below reads again. */
function useLedgerRound(): [number, () => void] {
  const [round, setRound] = useState(0)
  useEffect(() => {
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined
    const off = onLedgerChanged(() => {
      clearTimeout(timer)
      timer = setTimeout(() => setRound((r) => r + 1), REFRESH_DEBOUNCE_MS)
    })
    return () => {
      off()
      clearTimeout(timer)
    }
  }, [])
  const refresh = useCallback(() => setRound((r) => r + 1), [])
  return [round, refresh]
}

function useServerRead<T>(key: string | null, round: number, read: (key: string, signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<T>): ServerView<T> {
  const [state, setState] = useState<{ key: string; view: ServerView<T> } | null>(null)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (key === null) return
    const ac = new AbortController()
    read(key, ac.signal).then(
      (data) => {
        if (!ac.signal.aborted) setState({ key, view: { status: 'ready', data } })
      },
      (e: unknown) => {
        if (!ac.signal.aborted) setState({ key, view: viewOfFailure(e) })
      },
    )
    return () => ac.abort()
  }, [key, round, read])
  return state !== null && state.key === key ? state.view : { status: 'loading' }
}

/** Cash Flow of the server for the query (null: not yet, e.g. while the categories load). */
export function useCashFlowReport(orgId: string, query: ReportQuery | null): ServerRead<CashFlowReport> {
  const [round, refresh] = useLedgerRound()
  const key = query === null ? null : keyOf(orgId, reportQueryToParams(query).toString())
  return { view: useServerRead(key, round, readCashFlow), refresh }
}

export function usePLReport(orgId: string, query: ReportQuery | null): ServerRead<PLReport> {
  const [round, refresh] = useLedgerRound()
  const key = query === null ? null : keyOf(orgId, reportQueryToParams(query).toString())
  return { view: useServerRead(key, round, readPL), refresh }
}

export function useBalances(orgId: string, date: string): ServerRead<BalancesReport> {
  const [round, refresh] = useLedgerRound()
  return { view: useServerRead(keyOf(orgId, date), round, readBalancesOf), refresh }
}

export function useDashboard(orgId: string): ServerRead<DashboardReport> {
  const [round, refresh] = useLedgerRound()
  return { view: useServerRead(keyOf(orgId, ''), round, readDashboardOf), refresh }
}
```

Замінити `lib/utils/report-view.ts` повністю:

```ts
// lib/utils/report-view.ts
// The server reports (S12) as the pages and the export show them: labels of the period keys, the query of the toolbar,
// the rows the charts and tables already draw (major units), the account order of the sidebar and the «Станом на» days.
// Intl formatting stays in lib/utils and lib/i18n.
import type { AnalyticsFilters } from '@/lib/domain/analytics-filter/filter'
import { monthBounds, monthOf, prevMonth } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/days'
import { fromMinor } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/money'
import type { AccountType } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/types'
import { rowRank } from '@/lib/domain/reports/categories'
import type { BalancesReport, CashFlowReport, PLReport, ReportQuery } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import type { Locale } from '@/lib/i18n/config'
import type { Category } from '@/types'
import { formatDayLabel, formatMonthLabel, type CashFlowBucket, type PLRow } from './analytics'
import { dayKeyUtc } from './calendar-grid'
import { resolvePeriod, type PeriodSelection } from './period'

/** A day (`YYYY-MM-DD`) or a month (`YYYY-MM`) of a report as its label in the UI language («19 жов», «Жов 26»). */
export function periodKeyLabel(key: string, locale: Locale): string {
  const [year, month, day] = key.split('-').map(Number)
  return day === undefined ? formatMonthLabel(year, month - 1, locale) : formatDayLabel(new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day)), locale)
}

/** The report query of the toolbar's period (resolved against Warsaw's today) with the filters of the address. */
export function reportQueryOf(period: PeriodSelection, now: Date, filters: AnalyticsFilters): ReportQuery {
  const range = resolvePeriod(period, now)
  if (!range.start || !range.end) throw new RangeError('a report needs a bounded period')
  return { from: dayKeyUtc(range.start), to: dayKeyUtc(range.end), filters }
}

/** The buckets as the chart and the table draw them: major units of the company currency, labelled in the UI language. */
export function cashFlowBuckets(report: CashFlowReport, locale: Locale): CashFlowBucket[] {
  return report.buckets.map((b) => ({
    key: b.key,
    label: periodKeyLabel(b.key, locale),
    income: fromMinor(b.incomeBase),
    expense: fromMinor(b.expenseBase),
    net: fromMinor(b.netBase),
    balance: fromMinor(b.runningNetBase),
  }))
}

/** The P&L rows as the table and the charts draw them, in the order of the company's categories (the shell's list). */
export function plRows(report: PLReport, categories: readonly Category[], nameOf: (category: Category) => string, noCategory: string): { monthKeys: string[]; rows: PLRow[] } {
  const rank = rowRank(categories)
  const byId = new Map(categories.map((c) => [c.id, c]))
  const rows = [...report.rows]
    .sort((a, b) => (a.type === b.type ? rank(a.type, a.row) - rank(b.type, b.row) : a.type === 'income' ? -1 : 1))
    .map((line): PLRow => {
      const category = byId.get(line.row)
      return {
        categoryId: line.row,
        categoryName: category ? nameOf(category) : noCategory,
        type: line.type,
        months: Object.fromEntries(report.months.map((month, i) => [month, fromMinor(line.values[i])])),
        total: fromMinor(line.total),
      }
    })
  return { monthKeys: report.months, rows }
}

/** Lines in the order of the sidebar's accounts; accounts it does not know (yet) come last. */
export function inShellOrder<T extends { accountId: string }>(lines: readonly T[], accounts: ReadonlyArray<{ id: string }>): T[] {
  const index = new Map(accounts.map((a, i) => [a.id, i]))
  return [...lines].sort((a, b) => (index.get(a.accountId) ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER) - (index.get(b.accountId) ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER))
}

/** «Станом на» of the Balance page (label `analytics.balance.asOfOptions.<option>`). */
export const AS_OF_OPTIONS = ['today', 'prevMonthEnd', 'prevQuarterEnd', 'prevYearEnd'] as const
export type AsOfOption = (typeof AS_OF_OPTIONS)[number]

export function asOfDay(option: AsOfOption, today: string): string {
  const month = monthOf(today)
  switch (option) {
    case 'today':
      return today
    case 'prevMonthEnd':
      return monthBounds(prevMonth(month)).last
    case 'prevQuarterEnd': {
      const [year, m] = month.split('-').map(Number)
      const firstOfQuarter = `${year}-${String(Math.floor((m - 1) / 3) * 3 + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`
      return monthBounds(prevMonth(firstOfQuarter)).last
    }
    case 'prevYearEnd':
      return `${Number(today.slice(0, 4)) - 1}-12-31`
  }
}

/** One account of the Balance sheet: its own currency and the company currency (null without an NBP rate). */
export interface BalanceSheetLine {
  id: string
  name: string
  type: AccountType
  color: string
  currency: string
  balance: number
  base: number | null
  balanceMinor: number
  baseMinor: number | null
}

export function balanceSheetLines(report: BalancesReport, accounts: ReadonlyArray<{ id: string }>): BalanceSheetLine[] {
  return inShellOrder(report.accounts, accounts).map((l) => ({
    id: l.accountId,
    name: l.name,
    type: l.type,
    color: l.color,
    currency: l.currency,
    balance: fromMinor(l.balanceMinor),
    base: l.baseMinor === null ? null : fromMinor(l.baseMinor),
    balanceMinor: l.balanceMinor,
    baseMinor: l.baseMinor,
  }))
}
```

Контракти компонентів (тестові id потрібні e2e Task 14 і скриншотам Task 15):
- `ReportNotice({ kind: 'too_large' | 'error', cap?, onRetry? })`: `report-too-large` (`reports.tooLarge`, `cap` числом мови), `report-error` (`reports.error` і кнопка `reports.retry`). `ReportSource({ coverage })`: `report-source` з `data-source` (`rollups`/`operations`), `reports.source.*`; при `uncountable > 0` ще `report-uncountable`.
- `ExcludedLine({ excluded, currency, testId })`: нічого, коли `excluded` null або нулі; інакше `testId` з `-income`/`-expense` («Виключено з аналітики» і підказка).
- `ReconciliationBlock({ reconciliation, currency })`: `section` `reconciliation` з рядками `recon-opening`, `recon-net`, `recon-excluded` (коли `excludedBase !== null`), `recon-outside` (≠ 0), `recon-transfers` (≠ 0), `recon-fx` (коли є рахунок в іншій валюті), `recon-closing`, `recon-discrepancy` (≠ 0); під ними `recon-ok` (розбіжність 0), `recon-included`, `recon-unvalued`, `recon-rate` (по валюті), `recon-pending`.
- `CategoryBreakdown({ lines, currency })`: `cf-category-row` з `data-level`, відступ за рівнем, назви з `useCategoryName`, «Без категорії» з `journal.list.noCategory`.
- `CashFlowAccounts({ report })`: таблиця `cf-account-row` у порядку бічної панелі (`inShellOrder`): рахунок, початковий залишок, надходження, витрати, перекази (різниця), кінцевий залишок, у валюті рахунку; на телефоні таблиця прокручується у власному `overflow-x-auto`, сторінка ні.
- `ReportToolbar`: `onExport(format: 'csv' | 'xlsx')`, дві квадратні кнопки `analytics.exportCsv` і `analytics.exportXlsx` (іконки `Download`, `FileSpreadsheet`).

Наслідок для e2e: `tests/e2e/analytics-filters.spec.ts` і тести періодів у `tests/e2e/app-shell.spec.ts` пишуть операції в обхід журналу (`seedLedgerOperation`, без зведених), тож після цієї задачі їхні цифри Cash Flow без фільтрів нульові, доки Task 14 не переведе засів на API. import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
```ts
import type { AnalyticsSelection } from '@/lib/domain/analytics-filter/filter'
import { buildCalendar, formatDayLabel, formatMonthLabel } from '@/lib/utils/analytics'
import type { Transaction } from '@/types'

}
const excluded = (t: Transaction): Transaction => ({ ...t, tags: ['vat'], excluded: true })

    expect(months('uk')).toEqual(['Січ 26', 'Лют 26', 'Бер 26', 'Кві 26', 'Тра 26', 'Чер 26', 'Лип 26', 'Сер 26', 'Вер 26', 'Жов 26', 'Лис 26', 'Гру 26'])
    expect(months('pl')).toEqual(['Sty 26', 'Lut 26', 'Mar 26', 'Kwi 26', 'Maj 26', 'Cze 26', 'Lip 26', 'Sie 26', 'Wrz 26', 'Paź 26', 'Lis 26', 'Gru 26'])
    expect(months('en')).toEqual(['Jan 2026', 'Feb 2026', 'Mar 2026', 'Apr 2026', 'May 2026', 'Jun 2026', 'Jul 2026', 'Aug 2026', 'Sep 2026', 'Oct 2026', 'Nov 2026', 'Dec 2026'])
  })

  it('a day is its number and the short month, lower case in Ukrainian and Polish', () => {
    const day = new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 19))
    expect([formatDayLabel(day, 'uk'), formatDayLabel(day, 'pl'), formatDayLabel(day, 'en')]).toEqual(['19 жов', '19 paź', '19 Oct'])
  })
})

describe('buildCalendar (the Calendar stays a browser report until S13)', () => {

    const txs = [
      excluded(tx('income', 500, '2026-09-10T12:00:00Z')),

      excluded(tx('income', 230, '2026-10-20T10:00:00Z')),
      excluded(tx('expense', 230, '2026-10-20T11:00:00Z')),

    expect(days.reduce((s, d) => s + d.expense, 0)).toBe(40)
    expect(days[19]).toMatchObject({ date: '2026-10-20', income: 0, expense: 0, hasTransactions: false, balance: 100 })
    expect(days[30].balance).toBe(60)
    const { days: all } = buildCalendar(sel(txs, true), 2026, 9)
    expect(all[19]).toMatchObject({ income: 230, expense: 230, hasTransactions: true, balance: 600 })
    expect(all[30].balance).toBe(560)
  })

  it('an excluded transfer is never counted', () => {

  })

    const txs = [excluded(tx('income', 230, '2026-10-20T10:00:00Z')), tx('expense', 40, '2026-10-21T10:00:00Z')]
    expect(buildCalendar(sel(txs, true), 2026, 9).excludedCounted).toBe(true)
    expect(buildCalendar(sel(txs), 2026, 9).excludedCounted).toBe(false)

  it('says so for the visible month only, or for a balance carried in from an earlier month', () => {

    expect(buildCalendar(sel(txs, true), 2026, 8).excludedCounted).toBe(false)
    expect(buildCalendar(sel(txs, true), 2026, 9).excludedCounted).toBe(true)
    expect(buildCalendar(sel(txs, true), 2026, 10).excludedCounted).toBe(true)
  })
})
```

Замінити `lib/utils/analytics.ts` повністю (функції `shortMonthName`, `formatMonthLabel`, `formatDayLabel`, `buildCalendar` переносяться без змін; `buildCashFlow`, `buildPL`, `CashFlowReport`, `PLReport`, `CashFlowGranularity`, `CASHFLOW_DAY_BUCKETS_MAX_DAYS` і їхні помічники видаляються):

```ts
import type { Transaction } from '@/types'
import { countsInAnalytics, type AnalyticsSelection } from '@/lib/domain/analytics-filter/filter'
import { formatDate } from '@/lib/i18n/format'
import type { Locale } from '@/lib/i18n/config'

// Cash Flow and P&L are server reports since S12 (lib/domain/reports, lib/server/reports); what stays here is the view
// types the charts and tables draw, the labels of their buckets and columns, and the Calendar (a browser report until S13).

/** One Cash Flow bucket as the chart and the table draw it (major units of the company currency; `cashFlowBuckets`). */
export interface CashFlowBucket {
  label: string       // month: "Січ 26"; day: "12 жов" (in the UI language)
  key: string         // month: "2026-01"; day: "2026-01-12" (UTC)
  income: number
  expense: number
  balance: number     // running total of `net` from the start of the period
  net: number         // income - expense
}

/** One P&L row as the table and the charts draw it (major units; `plRows`). */
export interface PLRow {
  categoryId: string
  categoryName: string
  type: 'income' | 'expense'
  months: Record<string, number>  // { "2026-01": 5000, "2026-02": 3200 }
  total: number
}

export interface CalendarReport {
  /** The days of the month shown. */
  days: DayData[]
  /**
   * An operation excluded from analytics was counted: on one of the days, or in the balance carried in from before the
   * month (the days show that balance). The page's hint (S10f).
   */
  excludedCounted: boolean
}

export interface DayData {
  date: string        // "2026-04-06"
  income: number
  expense: number
  balance: number     // running end-of-day balance
  hasTransactions: boolean
}

/** «Січ» / «Sty» / «Jan»: the first three letters of the language's month name, capitalised. */
function shortMonthName(year: number, month: number, locale: Locale): string {
  const name = formatDate(new Date(Date.UTC(year, month, 1)), locale, { month: 'long' })
  const short = name.slice(0, 3)
  return short.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + short.slice(1)
}

/**
 * «Січ 26» (a month bucket / a P&L column); `month` is 0-based. English writes the full year
 * («Oct 2026»): «Oct 26» reads as the 26th of October.
 */
export function formatMonthLabel(year: number, month: number, locale: Locale): string {
  const yearText = locale === 'en' ? String(year) : String(year).slice(2)
  return `${shortMonthName(year, month, locale)} ${yearText}`
}

/** «12 жов» / «12 Oct» (a day bucket): lowercase month in Ukrainian and Polish, as the month name is written there. */
export function formatDayLabel(date: Date, locale: Locale): string {
  const month = shortMonthName(date.getUTCFullYear(), date.getUTCMonth(), locale)
  return `${date.getUTCDate()} ${locale === 'en' ? month : month.toLowerCase()}`
}
```

Після цього блоку перенести без змін функцію `buildCalendar` разом з її коментарем (`/**\n * The days of a month (\`month\` 0-based) with their income, expense and running balance. ...`) з поточного файлу: вона не використовує жодного з видалених помічників.

Створити `components/reports/ReportNotice.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client'

import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'
import type { Coverage } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import { useFormatters } from '@/lib/i18n/useFormatters'

/** A report the server refused (more operations than it reads: «звузьте період») or could not give (with a retry). */
export function ReportNotice({ kind, cap, onRetry }: { kind: 'too_large' | 'error'; cap?: number; onRetry?: () => void }) {
  const t = useTranslations('reports')
  const { number } = useFormatters()
  if (kind === 'too_large') {
    return (
      <p role="status" data-testid="report-too-large" className="rounded-field border border-line bg-surface px-4 py-3 text-sm text-ink">
        {t('tooLarge', { cap: number(cap ?? 0) })}
      </p>
    )
  }
  return (
    <div role="alert" data-testid="report-error" className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 rounded-field border border-line bg-surface px-4 py-3 text-sm text-ink">
      <span className="min-w-0">{t('error')}</span>
      {onRetry && (
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onRetry}
          className="rounded-control px-3 py-1.5 font-medium text-brand-soft-foreground hover:bg-brand-soft focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-brand-soft-foreground"
        >
          {t('retry')}
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

/** Where the figures came from (the response's `source`) and, when some were read but could not be counted, how many. */
export function ReportSource({ coverage }: { coverage: Coverage }) {
  const t = useTranslations('reports')
  const { number } = useFormatters()
  return (
    <p data-testid="report-source" data-source={coverage.source} className="text-xs text-ink-subtle">
      {coverage.source === 'rollups' ? t('source.rollups') : t('source.operations', { count: number(coverage.operationsRead) })}
      {coverage.uncountable !== null && coverage.uncountable > 0 && (
        <>
          {' · '}
          <span data-testid="report-uncountable" className="text-expense">{t('uncountable', { count: number(coverage.uncountable) })}</span>
        </>
      )}
    </p>
  )
}
```

```tsx
'use client'

import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'
import { fromMinor } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/money'
import type { Totals } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import { useFormatters } from '@/lib/i18n/useFormatters'

/** «Виключено з аналітики» (S12, the owner's 2026-10-29 requirement): what the report left out; nothing when a tag counts them in. */
export function ExcludedLine({ excluded, currency, testId }: { excluded: Totals | null; currency: string; testId: string }) {
  const t = useTranslations('reports.excluded')
  const { money } = useFormatters()
  if (excluded === null || (excluded.incomeBase === 0 && excluded.expenseBase === 0)) return null
  return (
    <div data-testid={testId} className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3 rounded-panel border border-line bg-surface px-6 py-4 text-sm">
      <div className="min-w-0">
        <p className="font-semibold text-ink">{t('title')}</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-ink-muted">{t('hint')}</p>
      </div>
      <div className="shrink-0 text-right tabular-nums">
        {excluded.incomeBase !== 0 && <p data-testid={`${testId}-income`} className="text-income">+{money(fromMinor(excluded.incomeBase), currency)}</p>}
        {excluded.expenseBase !== 0 && <p data-testid={`${testId}-expense`} className="text-expense">−{money(fromMinor(excluded.expenseBase), currency)}</p>}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

Створити `components/reports/ReconciliationBlock.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client'

import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'
import { fromDay } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/days'
import { fromMinor } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/money'
import type { Reconciliation } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import { useFormatters } from '@/lib/i18n/useFormatters'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

interface Line {
  id: string
  label: string
  value: number | null
  tone?: 'strong' | 'bad'
}

/**
 * «Звірка із залишками» (S12) under Cash Flow and P&L: the opening balance, the lines that move it and the closing balance,
 * in the company currency. A line that only explains a split (outside the filter, transfers) is left out at 0;
 * «Розбіжність» shows whenever the equation does not hold, never rounded away.
 */
export function ReconciliationBlock({ reconciliation: r, currency }: { reconciliation: Reconciliation; currency: string }) {
  const t = useTranslations('reports.reconciliation')
  const { money, date, number } = useFormatters()
  const day = (d: string) => date(fromDay(d), { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' })
  const amount = (minor: number | null) => (minor === null ? '—' : money(fromMinor(minor), currency))
  const lines: Line[] = [
    { id: 'opening', label: t('opening', { date: day(r.openingDay) }), value: r.openingBase, tone: 'strong' },
    { id: 'net', label: t('net'), value: r.reportNetBase },
  ]
  if (r.excludedBase !== null) lines.push({ id: 'excluded', label: t('excluded'), value: r.excludedBase })
  if (r.outsideFilterBase !== 0) lines.push({ id: 'outside', label: t('outside'), value: r.outsideFilterBase })
  if (r.transfersBase !== 0) lines.push({ id: 'transfers', label: t('transfers'), value: r.transfersBase })
  if (r.rates.length > 0) lines.push({ id: 'fx', label: t('fx'), value: r.fxBase })
  lines.push({ id: 'closing', label: t('closing', { date: day(r.closingDay) }), value: r.closingBase, tone: 'strong' })
  if (r.discrepancyBase !== null && r.discrepancyBase !== 0) lines.push({ id: 'discrepancy', label: t('discrepancy'), value: r.discrepancyBase, tone: 'bad' })
  const missing = [...new Set(r.missingRates.map((m) => m.currency))].join(', ')
  const pending = r.rates.some((x) => x.closing !== null && !x.closing.final)
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="reconciliation-title" data-testid="reconciliation" className="rounded-panel border border-line bg-surface">
      <h2 id="reconciliation-title" className="border-b border-line px-6 py-4 text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{t('title')}</h2>
      <dl className="divide-y divide-line px-6">
        {lines.map((line) => (
          <div key={line.id} data-testid={`recon-${line.id}`} className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-4 py-2.5 text-sm">
            <dt className={cn('min-w-0', line.tone === 'strong' ? 'font-semibold text-ink' : line.tone === 'bad' ? 'font-semibold text-expense' : 'text-ink-muted')}>{line.label}</dt>
            <dd className={cn('shrink-0 tabular-nums', line.tone === 'bad' ? 'font-semibold text-expense' : line.tone === 'strong' ? 'font-semibold text-ink' : 'text-ink')}>
              {amount(line.value)}
            </dd>
          </div>
        ))}
      </dl>
      <div className="space-y-1 border-t border-line px-6 py-3 text-xs text-ink-muted">
        {r.discrepancyBase === 0 && <p data-testid="recon-ok" className="font-medium text-income">{t('ok')}</p>}
        {r.excludedIncluded && <p data-testid="recon-included">{t('included')}</p>}
        {!r.valued && <p data-testid="recon-unvalued" className="text-expense">{t('unvalued', { currencies: missing })}</p>}
        {r.rates.map((x) =>
          x.closing === null ? null : (
            <p key={x.currency} data-testid="recon-rate">
              {t('rate', { currency: x.currency, rate: number(x.closing.rate, { minimumFractionDigits: 4, maximumFractionDigits: 4 }), date: day(x.closing.rateDate) })}
            </p>
          ),
        )}
        {pending && <p data-testid="recon-pending">{t('pending')}</p>}
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}
```

Створити `components/reports/CategoryBreakdown.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client'

import { useMemo } from 'react'
import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'
import { fromMinor } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/money'
import { categoryTreeRows } from '@/lib/domain/reports/categories'
import type { CategoryLine } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import { useShell } from '@/lib/hooks/useShell'
import { useCategoryName } from '@/lib/i18n/useCategoryName'
import { useFormatters } from '@/lib/i18n/useFormatters'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

/** «За категоріями» of Cash Flow (S12): each type's tree, a parent with the sum of its subcategories, «Без категорії» last. */
export function CategoryBreakdown({ lines, currency }: { lines: CategoryLine[]; currency: string }) {
  const t = useTranslations('reports.byCategory')
  const noCategory = useTranslations('journal.list')('noCategory')
  const categoryName = useCategoryName()
  const { incomeCategories, expenseCategories } = useShell()
  const { money } = useFormatters()
  const categories = useMemo(() => [...incomeCategories, ...expenseCategories], [incomeCategories, expenseCategories])
  const byId = useMemo(() => new Map(categories.map((c) => [c.id, c])), [categories])
  const rows = useMemo(() => categoryTreeRows(lines, categories), [lines, categories])
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="cf-categories-title" className="overflow-hidden rounded-panel border border-line bg-surface">
      <h2 id="cf-categories-title" className="border-b border-line px-6 py-4 text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{t('title')}</h2>
      {rows.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="p-6 text-center text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('empty')}</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="divide-y divide-line">
          {rows.map((r) => {
            const category = byId.get(r.row)
            return (
              <li
                key={`${r.type}|${r.row}`}
                data-testid="cf-category-row"
                data-level={r.level}
                className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-4 py-2.5 pr-6 text-sm"
                style={{ paddingLeft: `${24 + r.level * 20}px` }}
              >
                <span className={cn('min-w-0 truncate', r.level === 0 ? 'font-medium text-ink' : 'text-ink-muted')}>{category ? categoryName(category) : noCategory}</span>
                <span className={cn('shrink-0 tabular-nums', r.type === 'income' ? 'text-income' : 'text-expense')}>
                  {r.type === 'income' ? '+' : '−'}
                  {money(fromMinor(r.amountBase), currency)}
                </span>
              </li>
            )
          })}
        </ul>
      )}
    </section>
  )
}
```
