```tsx
'use client'

import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'
import { fromMinor } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/money'
import type { CashFlowReport } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import { useShell } from '@/lib/hooks/useShell'
import { useFormatters } from '@/lib/i18n/useFormatters'
import { inShellOrder } from '@/lib/utils/report-view'

/** «За рахунками» of Cash Flow (S12): every chosen account in its own currency, from its opening to its closing balance. */
export function CashFlowAccounts({ report }: { report: CashFlowReport }) {
  const t = useTranslations('reports.byAccount')
  const { accounts } = useShell()
  const { money } = useFormatters()
  const names = new Map(accounts.map((a) => [a.id, a.name]))
  const balances = new Map(report.reconciliation.accounts.map((a) => [a.accountId, a]))
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="cf-accounts-title" className="overflow-hidden rounded-panel border border-line bg-surface">
      <h2 id="cf-accounts-title" className="border-b border-line px-6 py-4 text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{t('title')}</h2>
      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full text-sm tabular-nums">
          <thead>
            <tr className="bg-canvas text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-ink-muted">
              <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left font-semibold">{t('account')}</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-semibold">{t('opening')}</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-semibold">{t('income')}</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-semibold">{t('expense')}</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-semibold">{t('transfers')}</th>
              <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right font-semibold">{t('closing')}</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {inShellOrder(report.accounts, accounts).map((line) => {
              const balance = balances.get(line.accountId)
              const currency = balance?.currency ?? report.currency
              const m = (minor: number) => money(fromMinor(minor), currency)
              return (
                <tr key={line.accountId} data-testid="cf-account-row" className="border-t border-line">
                  <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-6 py-3 font-medium text-ink">{names.get(line.accountId) ?? line.accountId}</td>
                  <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-right text-ink">{balance ? m(balance.openingMinor) : '—'}</td>
                  <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-right text-income">{m(line.inMinor)}</td>
                  <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-right text-expense">{m(line.outMinor)}</td>
                  <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-right text-ink-muted">{m(line.transferInMinor - line.transferOutMinor)}</td>
                  <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-6 py-3 text-right font-semibold text-ink">{balance ? m(balance.closingMinor) : '—'}</td>
                </tr>
              )
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}
```

`components/analytics/ReportToolbar.tsx`:

- [ ] **Step 4: Replace the Cash Flow page**

Замінити `app/(app)/[orgSlug]/analytics/cashflow/page.tsx` повністю:

```tsx
'use client'

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'
import { toast } from 'sonner'
import { ExcludedHint } from '@/components/analytics/AnalyticsFilters'
import { ReportToolbar } from '@/components/analytics/ReportToolbar'
import CashFlowChart from '@/components/charts/CashFlowChart'
import { CashFlowAccounts } from '@/components/reports/CashFlowAccounts'
import { CategoryBreakdown } from '@/components/reports/CategoryBreakdown'
import { ExcludedLine } from '@/components/reports/ExcludedLine'
import { ReconciliationBlock } from '@/components/reports/ReconciliationBlock'
import { ReportNotice, ReportSource } from '@/components/reports/ReportNotice'
import { downloadReport } from '@/lib/api/reports-client'
import { fromMinor } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/money'
import type { ExportFormat } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import { useAnalyticsFilters } from '@/lib/hooks/useAnalyticsFilters'
import { useCashFlowReport } from '@/lib/hooks/useReports'
import { useShell } from '@/lib/hooks/useShell'
import { useFormatters } from '@/lib/i18n/useFormatters'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
import { DEFAULT_REPORT_PERIOD, periodSlug, type PeriodSelection } from '@/lib/utils/period'
import { cashFlowBuckets, reportQueryOf } from '@/lib/utils/report-view'
import { todayUtcDay } from '@/lib/utils/today'

export default function CashFlowPage() {
  const t = useTranslations('analytics')
  const tCharts = useTranslations('charts')
  const tSections = useTranslations('shell.analyticsSections')
  const tReports = useTranslations('reports')
  const { locale, number } = useFormatters()
  const formatNum = (n: number) => number(n, { maximumFractionDigits: 0 })
  const { orgSlug } = useParams<{ orgSlug: string }>()
  const { can } = useShell()
  const [period, setPeriod] = useState<PeriodSelection>(DEFAULT_REPORT_PERIOD)
  // Warsaw's date, read once per visit (presets resolve against it).
  const [now] = useState(() => todayUtcDay())

  // The report filters live in the address (S10f); the categories decide their normal form, so the server is asked once
  // they are known. The figures come from the server (S12): this page no longer reads the company's operations.
  const { filters, setFilters, categoryTypes } = useAnalyticsFilters('operations')
  const query = useMemo(() => (categoryTypes === undefined ? null : reportQueryOf(period, now, filters)), [categoryTypes, period, now, filters])
  const { view, refresh } = useCashFlowReport(orgSlug, query)
  const report = view.status === 'ready' ? view.data : null
  const buckets = useMemo(() => (report ? cashFlowBuckets(report, locale) : []), [report, locale])
  const loading = view.status === 'loading'
  const unitLabel = report?.granularity === 'day' ? t('columns.day') : t('columns.month')

  const handleExport = (format: ExportFormat) => {
    if (query === null) return
    downloadReport(orgSlug, 'cashflow', format, query, `cashflow-${periodSlug(period)}.${format}`).catch(() => toast.error(tReports('exportFailed')))
  }

  return (
    // The shared container and the reports' sub-navigation come from analytics/layout.tsx.
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="font-display text-xl font-semibold text-ink">{tSections('cashflow')}</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('cashflow.subtitle')}</p>
        </div>
        <ReportToolbar period={period} onPeriodChange={setPeriod} filters={filters} onFiltersChange={setFilters} onExport={handleExport} />
        {report?.excludedCounted && <ExcludedHint />}
        {report && <ReportSource coverage={report.coverage} />}
      </div>

      {view.status === 'too_large' && <ReportNotice kind="too_large" cap={view.cap} />}
      {view.status === 'error' && <ReportNotice kind="error" onRetry={refresh} />}

      {report && buckets.length > 0 && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
          {[
            { id: 'income', label: t('cashflow.totalIncome'), value: fromMinor(report.totals.incomeBase), tone: 'text-income' },
            { id: 'expense', label: t('cashflow.totalExpense'), value: fromMinor(report.totals.expenseBase), tone: 'text-expense' },
            { id: 'net', label: t('columns.net'), value: fromMinor(report.totals.netBase), tone: report.totals.netBase >= 0 ? 'text-income' : 'text-expense' },
          ].map((card) => (
            <div key={card.id} className="rounded-panel border border-line bg-surface px-5 py-4">
              <p className="mb-1 text-xs text-ink-muted">{card.label}</p>
              <p data-testid={`cashflow-total-${card.id}`} className={cn('text-xl font-bold tabular-nums', card.tone)}>
                {card.value >= 0 && card.id === 'net' ? '+' : ''}
                {formatNum(card.value)}
              </p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {view.status !== 'too_large' && view.status !== 'error' && (
        <>
          <div className="rounded-panel border border-line bg-surface p-4 lg:p-6">
            <CashFlowChart data={buckets} loading={loading} />
          </div>

          <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-panel border border-line bg-surface">
            <div className="border-b border-line px-6 py-4">
              <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{report?.granularity === 'day' ? t('cashflow.detailDays') : t('cashflow.detailMonths')}</h2>
            </div>
            {loading ? (
              <div className="space-y-3 p-6">
                {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="h-10 animate-pulse rounded-control bg-canvas" />
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : buckets.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="p-6 text-center text-sm text-ink-muted">
                {t('cashflow.empty')}{' '}
                {can('data.write') && (
                  <Link href={`/${orgSlug}/transactions`} className="text-brand-soft-foreground underline">
                    {tCharts('addTransactions')}
                  </Link>
                )}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full text-sm tabular-nums">
                  <thead>
                    <tr className="bg-canvas text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-ink-muted">
                      <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left font-semibold">{unitLabel}</th>
                      <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right font-semibold">{t('columns.income')}</th>
                      <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right font-semibold">{t('columns.expense')}</th>
                      <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right font-semibold">{t('columns.net')}</th>
                      <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right font-semibold">{t('columns.balance')}</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {buckets.map((row) => (
                      <tr key={row.key} className="border-t border-line hover:bg-canvas">
                        <td className="px-6 py-3 font-medium text-ink">{row.label}</td>
                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-income">{row.income > 0 ? `+${formatNum(row.income)}` : '—'}</td>
                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-expense">{row.expense > 0 ? `−${formatNum(row.expense)}` : '—'}</td>
                        <td className={cn('px-6 py-3 text-right font-medium', row.net >= 0 ? 'text-income' : 'text-expense')}>
                          {row.net >= 0 ? '+' : ''}
                          {formatNum(row.net)}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right font-semibold text-ink">{formatNum(row.balance)}</td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {report && (
        <>
          <ExcludedLine excluded={report.excluded} currency={report.currency} testId="cashflow-excluded" />
          <CashFlowAccounts report={report} />
          <CategoryBreakdown lines={report.categories} currency={report.currency} />
          <ReconciliationBlock reconciliation={report.reconciliation} currency={report.currency} />
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
```

Замінити `app/(app)/[orgSlug]/analytics/pl/page.tsx` повністю:

```tsx
'use client'

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'
import { toast } from 'sonner'
import { ExcludedHint } from '@/components/analytics/AnalyticsFilters'
import { ReportToolbar } from '@/components/analytics/ReportToolbar'
import PLSummaryCharts from '@/components/charts/PLSummaryCharts'
import PLTable from '@/components/charts/PLTable'
import { ExcludedLine } from '@/components/reports/ExcludedLine'
import { ReconciliationBlock } from '@/components/reports/ReconciliationBlock'
import { ReportNotice, ReportSource } from '@/components/reports/ReportNotice'
import { downloadReport } from '@/lib/api/reports-client'
import type { ExportFormat } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import { useAnalyticsFilters } from '@/lib/hooks/useAnalyticsFilters'
import { usePLReport } from '@/lib/hooks/useReports'
import { useShell } from '@/lib/hooks/useShell'
import { useCategoryName } from '@/lib/i18n/useCategoryName'
import { DEFAULT_REPORT_PERIOD, periodSlug, type PeriodSelection } from '@/lib/utils/period'
import { plRows, reportQueryOf } from '@/lib/utils/report-view'
import { todayUtcDay } from '@/lib/utils/today'

export default function PLPage() {
  const t = useTranslations('analytics')
  const tReports = useTranslations('reports')
  const noCategory = useTranslations('journal.list')('noCategory')
  const categoryName = useCategoryName()
  const { orgSlug } = useParams<{ orgSlug: string }>()
  // Categories come from ShellProvider (one subscription): the names and the order of the rows.
  const { incomeCategories, expenseCategories } = useShell()
  const categories = useMemo(() => [...incomeCategories, ...expenseCategories], [incomeCategories, expenseCategories])
  const [period, setPeriod] = useState<PeriodSelection>(DEFAULT_REPORT_PERIOD)
  // Warsaw's date, read once per visit (presets resolve against it).
  const [now] = useState(() => todayUtcDay())

  // The figures come from the server (S12), by recognition date; the filters live in the address (S10f).
  const { filters, setFilters, categoryTypes } = useAnalyticsFilters('operations')
  const query = useMemo(() => (categoryTypes === undefined ? null : reportQueryOf(period, now, filters)), [categoryTypes, period, now, filters])
  const { view, refresh } = usePLReport(orgSlug, query)
  const report = view.status === 'ready' ? view.data : null
  const { monthKeys, rows } = useMemo(
    () => (report ? plRows(report, categories, categoryName, noCategory) : { monthKeys: [], rows: [] }),
    [report, categories, categoryName, noCategory],
  )

  const handleExport = (format: ExportFormat) => {
    if (query === null) return
    downloadReport(orgSlug, 'pl', format, query, `pl-${periodSlug(period)}.${format}`).catch(() => toast.error(tReports('exportFailed')))
  }

  return (
    // The shared container and the reports' sub-navigation come from analytics/layout.tsx.
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="font-display text-xl font-semibold text-ink">{t('pl.title')}</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('pl.subtitle')}</p>
        </div>
        <ReportToolbar period={period} onPeriodChange={setPeriod} filters={filters} onFiltersChange={setFilters} onExport={handleExport} />
        {report?.excludedCounted && <ExcludedHint />}
        {report && <ReportSource coverage={report.coverage} />}
      </div>

      {view.status === 'too_large' && <ReportNotice kind="too_large" cap={view.cap} />}
      {view.status === 'error' && <ReportNotice kind="error" onRetry={refresh} />}

      {report && rows.length > 0 && <PLSummaryCharts rows={rows} />}
      {view.status !== 'too_large' && view.status !== 'error' && <PLTable rows={rows} monthKeys={monthKeys} loading={view.status === 'loading'} />}

      {report && (
        <>
          <ExcludedLine excluded={report.excluded} currency={report.currency} testId="pl-excluded" />
          <ReconciliationBlock reconciliation={report.reconciliation} currency={report.currency} />
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { notFound, redirect } from 'next/navigation'
import { isAppPath } from '@/lib/auth/paths'

// The home address of a company (S12) opens «Гроші зараз». The dashboard itself lives at /<org>/dashboard: proxy.ts checks
// the session only on /<segment>/... (`isAppPath`), which keeps single-segment public pages such as /pricing open.
export default async function OrgHome({ params }: { params: Promise<{ orgSlug: string }> }) {
  const { orgSlug } = await params
  const dashboard = `/${orgSlug}/dashboard`
  // Only a segment the proxy treats as a company is sent on; anything else (a file name, a public page) stays a 404, as before.
  if (!isAppPath(dashboard)) notFound()
  redirect(dashboard)
}
```

Створити `app/(app)/[orgSlug]/dashboard/page.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client'

import { useParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'
import { ReportNotice } from '@/components/reports/ReportNotice'
import { fromDay } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/days'
import { fromMinor } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/money'
import { useDashboard } from '@/lib/hooks/useReports'
import { useShell } from '@/lib/hooks/useShell'
import { useFormatters } from '@/lib/i18n/useFormatters'
import { PAGE_CONTAINER } from '@/lib/ui/layout'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
import { inShellOrder } from '@/lib/utils/report-view'

/** «Гроші зараз» (S12): the balance of every account today, and the total in the company currency at NBP rates. */
export default function DashboardPage() {
  const t = useTranslations('reports.dashboard')
  const tReconciliation = useTranslations('reports.reconciliation')
  const tTypes = useTranslations('common.accountTypes')
  const { orgSlug } = useParams<{ orgSlug: string }>()
  const { accounts } = useShell()
  const { view, refresh } = useDashboard(orgSlug)
  const { money, number, date } = useFormatters()
  const report = view.status === 'ready' ? view.data : null
  const lines = report ? inShellOrder(report.accounts, accounts) : []
  const sync = new Map((report?.bank ?? []).map((b) => [b.accountId, b.lastSyncAt]))
  const missing = report ? report.byCurrency.filter((c) => c.baseMinor === null).map((c) => c.currency) : []
  const pending = report ? report.byCurrency.some((c) => c.rate !== null && !c.rate.final) : false
  const day = (d: string) => date(fromDay(d), { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' })
  const rate = (r: number) => number(r, { minimumFractionDigits: 4, maximumFractionDigits: 4 })

  return (
    <div className={cn(PAGE_CONTAINER, 'space-y-6')}>
      <div>
        <h1 className="font-display text-xl font-semibold text-ink">{t('title')}</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('subtitle')}</p>
      </div>

      {view.status === 'error' && <ReportNotice kind="error" onRetry={refresh} />}
      {view.status === 'loading' && (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="h-16 animate-pulse rounded-panel bg-canvas" />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {report && (
        <>
          <section data-testid="dashboard-total" className="rounded-panel border border-line bg-surface px-5 py-4">
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-muted">{t('total', { currency: report.currency })}</p>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold tabular-nums text-ink">{money(fromMinor(report.totalBase), report.currency)}</p>
            {!report.complete && (
              <p data-testid="dashboard-incomplete" className="mt-1 text-xs text-expense">
                {t('incomplete', { currencies: missing.join(', ') })}
              </p>
            )}
            {pending && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-ink-subtle">{tReconciliation('pending')}</p>}
          </section>

          {report.byCurrency.length > 1 && (
            <section aria-labelledby="dashboard-currencies" className="overflow-hidden rounded-panel border border-line bg-surface">
              <h2 id="dashboard-currencies" className="border-b border-line px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{t('byCurrency')}</h2>
              <ul className="divide-y divide-line">
                {report.byCurrency.map((c) => (
                  <li key={c.currency} data-testid="dashboard-currency" className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-x-4 gap-y-1 px-5 py-3 text-sm">
                    <span className="font-medium text-ink">{c.currency}</span>
                    <span className="min-w-0 text-right tabular-nums text-ink">
                      {money(fromMinor(c.balanceMinor), c.currency)}
                      {c.currency !== report.currency && (
                        <span className="block text-xs text-ink-muted">
                          {c.baseMinor === null || c.rate === null
                            ? t('noRate')
                            : `${money(fromMinor(c.baseMinor), report.currency)} · ${t('rate', { rate: rate(c.rate.rate), date: day(c.rate.rateDate) })}`}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </section>
          )}

          <section aria-labelledby="dashboard-accounts" className="overflow-hidden rounded-panel border border-line bg-surface">
            <h2 id="dashboard-accounts" className="border-b border-line px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{t('accounts')}</h2>
            {lines.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="px-5 py-6 text-center text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('empty')}</p>
            ) : (
              <ul className="divide-y divide-line">
                {lines.map((a) => {
                  const last = sync.get(a.accountId) ?? null
                  return (
                    <li key={a.accountId} data-testid="dashboard-account" className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-5 py-3 text-sm">
                      <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2.5">
                        <span aria-hidden className="size-2.5 shrink-0 rounded-full" style={{ background: a.color }} />
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                          <p className="truncate font-medium text-ink">{a.name}</p>
                          <p className="truncate text-xs text-ink-subtle">
                            {tTypes(a.type)}
                            {sync.has(a.accountId) && (
                              <span data-testid="dashboard-sync">
                                {' · '}
                                {last ? t('lastSync', { date: date(new Date(last), { dateStyle: 'medium', timeStyle: 'short', timeZone: 'Europe/Warsaw' }) }) : t('neverSynced')}
                              </span>
                            )}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="shrink-0 text-right tabular-nums">
                        <p className={cn('font-semibold', a.balanceMinor < 0 ? 'text-expense' : 'text-ink')}>{money(fromMinor(a.balanceMinor), a.currency)}</p>
                        {a.currency !== report.currency && (
                          <p className="text-xs text-ink-muted">{a.baseMinor === null ? t('noRate') : money(fromMinor(a.baseMinor), report.currency)}</p>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </li>
                  )
                })}
              </ul>
            )}
          </section>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
```

4. У `BalanceSheet`: `({ accounts, orgId = 'default', currency, loading }: BalanceSheetProps)` замінити на `({ lines, orgId = 'default', currency, loading }: BalanceSheetProps)`; `const cryptoAccounts = accounts.filter((a) => a.type === 'crypto')` на `const cryptoAccounts = lines.filter((a) => a.type === 'crypto')`; `const cryptoTotal = cryptoAccounts.reduce((s, a) => s + a.balance, 0)` на `const cryptoTotal = cryptoAccounts.reduce((s, a) => s + (a.base ?? 0), 0)`; `const moneyTotal = accounts.reduce((s, a) => s + a.balance, 0)` на `const moneyTotal = lines.reduce((s, a) => s + (a.base ?? 0), 0)`; `{accounts.length === 0 ? (` на `{lines.length === 0 ? (`; `accounts.map((acc) => (` на `lines.map((acc) => (`; `<SectionHeader roman="III" title={t('money')} total={moneyTotal} currency={currency} />` на `<SectionHeader roman="III" title={t('money')} total={moneyTotal} currency={currency} testId="balance-money-total" />`. Рядки рахунків і далі показують `money(acc.balance, acc.currency)` (кожен у своїй валюті); підсумки тепер у валюті компанії за курсом НБП, а не сума різних валют.

Замінити `app/(app)/[orgSlug]/analytics/balance/page.tsx` повністю:

```tsx
'use client'

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'
import { Download } from 'lucide-react'
import { AnalyticsFiltersButton } from '@/components/analytics/AnalyticsFilters'
import BalanceSheet from '@/components/charts/BalanceSheet'
import { ReportNotice } from '@/components/reports/ReportNotice'
import { NativeSelect } from '@/components/ui/native-select'
import { filterAccounts } from '@/lib/domain/analytics-filter/filter'
import { buildCsv, formatAmount } from '@/lib/domain/journal/csv'
import { toDay } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/days'
import { useAnalyticsFilters } from '@/lib/hooks/useAnalyticsFilters'
import { useBalances } from '@/lib/hooks/useReports'
import { useShell } from '@/lib/hooks/useShell'
import { decimalSignOf } from '@/lib/i18n/format'
import { useFormatters } from '@/lib/i18n/useFormatters'
import { downloadText } from '@/lib/utils/download'
import { AS_OF_OPTIONS, asOfDay, balanceSheetLines, type AsOfOption } from '@/lib/utils/report-view'
import { todayUtcDay } from '@/lib/utils/today'

export default function BalancePage() {
  const t = useTranslations('analytics')
  const tSections = useTranslations('shell.analyticsSections')
  const tTypes = useTranslations('common.accountTypes')
  const tDashboard = useTranslations('reports.dashboard')
  const { orgSlug } = useParams<{ orgSlug: string }>()
  const { org, accounts } = useShell()
  const { locale } = useFormatters()
  const [asOf, setAsOf] = useState<AsOfOption>('today')
  // Warsaw's date, read once per visit.
  const [today] = useState(() => toDay(todayUtcDay()))
  // The balances on the chosen day come from the server (S12), the same rule as the dashboard «Гроші зараз».
  const { view, refresh } = useBalances(orgSlug, asOfDay(asOf, today))
  // The Balance is made of accounts: of the report filters (S10f) only the accounts apply; the rest stay in the address.
  const { filters, setFilters } = useAnalyticsFilters('accounts')
  const report = view.status === 'ready' ? view.data : null
  const lines = useMemo(() => (report ? filterAccounts(balanceSheetLines(report, accounts), filters) : []), [report, accounts, filters])
  const missing = report ? report.byCurrency.filter((c) => c.baseMinor === null).map((c) => c.currency) : []

  // The journal's CSV rules (BOM, `;`, CRLF, the decimal sign of the language), from the same server balances.
  const handleExport = () => {
    if (report === null) return
    const decimal = decimalSignOf(locale)
    const header = [t('columns.account'), t('columns.type'), t('columns.balance'), t('columns.currency'), `${t('columns.balance')}, ${report.currency}`]
    const rows = lines.map((l) => [l.name, tTypes(l.type), formatAmount(l.balanceMinor, decimal), l.currency, l.baseMinor === null ? '' : formatAmount(l.baseMinor, decimal)])
    downloadText(`balance-sheet-${report.date}.csv`, buildCsv(header, rows))
  }

  return (
    // The shared container and the reports' sub-navigation come from analytics/layout.tsx.
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <h1 className="font-display text-xl font-semibold text-ink">{tSections('balance')}</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-ink-muted">
            {t('balance.subtitle')}
            {org?.name ? ` · ${org.name}` : ''}
          </p>
        </div>
        {/* Icon-only below sm so the title keeps its width; the accessible name stays the same. */}
        <button
          onClick={handleExport}
          aria-label={t('exportCsv')}
          className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 rounded-control border border-line bg-surface px-3 py-2 text-sm text-ink transition-colors hover:bg-canvas sm:px-4"
        >
          <Download className="h-4 w-4" />
          <span className="hidden sm:inline">{t('exportCsv')}</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* «Станом на» (S12: a real day of the server balances) and the account filter (S10f) */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <span className="text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('balance.asOf')}</span>
        <NativeSelect value={asOf} onChange={(e) => setAsOf(e.target.value as AsOfOption)} aria-label={t('balance.asOfLabel')} className="border-line">
          {AS_OF_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
            <option key={option} value={option}>
              {t(`balance.asOfOptions.${option}`)}
            </option>
          ))}
        </NativeSelect>
        <AnalyticsFiltersButton scope="accounts" value={filters} onChange={setFilters} />
      </div>

      {view.status === 'error' && <ReportNotice kind="error" onRetry={refresh} />}
      {report && !report.complete && (
        <p data-testid="balance-incomplete" className="text-sm text-expense">
          {tDashboard('incomplete', { currencies: missing.join(', ') })}
        </p>
      )}

      <BalanceSheet lines={lines} orgId={orgSlug} currency={report?.currency ?? org?.currency ?? 'PLN'} loading={view.status === 'loading'} />
    </div>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: The seed helper**

Створити `tests/e2e/helpers/report-seed.ts`:

```ts
// Operations written through the real create route for the S12 specs (the signed-in page's request context carries the
// session cookie), so the ledger keeps the balances, the rollups and `excluded` as the app's own writes do: the server
// reports read the rollups, which `seedLedgerOperation` (an admin write without them) does not produce.
import { expect, type Page } from '@playwright/test'

const ORIGIN = { Origin: 'http://localhost:3000' }

export interface ApiOperation {
  type: 'income' | 'expense' | 'transfer'
  accountId: string
  amountMinor: number
  paymentDate: string
  toAccountId?: string
  toAmountMinor?: number
  categoryId?: string
  tags?: string[]
  description?: string
  accrual?: { mode: 'date'; date: string } | { mode: 'period'; from: string; to: string }
}

export async function postOperation(page: Page, orgId: string, op: ApiOperation): Promise<void> {
  const res = await page.request.post(`/api/orgs/${orgId}/transactions`, { headers: ORIGIN, data: op })
  expect(res.status(), `operation «${op.description ?? op.type}»: ${await res.text()}`).toBe(201)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the reports spec and the 401 check**

Створити `tests/e2e/reports.spec.ts`:

```ts
// tests/e2e/reports.spec.ts
// Server reports (S12): Cash Flow from the rollups with the reconciliation and the «Виключено з аналітики» line; a concrete
// tag counts the excluded operation from the operations; the P&L spreads an accrual period over months; CSV and XLSX come
// from the server; the dashboard «Гроші зараз» values an EUR account at the NBP stub's rate; a refusal of the read limit
// says so; the Balance reads the server balances. Days come from today; expectations from the same rules as the code.
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { strFromU8, unzipSync } from 'fflate'
import { test, expect, type APIRequestContext, type Page } from '@playwright/test'
import { registerViaUi, waitForShell, type RegisteredUser } from './helpers/app-ui'
import { seedDoc } from './helpers/emulator-firestore'
import { seedLedgerAccount } from './helpers/ledger-seed'
import { postOperation } from './helpers/report-seed'
import { monthBounds, monthOf, prevMonth } from '../../lib/domain/ledger/days'
import { allocatePeriod } from '../../lib/domain/ledger/period'
import { formatMoney, formatNumber } from '../../lib/i18n/format'
import { todayDay } from '../../lib/utils/today'

const NEEDS_EMULATORS =
  'Needs the Auth + Firestore emulators. Run: firebase emulators:exec --only auth,firestore --project demo-finflow "npm run test:e2e"'

/** An amount in złoty as the Ukrainian pages write it (the same formatter as the app). */
const zl = (amount: number): string => formatMoney(amount, 'PLN', 'uk')

/** A tag in the exact shape the server stores (lib/domain/tags/doc.ts). */
async function seedTag(request: APIRequestContext, orgId: string, id: string, name: string, excludeFromAnalytics: boolean): Promise<void> {
  await seedDoc(request, `organizations/${orgId}/tags`, id, {
    schemaVersion: 1, name, color: excludeFromAnalytics ? 'rose' : 'amber', excludeFromAnalytics, archived: false, recalcPending: false,
    orgId, createdAt: new Date(), createdBy: 'e2e', updatedAt: new Date(),
  })
}

async function seedCategory(request: APIRequestContext, orgId: string, id: string, name: string, type: 'income' | 'expense'): Promise<void> {
  await seedDoc(request, `organizations/${orgId}/categories`, id, { name, type, color: '#6366f1', orgId, createdAt: new Date() })
}

/**
 * A PLN company: «Konto A» from 1000 zł, «Konto B» from 0; today a sale of 500 («Sprzedaż»), rent of 120 («Czynsz»), VAT of
 * 230 tagged «ПДВ» (excluded from analytics) and a transfer of 100 from A to B. The year closes at 1150 zł.
 */
async function setup(page: Page, request: APIRequestContext, prefix: string): Promise<RegisteredUser> {
  const user = await registerViaUi(page, prefix, 'PLN')
  await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
  const org = user.orgId
  await seedLedgerAccount(request, org, 'acc-a', { name: 'Konto A', balance: 1000, currency: 'PLN' })
  await seedLedgerAccount(request, org, 'acc-b', { name: 'Konto B', balance: 0, currency: 'PLN' })
  await seedTag(request, org, 'tag-vat', 'ПДВ', true)
  await seedCategory(request, org, 'cat-sales', 'Sprzedaż', 'income')
  await seedCategory(request, org, 'cat-rent', 'Czynsz', 'expense')
  const day = todayDay()
  await postOperation(page, org, { type: 'income', accountId: 'acc-a', amountMinor: 50_000, paymentDate: day, categoryId: 'cat-sales', description: 'Faktura 1/11' })
  await postOperation(page, org, { type: 'expense', accountId: 'acc-a', amountMinor: 12_000, paymentDate: day, categoryId: 'cat-rent', description: 'Czynsz biuro' })
  await postOperation(page, org, { type: 'expense', accountId: 'acc-a', amountMinor: 23_000, paymentDate: day, tags: ['tag-vat'], description: 'VAT' })
  await postOperation(page, org, { type: 'transfer', accountId: 'acc-a', toAccountId: 'acc-b', amountMinor: 10_000, paymentDate: day, description: 'Do kasy' })
  return user
}

test.describe('server reports (emulators)', () => {
  test.skip(!process.env.FIREBASE_AUTH_EMULATOR_HOST || !process.env.FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST, NEEDS_EMULATORS)

  test('Cash Flow comes from the server: the totals, the excluded line and a reconciliation that adds up', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const user = await setup(page, request, 'rp-cf')
    await page.goto(`/${user.orgId}/analytics/cashflow`)
    await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
    await expect(page.getByTestId('cashflow-total-income')).toHaveText('500')
    await expect(page.getByTestId('cashflow-total-expense')).toHaveText('120')
    await expect(page.getByTestId('report-source')).toHaveAttribute('data-source', 'rollups')
    await expect(page.getByTestId('cashflow-excluded-expense')).toContainText(zl(230))
    await expect(page.getByTestId('cf-account-row')).toHaveCount(2)
    const categories = page.getByTestId('cf-category-row')
    await expect(categories).toHaveCount(2)
    await expect(categories.filter({ hasText: 'Sprzedaż' })).toContainText(zl(500))
    await expect(categories.filter({ hasText: 'Czynsz' })).toContainText(zl(120))
    const recon = page.getByTestId('reconciliation')
    await expect(recon.getByTestId('recon-opening')).toContainText(zl(1000))
    await expect(recon.getByTestId('recon-net')).toContainText(zl(380))
    await expect(recon.getByTestId('recon-excluded')).toContainText(zl(-230))
    await expect(recon.getByTestId('recon-transfers')).toHaveCount(0)
    await expect(recon.getByTestId('recon-fx')).toHaveCount(0)
    await expect(recon.getByTestId('recon-closing')).toContainText(zl(1150))
    await expect(recon.getByTestId('recon-ok')).toHaveText('Сходиться')
    await expect(recon.getByTestId('recon-discrepancy')).toHaveCount(0)
  })

  test('a concrete tag counts the excluded operation from the operations; the reconciliation says it is included', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const user = await setup(page, request, 'rp-tag')
    await page.goto(`/${user.orgId}/analytics/cashflow?tg=tag-vat`)
    await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
    await expect(page.getByTestId('cashflow-total-income')).toHaveText('0')
    await expect(page.getByTestId('cashflow-total-expense')).toHaveText('230')
    await expect(page.getByTestId('analytics-excluded-hint')).toBeVisible()
    await expect(page.getByTestId('report-source')).toHaveAttribute('data-source', 'operations')
    await expect(page.getByTestId('cashflow-excluded')).toHaveCount(0)
    const recon = page.getByTestId('reconciliation')
    await expect(recon.getByTestId('recon-included')).toBeVisible()
    await expect(recon.getByTestId('recon-excluded')).toHaveCount(0)
    await expect(recon.getByTestId('recon-outside')).toContainText(zl(380))
    await expect(recon.getByTestId('recon-closing')).toContainText(zl(1150))
    await expect(recon.getByTestId('recon-ok')).toBeVisible()
  })

  test('the P&L counts by recognition date: an accrual period over two months gives each its share', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const user = await registerViaUi(page, 'rp-pl', 'PLN')
    await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
    const org = user.orgId
    await seedLedgerAccount(request, org, 'acc-a', { name: 'Konto A', balance: 1000, currency: 'PLN' })
    await seedCategory(request, org, 'cat-rent', 'Czynsz', 'expense')
    const today = todayDay()
    const from = `${prevMonth(monthOf(today))}-01`
    const to = monthBounds(monthOf(today)).last
    await postOperation(page, org, { type: 'expense', accountId: 'acc-a', amountMinor: 90_000, paymentDate: today, categoryId: 'cat-rent', accrual: { mode: 'period', from, to }, description: 'Najem' })
    // The current year's share: both months, or in January only this one (the other month is last year).
    const share = allocatePeriod(90_000, from, to).filter(([month]) => month.startsWith(today.slice(0, 4))).reduce((s, [, v]) => s + v, 0)
    await page.goto(`/${org}/analytics/pl`)
    await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
    const expenses = page.getByRole('row').filter({ hasText: 'Всього витрати' }).getByRole('cell').last()
    await expect(expenses).toHaveText(formatNumber(share / 100, 'uk', { maximumFractionDigits: 0 }))
    await expect(page.getByTestId('report-source')).toHaveAttribute('data-source', 'rollups')
    await expect(page.getByTestId('reconciliation').getByTestId('recon-ok')).toBeVisible()
  })

  test('CSV and XLSX are built by the server from the same report', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const user = await setup(page, request, 'rp-export')
    await page.goto(`/${user.orgId}/analytics/cashflow`)
    await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
    await expect(page.getByTestId('cashflow-total-income')).toHaveText('500')

    const csvDownload = page.waitForEvent('download')
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Експорт CSV', exact: true }).click()
    const csvFile = await csvDownload
    expect(csvFile.suggestedFilename()).toBe('cashflow-current-year.csv')
    const csv = readFileSync(await csvFile.path(), 'utf8')
    expect(csv.startsWith('﻿Звіт\r\n')).toBe(true)
    expect(csv).toContain('\r\nРазом;500,00;120,00;380,00;\r\n')
    expect(csv).toContain('\r\nSprzedaż;Надходження;500,00\r\n')
    expect(csv).toContain('\r\nПочатковий залишок;1000,00\r\n')
    expect(csv).toContain('\r\nКінцевий залишок;1150,00\r\n')

    const xlsxDownload = page.waitForEvent('download')
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Експорт XLSX', exact: true }).click()
    const xlsxFile = await xlsxDownload
    expect(xlsxFile.suggestedFilename()).toBe('cashflow-current-year.xlsx')
    const workbook = strFromU8(unzipSync(new Uint8Array(readFileSync(await xlsxFile.path())))['xl/workbook.xml'])
    for (const name of ['Звіт', 'За категоріями', 'Звірка']) expect(workbook).toContain(`name="${name}"`)
  })

  test('«Гроші зараз»: /<org> opens it; the balances of today and the total in PLN with an EUR account at the NBP rate', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const user = await registerViaUi(page, 'rp-dash', 'PLN')
    await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
    await seedLedgerAccount(request, user.orgId, 'acc-a', { name: 'Konto A', balance: 1000, currency: 'PLN' })
    await seedLedgerAccount(request, user.orgId, 'acc-eur', { name: 'Konto EUR', balance: 200, currency: 'EUR' })
    await page.goto(`/${user.orgId}`)
    await page.waitForURL(new RegExp(`/${user.orgId}/dashboard$`))
    await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Гроші зараз' })).toBeVisible()
    await expect(page.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Модулі' }).getByRole('link', { name: 'Гроші зараз' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', 'page')
    // The NBP stub (tests/e2e/helpers/nbp-stub.mjs) publishes EUR at 4.30 every weekday: 200 € is 860 zł.
    await expect(page.getByTestId('dashboard-total')).toContainText(zl(1860))
    await expect(page.getByTestId('dashboard-account')).toHaveCount(2)
    await expect(page.getByTestId('dashboard-account').filter({ hasText: 'Konto EUR' })).toContainText(zl(860))
    await expect(page.getByTestId('dashboard-currency').filter({ hasText: 'EUR' })).toContainText('4,3000')
    // A single segment that cannot be a company stays a 404 (no redirect into the shell).
    expect((await page.goto('/not.a.company'))?.status()).toBe(404)
  })

  test('a refusal of the read limit says to narrow the period instead of showing a figure', async ({ page }) => {
    // The server side of the limit is in tests/integration/reports-read.test.ts; here the page's answer to it.
    const user = await registerViaUi(page, 'rp-limit', 'PLN')
    await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
    await page.route('**/api/orgs/*/reports/cashflow?**', (route) =>
      route.fulfill({ status: 422, contentType: 'application/json', body: JSON.stringify({ error: 'too_many_operations', cap: 10_000 }) }),
    )
    await page.goto(`/${user.orgId}/analytics/cashflow`)
    await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
    await expect(page.getByTestId('report-too-large')).toHaveText(`Забагато операцій для цього звіту: більше ${formatNumber(10_000, 'uk')}. Звузьте період або зніміть фільтри.`)
    await expect(page.getByTestId('cashflow-total-income')).toHaveCount(0)
  })

  test('the Balance reads the server balances on the chosen day', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const user = await setup(page, request, 'rp-bal')
    await page.goto(`/${user.orgId}/analytics/balance`)
    await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
    await expect(page.getByTestId('balance-money-total')).toContainText(zl(1150))
    await page.getByLabel('Станом на').selectOption('prevYearEnd')
    await expect(page.getByTestId('balance-money-total')).toContainText(zl(1000))
  })
})
```

Створити `tests/ui-shots/reports.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { mkdirSync, readdirSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs'
import { join } from 'node:path'
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test'
import { registerViaUi, waitForShell } from '../e2e/helpers/app-ui'
import { seedDoc } from '../e2e/helpers/emulator-firestore'
import { seedLedgerAccount } from '../e2e/helpers/ledger-seed'
import { postOperation } from '../e2e/helpers/report-seed'
import { addDays, monthOf } from '../../lib/domain/ledger/days'
import { todayDay } from '../../lib/utils/today'

// S12: the server reports (Cash Flow with its reconciliation, P&L, the dashboard «Гроші зараз») at 1440 and 390 px in uk
// and pl, with an EUR account (the NBP stub's rate), a tagged VAT payment excluded from analytics and a subcategory.
// Files: reports-<cashflow|reconciliation|pl|dashboard>-<desktop|phone>-<uk|pl>.png.
const OUT_DIR = join(process.cwd(), 'ui-shots')
const VIEWPORTS = [
  { form: 'desktop', width: 1440, height: 900 },
  { form: 'phone', width: 390, height: 844 },
] as const
const LOCALES = ['uk', 'pl'] as const

test.describe('reports shots', () => {
  test.skip(
    !process.env.FIREBASE_AUTH_EMULATOR_HOST || !process.env.FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST,
    'Run with `npm run ui:shots` (it starts the Auth + Firestore emulators)',
  )

  test.beforeAll(() => {
    mkdirSync(OUT_DIR, { recursive: true })
    for (const old of readdirSync(OUT_DIR)) {
      if (old.startsWith('reports-') && old.endsWith('.png')) rmSync(join(OUT_DIR, old), { force: true })
    }
  })

  for (const locale of LOCALES) {
    test(`reports in ${locale}`, async ({ page, request, context }) => {
      test.setTimeout(300_000)
      await page.setViewportSize({ width: 1440, height: 900 })
      const user = await registerViaUi(page, `rpshots${locale}`, 'PLN')
      await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
      const org = user.orgId
      const day = todayDay()
      const firstOfMonth = `${monthOf(day)}-01`

      await seedLedgerAccount(request, org, 'acc-main', { name: 'Konto główne Millennium', balance: 25000, currency: 'PLN' })
      await seedLedgerAccount(request, org, 'acc-eur', { name: 'Rachunek walutowy EUR', balance: 1250, currency: 'EUR', color: '#6366f1' })
      const createdAt = new Date()
      await seedDoc(request, `organizations/${org}/categories`, 'cat-consult', { name: 'Usługi doradcze', type: 'income', color: '#10b981', orgId: org, createdAt })
      await seedDoc(request, `organizations/${org}/categories`, 'cat-it', { name: 'Oprogramowanie i licencje', type: 'expense', color: '#6366f1', orgId: org, createdAt })
      await seedDoc(request, `organizations/${org}/categories`, 'cat-cloud', { name: 'Chmura', type: 'expense', color: '#818cf8', parentId: 'cat-it', orgId: org, createdAt })
      await seedDoc(request, `organizations/${org}/tags`, 'tag-vat', {
        schemaVersion: 1, name: 'ПДВ', color: 'rose', excludeFromAnalytics: true, archived: false, recalcPending: false, orgId: org, createdAt, createdBy: 'shots', updatedAt: createdAt,
      })
      await postOperation(page, org, { type: 'income', accountId: 'acc-main', amountMinor: 1_240_000, paymentDate: firstOfMonth, categoryId: 'cat-consult', description: 'Faktura 14/11' })
      await postOperation(page, org, { type: 'income', accountId: 'acc-main', amountMinor: 980_000, paymentDate: addDays(firstOfMonth, -5), categoryId: 'cat-consult', description: 'Faktura 9/10' })
      await postOperation(page, org, { type: 'expense', accountId: 'acc-main', amountMinor: 320_000, paymentDate: day, categoryId: 'cat-it', description: 'Licencje roczne' })
      await postOperation(page, org, { type: 'expense', accountId: 'acc-main', amountMinor: 86_000, paymentDate: day, categoryId: 'cat-cloud', description: 'Hosting' })
      await postOperation(page, org, { type: 'expense', accountId: 'acc-main', amountMinor: 230_000, paymentDate: day, tags: ['tag-vat'], description: 'VAT na rachunek VAT' })
      await postOperation(page, org, { type: 'income', accountId: 'acc-eur', amountMinor: 50_000, paymentDate: day, categoryId: 'cat-consult', description: 'Invoice EU 7/11' })
      await postOperation(page, org, { type: 'transfer', accountId: 'acc-main', toAccountId: 'acc-eur', amountMinor: 430_000, toAmountMinor: 100_000, paymentDate: day, description: 'Wymiana walut' })

      if (locale !== 'uk') await context.addCookies([{ name: 'NEXT_LOCALE', value: locale, url: 'http://localhost:3000' }])

      const noHorizontalScroll = async (what: string): Promise<void> => {
        const over = await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth - document.documentElement.clientWidth)
        expect(over, `${what}: the page scrolls sideways by ${over}px`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0)
      }

      for (const viewport of VIEWPORTS) {
        await page.setViewportSize({ width: viewport.width, height: viewport.height })
        const shot = async (state: string): Promise<void> => {
          await page.screenshot({ path: join(OUT_DIR, `reports-${state}-${viewport.form}-${locale}.png`), animations: 'disabled' })
        }

        await page.goto(`/${org}/analytics/cashflow`)
        await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
        await expect(page.getByTestId('report-source')).toBeVisible()
        await expect(page.getByTestId('reconciliation')).toBeVisible()
        await noHorizontalScroll('cash flow')
        await shot('cashflow')
        await page.getByTestId('reconciliation').scrollIntoViewIfNeeded()
        await shot('reconciliation')

        await page.goto(`/${org}/analytics/pl`)
        await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
        await expect(page.getByTestId('report-source')).toBeVisible()
        await noHorizontalScroll('p&l')
        await shot('pl')

        await page.goto(`/${org}`)
        await page.waitForURL(/\/dashboard$/)
        await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
        await expect(page.getByTestId('dashboard-total')).toBeVisible()
        await noHorizontalScroll('dashboard')
        await shot('dashboard')
      }
    })
  }
})
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the measurement script**

Створити `scripts/measure-reports.ts`:

```ts
// S12 measurement (AGENTS.md «Performance»), on the Firestore EMULATOR only: one synthetic company written through the
// ledger, then the browser approach the reports replace (the client read every operation document of the company) against
// the server reports, each twice: cold (the first call in this process) and warm (the second). Never touches production.
//   npx firebase emulators:exec --only firestore --project demo-finflow "node --conditions=react-server --import tsx scripts/measure-reports.ts --ops=3000"
// One line per workflow and phase: documents read, aggregate queries, response bytes (UTF-8 JSON), elapsed ms, CPU ms of this
// process and its peak resident memory (MB). Emulator timings are estimates, not production timings.
import { performance } from 'node:perf_hooks'
import { initializeApp } from 'firebase-admin/app'
import { getFirestore, type Firestore } from 'firebase-admin/firestore'
import { EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS } from '@/lib/domain/analytics-filter/filter'
import { addDays, monthBounds, monthOf, toDay } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/days'
import { accountToDoc } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/doc'
import { parseTransactionInput } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/input'
import { ReportError } from '@/lib/domain/reports/errors'
import { REPORT_SCAN_CAP, type ReportQuery } from '@/lib/domain/reports/types'
import { tagToDoc } from '@/lib/domain/tags/doc'
import { tagNameKey } from '@/lib/domain/tags/keys'
import type { RateSnapshot } from '@/lib/server/fx/types'
import type { LedgerDeps } from '@/lib/server/ledger/deps'
import { transactionsCol } from '@/lib/server/ledger/paths'
import { createTransaction } from '@/lib/server/ledger/write'
import { readBalances, readDashboard } from '@/lib/server/reports/balances'
import { readCashFlowReport, readPLReport, type ReportReadStats } from '@/lib/server/reports/read'
import { todayUtcDay } from '@/lib/utils/today'

const ORG = 'measure-s12'
const UID = 'measure'

function fail(message: string, code: number): never {
  console.error(message)
  process.exit(code)
}

function opsArg(argv: readonly string[]): number {
  const raw = argv.find((a) => a.startsWith('--ops='))
  const n = raw === undefined ? 3000 : Number(raw.slice('--ops='.length))
  if (!Number.isInteger(n) || n < 1 || n > 20_000) fail('--ops must be a whole number from 1 to 20000', 2)
  return n
}

/** mulberry32: the same company on every run. */
function rng(seed: number): () => number {
  let a = seed >>> 0
  return () => {
    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0
    let t = a
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1)
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61)
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296
  }
}

/** EUR at 4.30 every day: this measures reads, not NBP. */
const fixedRate: LedgerDeps['resolveRate'] = async (from, to, date): Promise<RateSnapshot> => {
  const same = from === to
  return { rate: same ? 1 : 4.3, from, to, requestedDate: date, rateDate: same ? date : addDays(date, -1), tableNo: same ? null : 'M/2026', source: same ? 'identity' : 'NBP_A', final: true }
}

/** 4 accounts (one in EUR), 6 income and 6 expense categories, the tag «VAT» excluding from analytics; `count` operations over 13 months. */
async function seed(db: Firestore, deps: LedgerDeps, count: number, today: string): Promise<void> {
  await db.doc(`organizations/${ORG}`).set({ name: 'Measure', currency: 'PLN', ownerId: UID, plan: 'trial' })
  const accounts = [['main', 'PLN'], ['cash', 'PLN'], ['card', 'PLN'], ['eur', 'EUR']] as const
  for (const [id, currency] of accounts) {
    const doc = accountToDoc({ id, name: id, type: 'bank', currency, color: '#00c896', openingMinor: 10_000_000, balanceMinor: 10_000_000 })
    await db.doc(`organizations/${ORG}/accounts/${id}`).set({ ...doc, orgId: ORG })
  }
  const income = ['in1', 'in2', 'in3', 'in4', 'in5', 'in6']
  const expense = ['ex1', 'ex2', 'ex3', 'ex4', 'ex5', 'ex6']
  for (const id of income) await db.doc(`organizations/${ORG}/categories/${id}`).set({ orgId: ORG, name: id, type: 'income', color: '#000000' })
  for (const id of expense) await db.doc(`organizations/${ORG}/categories/${id}`).set({ orgId: ORG, name: id, type: 'expense', color: '#000000' })
  const tag = tagToDoc({ id: 'vat', name: 'VAT', color: 'slate', excludeFromAnalytics: true, archived: false, recalcPending: false })
  await db.doc(`organizations/${ORG}/tags/vat`).set({ ...tag, orgId: ORG })
  await db.doc(`organizations/${ORG}/tagKeys/${tagNameKey('VAT')}`).set({ tagId: 'vat' })
  const r = rng(12)
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const roll = r()
    const paymentDate = addDays(today, -Math.floor(r() * 395))
    const account = accounts[Math.floor(r() * accounts.length)][0]
    const amountMinor = 1_000 + Math.floor(r() * 499_000)
    const body: Record<string, unknown> =
      roll < 0.05 && account !== 'eur'
        ? { type: 'transfer', accountId: account, toAccountId: account === 'main' ? 'cash' : 'main', amountMinor, paymentDate }
        : roll < 0.3
          ? { type: 'income', accountId: account, amountMinor, paymentDate, categoryId: income[Math.floor(r() * income.length)] }
          : { type: 'expense', accountId: account, amountMinor, paymentDate, categoryId: expense[Math.floor(r() * expense.length)], ...(r() < 0.05 ? { tags: ['vat'] } : {}) }
    await createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, parseTransactionInput(body))
    if ((i + 1) % 500 === 0) console.log(`seeded ${i + 1} of ${count}`)
  }
}

interface Sample {
  docs: number
  aggregates: number
  bytes: number
}

const bytesOf = (value: unknown): number => Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(value), 'utf8')
const sampleOf = (stats: ReportReadStats, value: unknown): Sample => ({ docs: stats.operations + stats.rollups, aggregates: stats.aggregates, bytes: bytesOf(value) })

/** Runs a workflow twice and prints what it cost; a refusal of the read limit is a result too (with its time). */
async function measure(label: string, run: () => Promise<Sample>): Promise<void> {
  for (const phase of ['cold', 'warm'] as const) {
    const cpu = process.cpuUsage()
    const started = performance.now()
    let s: Sample | null = null
    try {
      s = await run()
    } catch (e) {
      if (!(e instanceof ReportError && e.code === 'too_many_operations')) throw e
    }
    const elapsed = performance.now() - started
    const used = process.cpuUsage(cpu)
    const peakMb = process.resourceUsage().maxRSS / 1024
    const result =
      s === null
        ? `refused: too_many_operations (cap ${REPORT_SCAN_CAP})`
        : `docs ${String(s.docs).padStart(6)}  aggregates ${String(s.aggregates).padStart(4)}  bytes ${String(s.bytes).padStart(9)}`
    console.log(
      `${label.padEnd(46)} ${phase}  ${result}  ${elapsed.toFixed(0).padStart(6)} ms  cpu ${((used.user + used.system) / 1000).toFixed(0).padStart(6)} ms  peak rss ${peakMb.toFixed(0)} MB`,
    )
  }
}

async function main(): Promise<void> {
  if (!process.env.FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST) fail('Emulator only: run it through firebase emulators:exec (FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST is not set).', 2)
  const count = opsArg(process.argv.slice(2))
  initializeApp({ projectId: 'demo-finflow' })
  const db = getFirestore()
  const today = toDay(todayUtcDay())
  const deps: LedgerDeps = { db, resolveRate: fixedRate, now: () => new Date(), today: () => today }
  console.log(`seeding ${count} operations over 13 months into the emulator...`)
  await seed(db, deps, count, today)

  const year: ReportQuery = { from: `${today.slice(0, 4)}-01-01`, to: `${today.slice(0, 4)}-12-31`, filters: EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS }
  const month: ReportQuery = { from: `${monthOf(today)}-01`, to: monthBounds(monthOf(today)).last, filters: EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS }
  const tagged = (q: ReportQuery): ReportQuery => ({ ...q, filters: { ...EMPTY_ANALYTICS_FILTERS, tagIds: ['vat'] } })

  await measure('before: the browser reads every operation', async () => {
    const snap = await transactionsCol(db, ORG).orderBy('paymentDate', 'desc').get()
    return { docs: snap.size, aggregates: 0, bytes: bytesOf(snap.docs.map((d) => d.data())) }
  })
  const cashFlows: Array<[string, ReportQuery]> = [
    ['Cash Flow, this year, no filter', year],
    ['Cash Flow, this year, tag VAT', tagged(year)],
    ['Cash Flow, this month, tag VAT', tagged(month)],
  ]
  for (const [label, q] of cashFlows) {
    await measure(`after: ${label}`, async () => {
      const { report, stats } = await readCashFlowReport(deps, ORG, q)
      return sampleOf(stats, report)
    })
  }
  const pls: Array<[string, ReportQuery]> = [
    ['P&L, this year, no filter', year],
    ['P&L, this year, tag VAT', tagged(year)],
  ]
  for (const [label, q] of pls) {
    await measure(`after: ${label}`, async () => {
      const { report, stats } = await readPLReport(deps, ORG, q)
      return sampleOf(stats, report)
    })
  }
  await measure('after: balances 30 days ago', async () => {
    const { report, stats } = await readBalances(deps, ORG, addDays(today, -30))
    return sampleOf(stats, report)
  })
  await measure('after: dashboard', async () => {
    const { report, stats } = await readDashboard(deps, ORG)
    return sampleOf(stats, report)
  })
  console.log('agent tokens: not applicable (S12 has no agent-facing interface).')
  console.log('not measured: production Firestore latency, NBP latency, the bytes on the wire after compression, the browser render time.')
}

main().then(
  () => process.exit(0),
  (e) => {
    console.error(e)
    process.exit(1)
  },
)
```

Expected: `tsc` чисто, `lint` 0 errors / 10 warnings; кожен прогін друкує 8 пар рядків (`cold`/`warm`) і два рядки про невиміряне. Очікувана форма (числа записати фактичні): «before» читає всі `N` операцій і віддає мегабайти; «Cash Flow, this year, no filter» читає лише денні зведені поточного року (сотні документів, не тисячі) і віддає кілобайти; «Cash Flow, this year, tag VAT» читає операції року (з `--ops=12000` близько 9-10 тис., під межею 10 000) і віддає кілобайти. «P&L, this year, tag VAT» читає операції року двічі (за датою платежу для звірки й за датою визнання для рядків), тож з `--ops=12000` очікувано дає `refused: too_many_operations` з часом відмови (лише `count()`, без документів): це правильна поведінка, її записати як є. Якщо й Cash Flow з тегом за рік дає відмову (сьогодні пізно в році, понад 10 000 операцій), так само записати.

```
## Server reports (S12)

Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-11-s12-reports-design.md`. Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-11-s12-reports.md`.

- **Split:** `lib/domain/reports/` is pure: the strict report address (`query.ts`: `from`, `to` and the S10f filters `types`, `cat`, `cp`, `tg`, `accounts`; what the lenient S10f reader would drop is a 400), the facts of a report in one shape for both paths (`facts.ts`), Cash Flow (`cashflow.ts`: days up to 31 days, months above) and P&L (`pl.ts`: by recognition date, an accrual period spread by `allocatePeriod`, a partial month by its days, `accrualShare`), the reconciliation (`reconcile.ts`), the balances (`balances.ts`), the category tree and row order (`categories.ts`, from `buildKindTree`) and the export sheets (`export.ts`). `lib/server/reports/` reads (`read.ts`, `balances.ts`), quotes NBP rates per request (`rates.ts`), renders XLSX (`xlsx.ts`, `write-excel-file` 4.1.1 exact) and the file (`file.ts`, words from `lib/i18n/report-labels.ts`); `index.ts` (`reports`) binds the production deps. The browser: `lib/api/reports-client.ts`, `lib/hooks/useReports.ts`, `lib/utils/report-view.ts`.
- **Two paths, one function:** without filters over whole months the figures come from the rollups (`cash_`/`x_cash_` of the chosen accounts by id range, `cashcat_` or `pl_` with their `x_` twins per month); otherwise from the operations of the period (`status, paymentDate` index widened by `MAX_ARRIVAL_DAYS`; the P&L also `accrual.date` and `accrual.to`, automatic single-field indexes), recomputed with the ledger's own `contributions`. Both become `ReportFacts`, the builders take only that, and a property test checks rollups = operations on random companies. The response says `coverage.source`. No new index.
- **Read limit:** `REPORT_SCAN_CAP` = 10 000 documents (operations and rollups) per request; the queries are counted first (`count()` in the same snapshot), more is `422 too_many_operations` with `cap`, never a partial figure. A filtered P&L reads the period's payments (for its reconciliation) and its accruals, so it reaches the limit at about half the operations of a filtered Cash Flow. All reads of one request are one read-only transaction (`readSnapshot`, `lib/server/ledger/reader.ts`). No response cache (`no-store`): no ledger version exists to validate one.
- **Reconciliation:** opening (close of `from − 1`, at most today) + net of the report + excluded from analytics (null when a concrete tag counts them in) + outside the filter + transfers (in PLN at the NBP rate of their day) + exchange difference = closing (close of `min(to, today)`); what is left is «Розбіжність», never rounded away. Balances at a day come from `balancesAt` (`lib/server/ledger/balance-at.ts`, shared with the journal: the account minus three `sum` aggregates after the day). The exchange difference is (opening + movement) at the closing rate − opening at the opening rate − the movement at its own rates, so a drift between the balance and the movement never hides in it. A missing rate leaves the PLN lines that need it null (`valued: false`, `missingRates`); the report's own figures stay. Operations that cannot be counted (another currency without `base`) are `coverage.uncountable`, never a zero.
- **Routes:** `GET /api/orgs/{orgId}/reports/cashflow|pl?from=&to=&<filters>`, `GET /reports/balances?date=`, `GET /reports/dashboard`, `GET /reports/export?report=cashflow|pl&format=csv|xlsx&...`; every member; 401 / 403 / 400 with `field` / 422 `too_many_operations`; `no-store`; `ReportError` mapped in `lib/server/reports/http.ts`. The export is built from the same report in the app's language (`NEXT_LOCALE`, else Accept-Language, else pl): sheets «Звіт», «За категоріями», «Звірка» (translated), CSV with the journal's rules (`buildCsvSections`), amounts as numbers in XLSX.
- **UI:** Cash Flow and P&L read the server (no `useTransactions`): `ReportSource` (`report-source`), `ExcludedLine` (`cashflow-excluded`, `pl-excluded`), «За рахунками» (`cf-account-row`), «За категоріями» (`cf-category-row`), `ReconciliationBlock` (`reconciliation`, `recon-*`), `ReportNotice` (`report-too-large`, `report-error`), two export buttons («Експорт CSV», «Експорт XLSX»). «Гроші зараз» at `/[org]/dashboard` (`/[org]` redirects there: the proxy checks the session only on `/<segment>/...`; first module tab `dashboard`): today's balances, the total in PLN at NBP rates, by currency, the last bank sync (`dashboard-*`). «Баланс»: the server balances at «Станом на» (today, end of last month, quarter, year), totals in PLN. The Calendar still reads the operations (S13); the sidebar stays a live subscription.
- **Rollout guard:** `npm run check:reports -- --project=finflow-e94b5` (read-only; refuses production without the flag; rates through a read-only view of the NBP cache) builds this month's Cash Flow and P&L from both paths, yesterday's balances and the dashboard per company, runs each new query shape once and prints counts, whether rollups = operations and whether the reconciliation adds up.
- **Tests:** unit `tests/unit/domain/reports/*` (with `property.test.ts`), `tests/unit/server/reports/*`, `tests/unit/api/reports/routes.test.ts`, `tests/unit/api/reports-client.test.ts`, `tests/unit/utils/report-view.test.ts`, `tests/unit/i18n/report-labels.test.ts`, `tests/unit/scripts/check-reports.test.ts`; integration `tests/integration/{balance-at,reports-read,reports-balances}.test.ts` (helper `report-helpers.ts`); e2e `tests/e2e/reports.spec.ts` (seed `helpers/report-seed.ts`, NBP stub `helpers/nbp-stub.mjs` through `NBP_API_BASE`); screenshots `tests/ui-shots/reports.spec.ts`; measurement `scripts/measure-reports.ts` (emulator only).
```

- рядок зі спекою й планом;
- **Статус:** реалізовано в гілці `s12-reports`, чекає огляду й мерджа; на production нічого не розгорнуто; нових індексів і правил немає; перед злиттям `check:reports` на production лише читанням (окреме «ок» власника);
- `### Зроблено`: домен звітів (адреса, факти обох шляхів, Cash Flow, P&L, звірка з курсовою різницею, баланси, дерево категорій, аркуші експорту); один read-only знімок і спільний з журналом баланс на день; читання з межею 10 000 і підрахунком до читання; курси НБП на запит; XLSX (`write-excel-file` 4.1.1) і CSV з тієї самої відповіді; маршрути; сторінки Cash Flow, P&L, «Баланс», «Гроші зараз» і головна адреса; `check:reports`; заглушка НБП для e2e; відповіді на шість відкритих питань спеки (розділ плану);
- `### Підсумок перевірок (дата, реально виміряно)`: числа з кроку 3 і порівняння з базовими; скриншоти (16 `reports-*`, що переглянуто);
- `### Не перевірено`: реальні дані production (`check:reports` ще не запускався), час звітів на production і з реальним НБП, рендер на справжньому телефоні, файли XLSX у справжньому Excel/LibreOffice (перевірено лише читанням XML), компанія з понад 10 000 операцій за рік на production;
- `### Known follow-ups (S12)`: фільтрований P&L читає операції періоду двічі (за платежем для звірки, за визнанням для рядків) і досягає межі вдвічі раніше за Cash Flow; рядок «Перекази між рахунками» в компанії з переказами між валютами показує різницю курсу банку й НБП окремо від курсової різниці; Календар досі читає всі операції (S13); вхід і перемикання компанії відкривають «Платежі», а не «Гроші зараз» (питання власнику); дашборд без блоків «Цей місяць», «Що попереду» й графіків (поза обсягом S12).

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**

```bash
cd /f/AI/finflow && git add CLAUDE.md PROGRESS.md docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-finflow-roadmap.md && git commit -m "docs(reports): S12 server reports in CLAUDE.md, PROGRESS.md and the roadmap

Co-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

## Після реалізації (веде контролер, не субагент)

1. Огляд усієї гілки opus-рев'юером (свіжий контекст, еталон: спека, розділи плану «Відповіді на відкриті питання», «Spec corrections», «Рішення плану» і цей план), виправлення знахідок з тестами. Окремо перевірити: обидва шляхи йдуть через одну функцію (`factsFromRollupDocs` → `buildCashFlowCore`/`buildPLCore`), а шлях з операцій бере `contributions`, а не свою копію правил сум, `base`, днів переказу й `excluded`; рядок категорії лише через `kindRowOf`, вибір операцій лише через `filterForAnalytics`/`matchesSharedChoices`, «конкретний тег» лише через `includesExcluded`; курси лише через `resolveRate` (оцінка залишку на день D як `D + 1`); усі читання запиту в одному read-only знімку; повнота з підрахованого (`count()` → `limit(count)`), понад межу відмова без документів; кожна сума ціла в мінорних одиницях, округлення лише в `mulRate` і `mulDivRound`; рівність звірки на будь-яких даних (властивісний тест), «Розбіжність» не поглинається курсовою різницею; сторінки Cash Flow, P&L, «Баланс» не викликають `useTransactions` (лише Календар); експорт будується з тієї самої відповіді; жоден новий запит не потребує складеного індексу (переглянути форми в `read.ts`, `balance-at.ts`, `balances.ts`); жодного `Intl` у домені, паритет каталогів, правила React Compiler.
2. Візуальна перевірка: `npm run ui:shots` (усі специфікації, бо змінився рядок вкладок модулів і екрани аналітики) і перегляд `reports-*`, `analytics-filters-*` і знімків оболонки (журнал, аналітика, налаштування) на 1440 і 390 px українською й польською.
3. `git push -u origin s12-reports`, чернетка PR (тіло англійською за зразком PR #17-#23, у кінці `🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)`), зелений CI (job `ci`). Якщо в CI падає e2e, якого не видно локально, спершу розібрати його з артефактів CI (звіт Playwright, трейси, скриншоти), знайти причину, виправити з тестом і перезапустити; не перезапускати наосліп.
4. Після зеленого CI, з окремого «ок» власника: на production лише читання `npm run check:reports -- --project=finflow-e94b5`. Індексів не розгортаємо (нових немає); якщо скрипт дає `FAILED_PRECONDITION`, це означає, що якась форма запиту все ж потребує індексу: зупинитись і розібратись окремим погодженим кроком. Записати лічильники; компанії з `rollups = operations: NO` або `reconciled: NO` перевірити `verifyLedger` (лише читання) до злиття.
5. **Зупинка і запит явного «ок» власника на злиття.** Далі кожен крок розгортання за окремим «ок»:
   1. Злиття PR. Правила й індекси не змінюються, міграції даних немає.
   2. Vercel production READY; перевірка без входу: `GET /api/orgs/x/reports/cashflow?from=2026-01-01&to=2026-12-31`, `/reports/pl?...`, `/reports/balances?date=...`, `/reports/dashboard`, `/reports/export?...` дають `401`; `verifyLedger` лише читання на production (власник: `POST /api/orgs/{org}/ledger/rebuild?apply=0`).
   3. Власник відкриває `/<org>` («Гроші зараз»), Cash Flow і P&L за поточний рік і місяць (джерело «зі зведених»), вибирає тег «не враховувати» (джерело «з операцій», рядок «включено»), перевіряє, що звірка сходиться, завантажує CSV і XLSX і відкриває їх у Excel; дивиться «Баланс» на кінець минулого місяця.
   4. Оновити пам'ять і хаб в Obsidian.

## Питання власнику (продуктові, не блокують реалізацію)

1. Вхід, реєстрація й перемикання компанії зараз відкривають «Платежі». Чи відкривати натомість «Гроші зараз» (головну адресу компанії)? План лишає «Платежі», бо спека цього не вирішує, а зміна торкається кожного e2e-входу.
2. Вибір «Станом на» на сторінці «Баланс» нічого не робив; план дає йому зміст: «Сьогодні», «Кінець минулого місяця», «Кінець минулого кварталу», «Кінець минулого року». Чи потрібні інші дати (наприклад, довільний день)?

## Самоперевірка плану (виконано під час написання)

- **Покриття спеки:** підхід А, два шляхи через одну чисту функцію і `source` у відповіді (Task 2, 3, 7; властивісний тест Task 5); дашборд з балансами з документів рахунків, разом у PLN за НБП, розбивка за валютою, остання синхронізація, без кешу довше за запит, головна `/[orgSlug]` (Task 4, 7, 9, 13); CSV і XLSX з тих самих відповідей, аркуші «Звіт», «За категоріями», «Звірка» (Task 5, 8, 9, 12); Cash Flow за датою платежу, P&L за датою визнання з розподілом періоду, спільний `PeriodSelect` (Task 2, 3, 11, 12); рядок «Виключено з аналітики» і рядок курсових різниць (Task 3, 4, 12); Календар як є, «Баланс» на серверних балансах (Task 12, 13); звірка закриття місяця з рівністю, перекази нулем для всіх рахунків, планові не входять, курсова різниця за НБП на день чи раніше, «Розбіжність» замість округлення, конкретний тег ховає «Виключено» й каже «включено» (Task 4, 7, 12; «Spec corrections» 1-4); Cash Flow за рахунками, за днями/місяцями, за категоріями з підкатегоріями й «Без категорії» за `kindRowOf` (Task 3, 5, 12); фільтри S10f з тими самими параметрами й логікою (Task 1, 7); межа читання з явною відмовою і повнотою з прочитаного (Task 7, 9, 11, 12, 14); без нових колекцій, правил і індексів, перевірка лише читанням на проді (Task 10, «Рішення плану»); маршрути для всіх членів, 400/403/401, тест покриття авторизації (Task 9, 14); `lib/domain/reports`, `lib/server/reports`, `lib/api/reports-client.ts` (Task 1-11); інтерфейс uk/pl/en, 390 px без горизонтальної прокрутки, пункт меню (Task 8, 12, 13, 15); тести модульні, властивісні, інтеграційні, e2e, скриншоти, вимірювання (Task 1-15); розгортання (розділ вище); шість відкритих питань (розділ на початку).
- **Плейсхолдери:** немає «TBD»/«TODO»/«додати обробку помилок»; кожен крок з кодом має код; де задача переносить наявний код без змін (`buildCalendar`, тіла `netAfterDay`/`arrivalShift`), вона каже, що саме перенести і звідки.
- **Узгодженість імен:** `ReportQuery`, `parseReportQuery`/`reportQueryToParams`/`reportSource`/`isWholeMonths`/`parseBalancesDate`/`assertNoParams`/`parseExportRequest`; `ReportFacts`/`CashCell`/`parseRollupId`/`factsFromRollupDocs`/`selectRollupDocs`/`operationRollups`/`accrualShare`/`mulDivRound`/`DayRange`; `buildCashFlowCore`/`rowOfStored`/`byTypeThenAmount`/`categoryLines`; `buildPLCore`; `reconciliationDays`/`rateKey`/`valuationDate`/`ratesForReconciliation`/`reconcile`/`ReconcileAccount`/`RateBook`; `balanceRates`/`balancesReport`; `categoryTreeRows`/`rowRank`/`parseReportCategory`/`ReportCategory`; `ExportLabels`/`ExportContext`/`ExportSheet`/`cashFlowSheets`/`plSheets`/`reconciliationSheet`/`sheetsToCsv`/`buildCsvSections`; `Reader`/`directReader`/`transactionReader`/`readSnapshot`; `balancesAt`/`netAfterDay`/`arrivalsQuery`/`BalanceReadStats`; `quoteRates`; `readContext`/`readCashFlowReport`/`readPLReport`/`ReportRead`/`ReportReadStats`/`ReportContext`/`REPORT_FIELDS`/`paymentsQuery`/`accrualDaysQuery`/`accrualPeriodsQuery`/`cellsQuery`; `readBalances`/`readDashboard`/`readBankSync`; `renderXlsx`/`reportFile`/`toReportHttpError`/`reports.{cashflow,pl,balances,dashboard,exportFile}`; `reportExportLabels`/`defaultCategoryTranslator`/`decimalSignOf`/`readCookie`; `fetchCashFlow`/`fetchPL`/`fetchBalances`/`fetchDashboard`/`downloadReport`/`viewOfFailure`/`ServerView`; `useCashFlowReport`/`usePLReport`/`useBalances`/`useDashboard`; `periodKeyLabel`/`reportQueryOf`/`cashFlowBuckets`/`plRows`/`inShellOrder`/`AS_OF_OPTIONS`/`asOfDay`/`balanceSheetLines`/`BalanceSheetLine`; `postOperation`: однакові в задачах, де їх визначено й використано.
